They are trained in “political awareness” and must report anything untoward. 作为回应，表演者自我审查并承诺传播“正能量”。但政府仍然密切关注。上海某区派志愿者去剧院。他们受过“政治意识”的训练，必须报告任何不利情况。 One described how she follows the approved version of a play’s script on her mobile phone to be sure actors are sticking to their lines. “I can’t relax, for fear of missing something,” she told state media. The stress is worth it, though, she says. 其中一位讲述了她是如何在手机上按照经过批准的剧本演出的，以确保演员们坚持自己的台词。“我不能放松，因为害怕错过什么,”她告诉官方媒体。不过，她表示，这种压力是值得的。 “We’re doing our little bit to build Shanghai into the performing-arts capital of Asia.” “我们正在为把上海打造成亚洲的表演艺术之都而努力。” If people think things are going too far, it is hard for them to say so. Hu Xijin, a well-known nationalist commentator, denounced Mr Li’s dog joke, but said he should be given another chance. Mr Hu was scolded for being too soft. 如果人们认为事情走得太远，他们很难这样说。著名民族主义评论员胡锡进谴责了李先生的狗笑话，但表示应该再给他一次机会。胡先生因为太软弱而受到批评。 One woman in the coastal city of Dalian was bold enough to defend Mr Li on social media. She was detained by police. ■ 在沿海城市大连，一位妇女大胆地在社交媒体上为李先生辩护。她被警察拘留了。￭ Subscribers can sign up to Drum Tower , our new weekly newsletter, to understand what the world makes of China—and what China makes of the world. 订阅者可以在我们新的每周通讯《鼓楼》(Drum Tower)上注册，了解世界如何看待中国ーー以及中国如何看待世界。 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/china/2023/05/25/chinas-cancel-culture-is-nationalist-not-woke 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/china/2023/05/25/chinas-cancel-culture-is-nationalist-not-woke 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Chaguan 茶馆 Why the Communist Party fears gay rights 为什么共产党害怕同性恋权利 Social conservatism plus paranoia about foreign infiltration prompts a crackdown 社会保守主义加上对外国渗透的疑虑促使政府进行了打击 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 T HESE ARE grim and lonely times for China’s gay communities, as the country’s LGBT support groups are forced to close, one by one. This campaign of repression reveals a lot about President Xi Jinping’s China, a paranoid place in which security agencies and ideological commissars enjoy ever-greater clout. 对于中国的同性恋群体来说，这是一个严峻而孤独的时代，因为这个国家的 LGBT 支持团体被迫一个接一个地关闭。这场镇压运动在很大程度上揭示了中国国家主席习近平治下的中国。在这个偏执的国家，安全机构和意识形态领导人的影响力越来越大。 For the same message is delivered, time and again, whenever policemen question gay-rights advocates (and sometimes their family members). It is heard when university chiefs punish students for handing out rainbow flags, or when officials press landlords to evict non-profit groups. 因为每当警察询问同性恋权利倡导者(有时是他们的家庭成员)时，同样的信息就会一次又一次地被传递出去。当大学校长因为学生分发彩虹旗而惩罚他们，或者当官员向房东施压驱逐非营利组织时，人们就会听到这种声音。 The message is that sexual minorities pose a political risk. True, some officials and state-backed scholars also call same-sex love an affront to mainstream Chinese morality, and a threat to young people whose patriotic duty is to marry and have more babies for the Motherland. 传递的信息是，性少数群体构成了政治风险。诚然，一些官员和国家支持的学者也称同性恋是对中国主流道德的侮辱，是对那些爱国责任是为祖国结婚生子的年轻人的威胁。 But activists report that, during interrogations, national security is emphasised much more than morality. Despite the political chill, gay people (as well as bars and dating apps) enjoy far more tolerance than they did a generation ago—but only if they keep it quiet. 但活动人士报告称，在审讯期间，国家安全的重要性远远超过道德。尽管政治气氛冷淡，同性恋者(以及酒吧和约会应用程序)比上一代人享受到了更多的宽容ーー但前提是他们要保持沉默。 In today’s China, forming a community is a graver offence than being gay. 在今天的中国，组建社区比同性恋更严重。 The latest casualty, the Beijing LGBT Centre, announced its closure on May 15th, citing forces beyond its control. Over 15 years in existence the centre had some high-profile successes, including a court victory in 2014 against a clinic offering electroshock therapy to “convert” gay patients. 最新的受害者，北京 LGBT 中心，于5月15日宣布关闭，理由是其无法控制的力量。该中心成立15年来取得了一些引人注目的成功，包括2014年在法庭上胜诉，一家诊所提供电击疗法来“转变”同性恋患者。 Activists talk of their cause being set back a decade or more. Within living memory, China denounced homosexuality as a mental illness imported by foreigners. Criminal penalties for gay sex were abolished only in 1997. During a first posting to Beijing in 2000, Chaguan heard a foreign government minister report, with disbelief, the claim of a Chinese counterpart that there were no gay men in China. 活动家们谈到他们的事业已经倒退了十年甚至更久。在人们的记忆中，中国谴责同性恋是外国人输入的一种精神疾病。对同性性行为的刑事处罚直到1997年才废除。在2000年第一次被派往北京期间，察官听到一位外国政府部长难以置信地报告说，一位中国同行声称中国没有同性恋男子。 Remarkable changes followed. Activists worked to persuade Chinese officials that social stability—the party’s great obsession—is strengthened by their activities. Some state media and institutions seemed to agree, offering praise for health-education campaigns or HIV -prevention work done by gay groups. 随之而来的是显著的变化。活动人士努力说服中国官员，他们的活动加强了社会稳定。一些国家媒体和机构似乎同意这一观点，赞扬同性恋团体开展的健康教育活动或艾滋病预防工作。 By 2019, your columnist could meet a gay couple in a Guangzhou cafe as they cheerfully described their plans to register as one another’s legal guardians. This baby step towards gay marriage took advantage of a law initially drafted with the elderly in mind. 到2019年，你的专栏作家可能会在广州的一家咖啡馆遇到一对同性恋伴侣，他们兴高采烈地描述他们计划注册成为对方的法定监护人。这一迈向同性婚姻的婴儿步利用了一项最初起草时考虑到老年人的法律。 Later it was adapted by creative lawyers to let same-sex couples take medical and some financial decisions for each other, should they grow infirm, undergo surgery or otherwise lose their faculties. 后来，创造性的律师改编了它，允许同性伴侣为对方做医疗和一些财务决定，如果他们变得虚弱，接受手术或以其他方式失去他们的能力。 That same year, over 180,000 members of the public submitted comments to legislators drafting new regulations on marriage in China’s civil code. Many suggested changing “husband and wife” to “spouses” as a first step towards same-sex marriage. 同年，超过18万公众向立法者提交了中国民法典中关于婚姻的新规定草案的意见。许多人建议将“夫妻”改为“配偶”，作为迈向同性婚姻的第一步。 A large number of petitioners were mobilised by legal-rights groups such as LGBT Rights Advocacy China. Since then, that petition drive backing same-sex unions has been cited by Chinese diplomats abroad as proof that their country allows free speech and bans discrimination against gay people, most recently at a hearing this February of a UN rights committee. What those smooth-talking envoys did not mention is that, back at home, civil society groups behind that self-same petition have been shut down, including LGBT Rights Advocacy China, which closed in 2021. 大量的请愿者被法律权利组织如 LGBT 权利倡导中国动员起来。此后，支持同性恋结合的请愿活动被海外的中国外交官援引为中国允许言论自由和禁止歧视同性恋的证据，最近一次是在今年2月联合国人权委员会的一次听证会上。那些油嘴滑舌的特使没有提到的是，在国内，支持这种自我同样请愿的民间社会团体已被关闭，包括2021年关闭的 LGBT 权利倡导中国。 Large companies have bowed to political pressures, too. In 2020 a state-owned airline, China Southern, fired a gay flight attendant seen kissing a male pilot on a surveillance video that went viral on social media. 大公司也屈服于政治压力。2020年，国有航空公司中国南方航空解雇了一名同性恋空中乘务员。监控录像显示，这名男子正在亲吻一名男飞行员。这段视频在社交媒体上疯传。 The airline told an employment court that the attendant might provoke mid-air disturbances if passengers recognised him from the video. In 2021 WeChat, the omnipresent messaging, e-commerce and social media app, closed dozens of accounts on LGBT topics, especially those run by university students. In recent years, regulators have banned “effeminate men” from television shows, and denounced “boys’ love” dramas, a genre involving thinly disguised homo-erotic themes. 航空公司告诉就业法庭，如果乘客从视频中认出他，乘务员可能会引起空中骚乱。2021年，无处不在的即时通讯、电子商务和社交媒体应用微信关闭了数十个关于 LGBT 话题的账户，尤其是那些由大学生运营的账户。近年来，监管机构禁止“娘娘腔男人”出现在电视节目中，并谴责“男孩之爱”剧，这种类型的剧集几乎毫无掩饰地涉及同性恋主题。 Gay groups worked hard to avoid trouble. As a young Malaysian studying in China, Raymond Phang helped to co-found Shanghai PRIDE , an organiser of gay celebrations and events. He and fellow volunteers grew used to assuring police that events would not feature political statements from foreign diplomats, or touch on such sensitive topics as Taiwan. No public parades or outdoor gatherings were attempted. 同性恋团体努力避免麻烦。作为一个在中国学习的年轻马来西亚人，Raymond Phang 帮助创立了 Shanghai PRIDE，一个同性恋庆祝和活动的组织者。他和其他志愿者逐渐习惯于向警方保证，这些活动不会出现外国外交官的政治声明，也不会涉及台湾等敏感话题。没有公共游行或户外集会的尝试。 The group eschewed slogans like “Gay Rights Now”, says Mr Phang. “We don’t do that in the mainland, it’s more about awareness, and ‘ LGBT is not a sickness’.” He calls Shanghai PRIDE lucky, because none of its volunteers or staff was taken into custody for 24 or 48 hours, as happened to some groups. Even so, the frequency of official visits became exhausting. In 2020, Shanghai PRIDE announced that it was cancelling all activities. Phang 先生说，该组织避免使用“现在同性恋权利”这样的口号。“我们在大陆没有这样做，更多的是为了提高认识，‘ LGBT 不是一种病’。”他说，“上海骄傲”很幸运，因为没有一个志愿者或工作人员像一些组织那样被拘留24或48小时。即便如此，官方访问的频率也让人精疲力尽。2020年，上海傲慢宣布取消所有活动。 Other groups were forced to close to secure the release of an organiser in detention, says a veteran campaigner now outside China. As spaces to talk about shared challenges vanish, the young are feeling isolated, he worries. “ LGBT student groups or associations have become very underground.” 目前在中国境外的一位资深活动人士表示，其它组织被迫关闭，以确保一名被拘留的组织者获释。他担心，随着谈论共同挑战的空间消失，年轻人会感到孤立。“ LGBT 学生团体或协会已经变得非常地下化。” Tyrannies distrust minorities 暴政不信任少数民族 Alas, in the party’s bleak logic, being vulnerable is grounds for suspicion. Early in the Xi era in 2012, Geremie Barmé, an Australian Sinologist, highlighted an essay published in the People’s Daily by Yuan Peng, an expert on America and later head of a think-tank affiliated with the ministry of state security. 唉，按照党暗淡的逻辑，脆弱是值得怀疑的。2012年习近平时代早期，澳大利亚汉学家白杰明(Geremie Barmé)在《人民日报》上发表了一篇文章，重点介绍了美国问题专家、后来担任国家安全部下属智库负责人的袁鹏发表的一篇文章。 Mr Yuan identified five groups that America might use to infiltrate Chinese society: rights lawyers, underground religious activitists, dissidents, internet leaders and—most chillingly— ruoshi qunti or “vulnerable groups”. A decade on, gay groups, feminists, labour activists and ethnic minorities are all treated as potential tools of subversion by a hostile West. In Mr Xi’s China, the marginalised represent a security threat, not an object for compassion. ■ 袁先生列出了五个美国可能用来渗透中国社会的团体: 维权律师、地下宗教活动家、异见人士、互联网领袖以及最令人不寒而栗的“弱势群体”。十年过去了，同性恋团体、女权主义者、劳工活动家和少数民族都被敌对的西方视为潜在的颠覆工具。在习治下的中国，被边缘化的人代表着安全威胁，而不是同情的对象。￭ Read more from Chaguan, our columnist on China: 阅读更多来自 Chaguan 的文章，我们的中国专栏作家: China learns to manage decline (May 11th) 中国学会应对衰退(5月11日) China’s new “Top Gun” normalises war with America (May 4th) 中国新的“壮志凌云”正常化与美国的战争(5月4日) China’s rulers play the law-and-order card, and lose (Apr 27th) 中国的统治者打出了法律和秩序的牌，结果输了(4月27日) Also: How the Chaguan column got its name 还有: 茶馆柱的名字是怎么来的 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/china/2023/05/25/why-the-communist-party-fears-gay-rights 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/china/2023/05/25/why-the-communist-party-fears-gay-rights 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

China 中国 Sections 部分 Europe 欧洲 Middle East & Africa 中东和非洲 Business leaders fear that South Africa risks becoming a failed state 商界领袖们担心，南非有可能成为一个失败的国家 Downhill from rainbow nation :: CEOs have realised that running the country cannot be left to the ANC 从彩虹国家走下坡路: : CEO 们已经意识到，治理国家不能留给非国大 African governments say credit-rating agencies are biased against them 非洲各国政府表示，信用评级机构对他们有偏见 My ratings are better than yours :: They claim that pessimism about debt sustainability is unwarranted 我的评级比你的好: 他们声称对债务可持续性的悲观是没有根据的 Who will be Iran’s next leader? 谁将成为伊朗的下一任领导人？ Waiting for God’s choice :: Nobody knows who might grab the reins of power 等待上帝的选择: 没有人知道谁会掌握权力 Can Jordan fall in love with Saudi Arabia? 约旦会爱上沙特阿拉伯吗？ A wishful wedding :: Dynastic alliances don’t always last ❗ 🔄 China 中国 Sections 部分 Europe 欧洲

Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Downhill from rainbow nation 从彩虹国度下山 Business leaders fear that South Africa risks becoming a failed state 商界领袖们担心，南非有可能成为一个失败的国家 CEOs have realised that running the country cannot be left to the ANC 首席执行官们已经意识到，治理国家不能留给非国大 May 22nd 2023 | JOHANNESBURG 2023年5月22日 | 约翰内斯堡 W HEN CYRIL RAMAPHOSA became South Africa’s president in 2018, business leaders were ecstatic. Here was one of their own: a pragmatic tycoon to fix the incompetent kleptocracy of Jacob Zuma. Yet five years on, bosses of large businesses are exasperated. CEO s from several different industries—such as Neal Froneman of Sibanye-Stillwater, a mining company; Daniel Mminele, the incoming chair of Nedbank; and Ralph Mupita, of MTN , a telecoms firm—have sounded the alarm. Could South Africa become a failed state? 2018年，当西里尔•拉马福萨(CYRIL RAMAPHOSA)成为南非总统时，商界领袖们欣喜若狂。这是他们中的一员: 一个务实的大亨，来修复雅各布•祖马(Jacob Zuma)无能的盗贼统治。然而，五年过去了，大型企业的老板们感到愤怒。来自几个不同行业的首席执行官已经敲响了警钟，比如矿业公司 Sibanye-Stillwater 的尼尔•弗朗曼(Neal Froneman)、即将上任的耐德银行(Nedbank)董事长丹尼尔•米内莱(Daniel Mminele) ，以及电信公司 MTN 的拉尔夫•穆皮塔(Ralph Mupita)。南非会成为一个失败的国家吗？ Already in 2023, blackouts by Eskom , the electricity utility, have surpassed those of 2022, hitherto the worst year on record (see chart). Businesses are planning for the total collapse of the grid. 2023年，电力公司 Eskom 的停电事故已经超过了2022年，这是有记录以来最糟糕的一年(见图表)。企业正在为电网的全面崩溃做准备。 “If this crisis continues, we will not be able to guarantee stable supplies of food, medicines and other essential goods,” wrote retail bosses in a letter to the president in February. “如果这场危机持续下去，我们将无法保证食品、药品和其它基本商品的稳定供应,”零售业老板们在今年2月致总统的一封信中写道。 Morale dipped further when the rand hit a record low after America’s ambassador earlier this month accused South Africa of covertly sending arms to Russia . 在美国大使本月早些时候指责南非秘密向俄罗斯运送武器后，兰特创下历史新低，士气进一步下滑。 CEO s have all but given up on Mr Ramaphosa fixing the problem himself. So firms are sending staff to government departments, including his office: a very South African solution. 首席执行官们几乎已经放弃了让拉马福萨先生自己解决问题。所以公司派员工到政府部门，包括他的办公室: 一个非常南非式的解决方案。 Those with means find alternatives to a failing public sector, paying for private health care, schools and security, and installing solar panels to power their homes. 那些有经济能力的人可以找到替代失败的公共部门的方法，支付私人医疗保健、学校和安全费用，并安装太阳能电池板为家庭供电。 Links between big business and the government began before 1994, when Nelson Mandela and his African National Congress ( ANC ) came to power. As international firms and capital left South Africa in the 1980s, the domestic groups that dominated South African business got bigger. 大企业和政府之间的联系始于1994年，当时纳尔逊 · 曼德拉和他的非洲人国民大会(ANC)上台执政。20世纪80年代，随着国际公司和资本离开南非，主导南非商业的国内集团变得更大。 By the end of the decade three conglomerates controlled companies accounting for 75% of the market value of the Johannesburg Stock Exchange ( JSE ). But some tycoons saw that white rule was unsustainable. So in 1985, defying the wishes of the apartheid regime, the chair of Anglo-American took a group to meet exiled leaders of the ANC . One delegate, upon meeting Thabo Mbeki, joked to the future president: “Welcome to the capitalist class!” 到2010年底，三大企业集团控制的公司占到南非证券交易所(JSE)市值的75% 。但一些大亨认为，白人统治是不可持续的。因此，在1985年，英裔美国人主席不顾种族隔离政权的意愿，带领一个小组会见了流亡的非国大领导人。一位代表在会见塔博 · 姆贝基时，对未来的总统开玩笑说: “欢迎来到资产阶级!” Those talks led to an unwritten deal. The ANC would drop its pledge to nationalise big companies. Business would embrace affirmative action and enrich a black elite including, most notably, Mr Ramaphosa. In other respects the basic structure of the South African economy changed little. 那些谈判导致了一个不成文的协议。非国大将放弃将大公司国有化的承诺。商业将拥抱平权法案，并使黑人精英阶层富裕起来，其中最引人注目的就是拉马福萨先生。在其他方面，南非经济的基本结构变化不大。 Many of the same big firms still predominate—and feel less competitive pressure than peers in other parts of the world. According to research by the IMF , mark-ups by publicly listed firms increased by 25% from 2000 to 2016. The average rise globally was 6%. Sustained mark-ups, namely prices above the marginal cost of production, imply a lack of competition. 与世界其它地区的同行相比，许多同样的大公司仍然占据着主导地位，它们感受到的竞争压力也较小。根据国际货币基金组织的研究，从2000年到2016年，上市公司的加价幅度增加了25% 。全球平均增长率为6% 。持续的加价，即价格高于边际生产成本，意味着缺乏竞争。 Problems have been thrashed out in private. In 2015, Mr Zuma tanked the rand by replacing a respected finance minister—who had helped block a multi-billion dollar nuclear-power deal with Russia—with an obscure MP . Captains of industry called a meeting with ANC bigwigs, including Mr Ramaphosa, who by then had amassed a fortune and returned to politics. Mr Zuma changed tack. 问题已经在私下讨论过了。2015年，祖马先生用一位默默无闻的议员取代了一位受人尊敬的财政部长，这位部长曾帮助阻止与俄罗斯达成一项数十亿美元的核能协议。工业巨头们召集了一次与非国大重要人物的会议，其中包括拉马福萨先生，当时他已经积累了一大笔财富并重返政坛。祖马先生改变了策略。 Yet today’s crises cannot be solved with a quiet word. Bosses typically cite three issues: power, logistics and crime. Last year power cuts may have reduced GDP by 7-8%; 2023 could be even darker. The blackouts caused South Africa to go from being the most reliable among MTN ’s 19 African networks at the start of 2022 to possibly the worst a year later. The largest supermarket chain says spending on diesel for generators cut profits by 7% in 2022. 然而，当今的危机不可能用一句悄悄话来解决。老板们通常会提到三个问题: 权力、后勤和犯罪。去年停电可能使 GDP 减少了7-8% ; 2023年可能更加黑暗。停电导致南非从2022年初 MTN 的19个非洲网络中最可靠的一个变成了一年后可能最糟糕的一个。这家最大的连锁超市表示，2022年用于发电机的柴油消费将使利润减少7% 。 Transnet, the state-owned firm that operates freight rail has, like Eskom, been battered by allegations of corruption and mismanagement. 运营货运铁路的国有公司 Transnet 和 Eskom 一样，也受到腐败和管理不善指控的打击。 Last year miners lost out on 300bn rand ($16bn) worth of exports, roughly a third of what they managed to sell abroad, according to Jan Havenga of Stellenbosch University, because they could not get their goods out of the country. 根据斯泰伦博斯大学的扬•哈文加(Jan Havenga)的数据，去年矿商亏损了价值3000亿兰特(合160亿美元)的出口产品，大约相当于它们出口总额的三分之一，原因是它们无法将产品运出南非。 Transnet moved less general freight—excluding minerals and metals—than at any time since the 1940s. Transnet 的一般货运量(不包括矿物和金属)低于上世纪40年代以来的任何时候。 The crook factor 骗子因素 Crime makes everything worse. In 2019 at least 183 infrastructure projects were disrupted by “construction mafia” demanding jobs and bribes. 犯罪使一切变得更糟。2019年，至少有183个基础设施项目被要求工作和贿赂的“建筑黑手党”破坏。 The theft of cables from Eskom, Transnet and passenger railways cost South Africa an estimated 50bn rand in 2022, roughly akin to the economic contribution of the wine industry. 据估计，2022年南非因窃取 Eskom、 Transnet 和客运铁路的电缆而损失了500亿兰特，大致相当于葡萄酒行业的经济贡献。 Andre de Ruyter, Eskom’s former head who courageously tried to root out graft, was nearly fatally poisoned in December. 安德烈•德鲁伊特(Andre de Ruyter)是 Eskom 的前负责人，他勇敢地试图根除贪污行为，但在去年12月几乎被毒死。 (Do not use a personalised coffee mug, he says, when asked if he has advice for his successor.) Western officials worry that South Africa is becoming to money-laundering what Silicon Valley is to venture capital. (他表示，当被问及是否对自己的继任者有什么建议时，不要使用个性化的咖啡杯。)西方官员担心，南非正在成为洗钱的对象，就像硅谷成为风险投资的对象一样。 In February the Financial Action Task Force, a global watchdog, put the country on its “grey list”, meaning that South Africa’s banks and authorities will come under greater scrutiny. 今年2月，全球监管机构金融行动特别工作组(Financial Action Task Force)将南非列入“灰名单”，这意味着南非的银行和监管机构将受到更严格的审查。 Mr Ramaphosa has repeatedly pledged to get a grip. Alas, he has failed. One CEO sketches out a two-by-two diagram, the sort beloved by management consultants. One axis is labelled “morality”, the other “competence”. Mr Ramaphosa is placed in the moral but incompetent square, whereas Mr Zuma is in the one marked immoral and incompetent. Ramaphosa 先生一再保证要控制局面。唉，他失败了。一位首席执行官草拟了一张二乘二的图表，这种图表深受管理顾问的喜爱。一条轴线被标记为“道德”，另一条轴线被标记为“能力”。拉马福萨先生被放在道德但无能的广场上，而祖马先生则被放在一个明显不道德和无能的广场上。 When Janet Yellen, America’s treasury secretary, visited South Africa earlier this year, CEO s asked her to talk sense into the president. 今年早些时候，当美国财政部长珍妮特•耶伦(Janet Yellen)访问南非时，首席执行官们请她向总统讲讲道理。 “Earlier this year we came to the realisation that we’re on the edge as a country,” says Martin Kingston, chair of Business for South Africa, a pan-industry body. 泛行业组织“南非商业”(Business for South Africa)主席马丁•金斯顿(Martin Kingston)表示: “今年早些时候，我们意识到，作为一个国家，我们正处于危机边缘。”。 “We realised the status quo was just not tenable.” Drawing on the experience of procuring protective gear and vaccines during the pandemic, business groups formalised what had previously been only ad hoc efforts to assist the government. “我们意识到，现状是站不住脚的。”借鉴大流行期间采购防护装备和疫苗的经验，商业团体正式确定了以前只是协助政府的临时性努力。 For several years firms have sent staff to help run government departments, paying their wages. Corporate lawyers work at the prosecuting authority; bankers toil in the department of industry. 几年来，公司一直派员工帮助管理政府部门，支付他们的工资。公司律师在检察机关工作; 银行家在工业部门辛勤工作。 Several secondees are involved in Operation Vulindlela (“Make Way”), a joint initiative of the presidency and finance department that is meant to beat bureaucratic inertia. 几名候选人参与了总统和财政部联合发起的旨在打击官僚主义惰性的“让路行动”(Operation Vulindlela，简称“让路行动”)。 Last year a new unit, sitting in the presidency, was set up to reform the electricity industry. In March Mr Ramaphosa said that the private sector would pay into the Resource Mobilisation Fund ( RMF ), a whip-round designed to hire outside consultants. The RMF may well pay for a private team to try to reform logistics, too. “The aim is to create a parallel system of institutions to make things work in the moment,” says Morris Mthombeni of Gordon Institute of Business Science, based in Johannesburg. 去年，为了改革电力行业，成立了一个新的部门，担任总裁。三月份，Ramaphosa 先生说，私营部门将向资源动员基金(RMF)支付资金，这是一个旨在雇佣外部顾问的激励机制。俄罗斯货币基金组织很可能也会出资组建一支私人团队，试图改革后勤保障体系。总部位于约翰内斯堡的戈登商业科学研究所(Gordon Institute of Business Science)的莫里斯•姆托姆贝尼(Morris Mthombeni)表示: “我们的目标是创建一个平行的机构体系，让事情顺利进行。”。 Business groups stress that their efforts will be transparent. And the approach offers a better chance of success than relying on cabinet ministers who are instinctively sceptical of the free market. 商业团体强调，他们的努力将是透明的。这种方法比依靠内阁部长们提供了更好的成功机会，内阁部长们本能地对自由市场持怀疑态度。 But critics still worry about how business is behaving. Organised business has, at least until recently, had a case of Stockholm syndrome, argues Ann Bernstein of the Centre for Development and Enterprise, a think-tank. 但批评人士仍担心企业的表现。智库发展与企业中心(Centre for Development and Enterprise)的安•伯恩斯坦(Ann Bernstein)认为，至少直到最近，有组织的企业还存在斯德哥尔摩综合征。 “They have covered up for a failing president.” It would be better if business leaders explained to the public the true extent of South Africa’s crisis. “他们掩盖了一位失败的总统。”如果商界领袖向公众解释南非危机的真实程度，情况会更好。 South Africans might listen. As part of a global poll published earlier this year by Edelman, an American consultancy, South Africans said they trusted business more than government, the media or NGO s. Indeed, the gap of 40 percentage points between trust expressed in business (62%) and in government (20%) was larger than in any of the other 27 countries polled. 南非人可能会听。美国咨询公司爱德曼(Edelman)今年早些时候公布的一项全球调查显示，南非人表示，他们更信任企业，而不是政府、媒体或非政府组织。事实上，商界(62%)和政府(20%)之间的信任差距比其他27个受访国家都要大。 Mr de Ruyter argues that business is still too timid when it comes to calling out corruption. After he was poisoned he was “disappointed” by the reaction of other business leaders, many of whom sent private messages but stayed quiet in public. “But that’s the culture,” he sighs. 德鲁伊特先生认为，企业在揭露腐败方面仍然过于胆小。在中毒后，他对其他商界领袖的反应感到“失望”，其中许多人发送了私人信息，但在公开场合保持沉默。“但这就是文化,”他叹了口气。 According to Mcebesi Jonas, a former deputy finance minister, “Until you recognise that the ANC is unfixable you can’t fix the country’s problems.” The ruling party, he explains, is “fundamentally statist”. The situation is so dire, he contends, that society could “explode at any minute”. 按照前财政部副部长 Mcebesi Jonas 的说法，“除非你认识到非国大是无法解决的，否则你无法解决这个国家的问题。”他解释说，执政党“根本上是中央集权主义者”。他认为，形势如此严峻，以至于社会“随时可能爆炸”。 Enter the bosses 老板们来了 “This is a 1985 moment,” argues Songezo Zibi, a former business-newspaper editor and corporate executive, who in April set up his own political party. “这是1985年的一个时刻,”曾任商业报纸编辑和企业高管的松格佐•齐比(Songezo Zibi)辩称。齐比在今年4月成立了自己的政党。 Though he concedes that no single firm has the power that the Anglo-American conglomerate did in the 1980s, he says business must show similar courage today. Rather than take part in yet more state-led initiatives, the top 40 or 50 CEO s should gather with unions and NGO s to thrash out a new “social contract”. No amount of technocratic assistance will reform the government, he insists. “Until you change the politics, you’re not going to solve the problems.” 尽管他承认，没有哪家公司拥有上世纪80年代这家英美联合企业集团所拥有的实力，但他表示，如今企业界必须展现出类似的勇气。与其参与更多由政府主导的举措，40或50位首席执行官应该与工会和非政府组织一起研究出一种新的“社会契约”。他坚称，再多的技术官僚援助也无法改革政府。“除非你改变政治，否则你解决不了问题。” Business is unlikely to embrace such proposals—and not just because they are fuzzy. “Whether you like it or not, the ANC is the government today and will be the largest party tomorrow [ie, after the next election, due in 2024]. It would be foolish not to talk to them,” says Mr Kingston. 企业界不太可能接受这样的提议ーー不仅仅是因为它们模糊不清。“不管你喜欢与否，非国大现在是政府，明天(即2024年下次选举之后)将成为最大的政党。不和他们交谈是愚蠢的,”金斯顿先生说。 Firms are already wooing Paul Mashatile, who became Mr Ramaphosa’s deputy in March and is his most likely successor. “He seems like a man we can do business with,” says a CEO . He omits to mention that the same was said of Mr Ramaphosa. 公司已经在争取保罗 · 马希蒂尔，他在三月份成为拉马福萨先生的副手，也是他最有可能的继任者。一位首席执行官表示: “他似乎是一个我们可以合作的人。”。他没有提到 Ramaphosa 先生也是这么说的。 The insourcing of help to Mr Ramaphosa’s office does, however, show that large firms no longer feel insulated from South Africa’s problems. Potential competitors are relatively scarce: the World Bank suggests that South Africa has one-third of the small businesses it should have, given its GDP per person. Much of the revenue earned by the 40 largest JSE -listed firms comes from outside South Africa. 然而，向拉马福萨先生办公室提供帮助的内包行为确实表明，大公司不再感到与南非的问题隔绝。潜在的竞争对手相对稀少: 世界银行指出，考虑到南非的人均 GDP，南非拥有本应拥有的三分之一的小企业。在约翰内斯堡证券交易所上市的40家最大的公司的大部分收入来自南非以外的地区。 But there are limits to what even the biggest firms can do to protect themselves. Mike Brown, Nedbank’s CEO , says that large firms will perhaps be able to ride out problems in the short run. “But in the long term our fates are inextricably linked to the success or failure of the South African economy,” he laments. 但是，即使是最大的公司为了保护自己也是有限度的。莱利银行首席执行官迈克•布朗(MikeBrown)表示，大公司或许能够在短期内安然度过难关。“但从长远来看，我们的命运与南非经济的成败密不可分,”他哀叹道。 Mr de Ruyter puts it more starkly. Over unpoisoned coffee, he worries about what will happen if the country continues on its downward trajectory. “You can put solar on your roof, live in a gated estate, have private security, own a holiday home. 德鲁伊特先生说得更直白。喝着未加毒的咖啡，他担心如果这个国家继续走下坡路会发生什么。“你可以在屋顶上安装太阳能，住在封闭的小区里，拥有私人保安，拥有一套度假屋。 But if 90% of the population are suffering, how is that sustainable?” ■ 但如果90% 的人口都在遭受痛苦，这怎么可能持续下去呢? ￭ 八点一刻杂志网 www.badianyike.com This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2023/05/22/business-leaders-fear-that-south-africa-risks-becoming-a-failed-state 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2023/05/22/business-leaders-fear-that-south-africa-risks-becoming-a-failed-state 下载的 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 My ratings are better than yours 我的收视率比你高 African governments say credit-rating agencies are biased against them 非洲各国政府表示，信用评级机构对他们有偏见 They claim that pessimism about debt sustainability is unwarranted 他们声称，对债务可持续性的悲观情绪是毫无根据的 May 25th 2023 | Kampala 2023年5月25日 | 坎帕拉 S INCE THE spring of 2022, no country in sub-Saharan Africa has issued a bond on international markets. The yields on African debt have climbed so high that most governments can no longer afford to borrow. The usual explanation is that investors are fleeing risky assets at a time of global uncertainty. But is African debt as perilous as foreign lenders assume? 自2022年春季以来，没有一个撒哈拉以南非洲国家在国际市场上发行债券。非洲债务的收益率已经攀升到如此高的水平，以至于大多数国家的政府再也负担不起借贷了。通常的解释是，在全球不确定性的时期，投资者正在逃离高风险资产。但是，非洲债务真的像外国债权人所认为的那样危险吗？ African governments say no. “The perception of risk continues to be higher than the actual risk,” argued Senegal’s President Macky Sall , speaking as chair of the African Union ( AU ), in a speech to the UN last year. He pointed an accusing finger at the credit-rating agencies. So did Ghana’s finance minister, Ken Ofori-Atta, in the early months of the covid-19 pandemic, as funds dried up and downgrades loomed. 非洲各国政府拒绝了。塞内加尔总统马基 · 萨尔去年在联合国发表演讲时以非洲联盟(AU)主席的身份表示: “人们对风险的认识仍然高于实际风险。”。他把矛头指向信用评级机构。加纳财政部长肯•奥福里-阿塔(Ken ofori-Atta)在2019冠状病毒疾病疫情爆发后的最初几个月也是如此，因为资金枯竭，评级下调迫在眉睫。 “Are the rating agencies beginning to tip our world into the first circle of Dante’s Inferno?” he asked. In that poem the monstrous Minos wraps his tail around his body as he sorts sinners into nine tiers of damnation. “评级机构是否开始将我们的世界推向但丁的《地狱》的第一层?”他问道。在那首诗中，可怕的米诺斯用尾巴缠住自己的身体，将罪人分为九层地狱。 Credit-rating agencies prefer to declaim in spreadsheets. They combine economic data with their own subjective judgment to assess whether a borrower is likely to repay on time and in full. Countries with lower ratings face higher interest rates on international markets. 信用评级机构更喜欢在电子表格中声明。他们将经济数据与自己的主观判断结合起来，以评估借款人是否有可能按时全额偿还。评级较低的国家在国际市场上面临更高的利率。 A sovereign downgrade also raises the cost of borrowing for firms and deters foreign direct investment. Only two African countries, Botswana and Mauritius, are now rated better than junk. 主权信用评级下调还会提高企业的借贷成本，阻碍外国直接投资。目前，只有博茨瓦纳和毛里求斯两个非洲国家的评级高于垃圾级。 Misheck Mutize, an economist studying the issue for the AU, casts doubt on the rating agencies. When they look at Africa, they ignore good news and downgrade at the first sign of trouble, he reckons. They take a “blanket approach” to risk that can ignore the differences between African countries. 为非盟研究这个问题的经济学家 MisheckMutize 对评级机构表示怀疑。他认为，当他们关注非洲时，他们忽视了好消息，一有麻烦的迹象就会下调评级。他们对风险采取“一刀切”的态度，可以忽视非洲国家之间的差异。 Their jeremiads often become a self-fulfilling prophecy as investors flee. 随着投资者纷纷逃离，他们的哀叹往往成为自我应验预言。 Rating agencies respond that they apply the same methods everywhere. African governments have low ratings because they collect little tax, borrow in foreign currencies and face other genuine obstacles. 评级机构回应称，它们在任何地方都使用同样的方法。非洲各国政府的评级较低，因为它们几乎不征税，借入外币资金，并面临其它真正的障碍。 A credit rating assesses risk; it does not indict policy, says Matt Robinson, who manages the team that rates African sovereigns at Moody’s Investors Service. Without trusted information, investors would put even less capital into African markets. 穆迪投资者服务公司(Moody’s Investors Service)为非洲主权国家评级的团队负责人马特•罗宾逊(Matt Robinson)表示，信用评级是评估风险的，而不是指控政策。如果没有可信的信息，投资者对非洲市场的投资将更少。 The debate hinges on how to weigh factors that are not easily measured, like the strength of institutions, or political will. Rating agencies say that expert discretion is where they add value. But their African critics worry that too much space is given to gut feelings. 争论的焦点在于如何衡量那些不容易衡量的因素，比如制度的力量或政治意愿。评级机构表示，专家的自由裁量权是它们增加价值的地方。但是他们的非洲批评者担心太多的空间给了直觉。 Studies find that agencies tend to look more favourably on their home country and on places which are culturally similar. The big three rating agencies that dominate the global market are all headquartered in America. Moody’s and S & P Global each has one African office, in Johannesburg; Fitch has none at all. 研究发现，评级机构往往更看好自己的祖国和文化相似的地方。主导全球市场的三大评级机构的总部都设在美国。穆迪(Moody’s)和标普全球(S & P Global)在约翰内斯堡各有一个非洲办事处，而惠誉(Fitch)则一个都没有。 A recent report by the UN Development Programme argues that Africa would save money if rating agencies were more “objective”. 联合国开发计划署(UN Development Programme)最近的一份报告指出，如果评级机构更加“客观”，非洲将节省资金。 It compares the ratings assigned by the big three with country scores from Trading Economics, a data platform which uses a different economic model to assess risk and leaves little room for discretion. The rating agencies, it turns out, are sometimes (but not always) glummer. 它将三大评级机构给出的评级与 Trading Economics 给出的国家评级进行了比较。 Trading Economics 是一个数据平台，使用不同的经济模型来评估风险，没有留下多少自由裁量的空间。事实证明，评级机构有时(但并非总是)情绪低落。 If ratings matched the Trading Economics scores, African governments could borrow more often and more cheaply: the cumulative value of these gains could be $75bn, the report estimates. 如果评级与 Trading Economics 的得分相符，非洲各国政府可以更频繁、更廉价地借款: 报告估计，这些收益的累计价值可能达到750亿美元。 That is not clinching evidence of built-in bias, because human judgment may detect risks that pure number-crunching misses. Markets seem to think so. 这并不是内在偏见的确凿证据，因为人类的判断可能会发现纯粹数字运算失误的风险。市场似乎是这么认为的。 Some studies find that investors demand an interest premium of one or two percentage points on African debt, above what would be expected from economic data and credit ratings alone. 一些研究发现，投资者要求非洲债务的利息溢价高出经济数据和信用评级本身预期的一个或两个百分点。 Ghana’s bonds began to sell off even before the rating agencies issued gloomier judgments, notes Stuart Culverhouse of Tellimer, a British research firm specialising in emerging markets. 专注于新兴市场的英国研究公司 Tellimer 的斯图尔特•卡尔弗豪斯(Stuart Culverhouse)指出，在评级机构做出更悲观的判断之前，加纳的债券就已经开始遭到抛售。 When Moody’s issued a downgrade last year, Ghana’s government complained of an “institutionalised bias against African economies”. Yet it has now suspended repayments on much of its external debt. 当穆迪(Moody’s)去年下调加纳评级时，加纳政府抱怨称，该国“对非洲经济体存在制度化的偏见”。然而，如今它已暂停偿还大部分外债。 The argument about credit ratings is one sign of Africa’s renewed assertiveness. From the distribution of covid-19 vaccines to the handling of economic crises, Africans complain they are treated like second-class citizens in the world order. 有关信用评级的争论，是非洲重拾自信的迹象之一。从分发2019冠状病毒疾病疫苗到处理经济危机，非洲人抱怨自己在世界秩序中被视为二等公民。 Their case against the rating agencies is unproven. But the Africans may have a point. ■ 他们反对评级机构的理由尚未得到证实，但非洲人或许说得有道理。 ￭ This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2023/05/25/african-governments-say-credit-rating-agencies-are-biased-against-them 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2023/05/25/african-governments-say-credit-rating-agencies-are-biased-against-them 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Waiting for God’s choice 等待上帝的选择 Who will be Iran’s next leader? 谁将成为伊朗的下一任领导人？ Nobody knows who might grab the reins of power 没人知道谁会掌权 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 W HEN AYATOLLAH RUHOLLAH KHOMEINI , the charismatic leader of Iran’s Islamic revolution, died in 1989, hundreds of thousands of mourners packed the streets, fearful of what might happen next. Nowadays the mood is very different. For more than six months Iranians have been demonstrating en masse , chanting death to Ayatollah Ali Khamenei, the founder’s ailing successor as supreme leader. Yet no one knows who might fill his shoes when he goes—or whether the Middle East’s last theocracy will actually survive. 1989年，当伊朗伊斯兰革命领袖阿亚图拉的鲁霍拉·穆萨维·霍梅尼逝世时，成千上万的哀悼者涌上街头，担心接下来会发生什么。现在的情绪很不一样。六个多月以来，伊朗人民一直在集体示威，高喊着要杀死阿亚图拉 · 阿里 · 哈梅内伊(Ayatollah Ali Khamenei) ，哈梅内伊创始人身体不适的继任者，成为伊朗最高领袖。然而，没有人知道当他离开时，谁会接替他的位置ーー或者中东最后一个神权政体是否会真正存活下来。 Despite several cancer scares, the 84-year-old may hang on for years yet. But doubts about his health—and the strength of his system, the Velayat-e Faqih , or rule of the cleric—are growing. Even religious-minded Iranians have begun to lose faith in theocracy. 尽管有几次癌症恐慌，这位84岁的老人可能还要坚持几年。但人们对他的健康状况ーー以及他的体系—— Velayat-e Faqih (即神职人员的统治)的力量ーー的怀疑正在增长。即使是有宗教信仰的伊朗人也开始对神权政治失去信心。 The regime is patently unpopular. Elections have long been a charade. Non-alignment in foreign policy looks increasingly phoney as the regime snuggles up to China and Russia. The succession is bound to shake the status quo, says Pejman Abdolmohammadi of the University of Trento in Italy. “It could speed up a full authoritarian takeover or spark a democratic transition.” 这个政权显然不受欢迎。长期以来，选举一直是一场游戏。随着朝鲜政权依偎在中国和俄罗斯身边，外交政策中的不结盟看起来越来越虚假。意大利特伦托大学的佩杰曼•阿卜杜勒-穆罕默迪(Pejman Abdolmohammadi)表示，继任者必然会动摇现状。“这可能会加速全面的威权主义接管，或引发民主转型。” On paper the succession should be smooth. As things stand, the supreme leader has the final say on all matters of state, making him far superior to the president, who is elected under undemocratic conditions every four years. 理论上，接班人应该是平稳的。就目前情况而言，最高领导人对所有国家事务拥有最终发言权，这使得他远远优于每四年在非民主条件下选举产生的总统。 The supreme leader, by contrast, is picked by an assembly of 88 clerics. If this turbaned elite is unsure whom to choose, a triumvirate composed of the president, the chief justice and a senior jurist decides and a referendum confirms their choice. 相比之下，最高领袖是由88名神职人员组成的议会选举产生的。如果这些戴着头巾的精英们不确定该选谁，那么由总统、首席大法官和一名资深法学家组成的三人小组将做出决定，全民公决将确认他们的选择。 In 1989 the triumvirate, including a son of Mr Khomeini, wangled Mr Khamenei, a middle-ranking cleric who was already the president, into the job behind the assembly’s back. A constitutional referendum duly followed. 1989年，三巨头，包括霍梅尼先生的儿子，哄骗哈梅内伊先生，一位已经是总统的中层神职人员，在议会背后的工作。宪法公投如期举行。 Three decades later, Mr Khamenei has shrunk from naming a successor. Meanwhile the regime has been rocked by demonstrations and dissent after the death last September of a young woman in the custody of the so-called morality police. 三十年过去了，哈梅内伊先生已经不再指定继任者。与此同时，在去年9月一名被所谓的道德警察拘留的年轻女子死亡后，示威和异议震动了政权。 The two prime contenders have even weaker religious pedigrees than Mr Khamenei’s. Ebrahim Raisi , formerly the chief justice and now an arch-reactionary president, spent a relatively short time studying in seminaries. “He’s only as qualified as a mosque imam,” says Mustafa Fahas, a Shia Muslim academic in Lebanon who monitors events in Iran. 这两位主要竞争者的宗教血统甚至比哈梅内伊先生还要弱。前首席大法官、现任极端反动派总统易卜拉欣•莱西(Ebrahim Raisi)在神学院学习的时间相对较短。穆斯塔法•法哈斯(Mustafa Fahas)是黎巴嫩的什叶派穆斯林学者，负责监督伊朗的事态发展。 Mr Raisi’s father-in-law is an ultra-conservative cleric who, among other things, wants to ban music. This alienates him from most Iranians. Raisi 先生的岳父是一位极端保守的神职人员，他想要禁止音乐。这使他与大多数伊朗人疏远。 The other front-runner is Mr Khamenei’s second son, Mojtaba. He runs the supreme leader’s office and is close to Hossein Taib, until recently the powerful intelligence chief of the Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps ( IRGC ), the regime’s praetorian body. Mr Khamenei junior has lately been hailed on state TV as an ayatollah, the clergy’s highest rank, but has never held an official post and is rarely seen in public. Many regime stalwarts dislike the prospect of a dynastic succession: their revolution was founded on the overthrow of a shah. 另一位领先者是哈梅内伊先生的二儿子莫伊塔巴。他负责最高领导人的办公室，并与侯赛因•泰伊卜(Hossein Taib)关系密切。泰伊卜直到不久前还是伊斯兰革命卫队(IRGC)强大的情报负责人。小哈梅内伊先生最近在国家电视台被誉为阿亚图拉，神职人员的最高级别，但从未担任过官方职务，也很少在公众场合露面。许多政权的忠实拥护者不喜欢王朝继承的前景: 他们的革命是建立在推翻国王的基础上的。 The big new factor, compared with the situation in 1989, is the rise of the IRGC . It now has the whip-hand over the clerics. For three decades Mr Khamenei has built up its power as a defence against his rivals among the clergy—and against the risk of opposition in the street. The IRGC ’s membership in parliament has risen from 6% in 1980 (a year after the revolution) to 26% today, according to Chatham House, a think-tank in London, while clerical representation has dropped from 52% to 11%. The clergy used to post clerics to IRGC units; now the IRGC trains its own clerics and posts them to the seminaries, says Ali Alfoneh, an expert in America who wrote a book about Iran’s leadership succession. 与1989年的情况相比，最大的新因素是革命卫队的崛起。它现在对神职人员拥有控制权。30年来，哈梅内伊先生建立了自己的权力，以抵御他在神职人员中的对手ーー以及街头反对的风险。根据伦敦智库查塔姆研究所的数据，革命卫队在议会的成员比例从1980年(革命一年后)的6% 上升到今天的26% ，而教士代表比例则从52% 下降到11% 。过去，伊斯兰革命卫队的神职人员由神职人员担任; 现在，伊斯兰革命卫队培训自己的神职人员，并将他们派往神学院，美国专家阿里 · 阿尔弗内(Ali Alfoneh)说。阿尔弗内曾写过一本关于伊朗领导层继任的书。 As the IRGC has gained strength, many Iran-watchers think its commanders could keep the supreme leader in place but reduce him to a figurehead. It might prefer Mr Raisi as “a useful idiot who fits the bill”, says Saied Golkar, another Iran expert in America. The IRGC could conceivably take charge and junk clerical rule altogether, replacing it with an equally authoritarian alternative. The IRGC may be more pragmatic than the ruling clergy and try harder not to clash with the already disgruntled middle class. In foreign policy, however, it could be just as forceful, dropping Mr Khamenei’s vaunted fatwa against nuclear weapons and openly racing to build a nuclear bomb. While continuing to oppose America’s presence in the Gulf, it may nonetheless be more flexible in negotiating with “the Great Satan”. 随着伊斯兰革命卫队的力量不断壮大，许多伊朗问题观察家认为，其指挥官可以保留最高领袖，但把他降格为傀儡。美国的另一位伊朗问题专家赛义德•戈尔卡(SaiedGolkar)表示，伊朗政府可能更愿意让赖西先生成为“一个符合条件的有用的白痴”。可以想象，伊斯兰革命卫队完全可以接管并废除教士统治，用一个同样专制的替代方案取而代之。伊斯兰革命卫队可能比统治阶层的神职人员更务实，更努力地避免与已经心怀不满的中产阶级发生冲突。然而，在外交政策上，它可能同样强有力，放弃哈梅内伊先生对核武器的自吹自擂的追杀令，公开竞相制造核弹。尽管继续反对美国在海湾地区的存在，但在与“大撒旦”的谈判中可能更加灵活。 Some argue that the IRGC could impose a new social contract whereby Iranians would have more freedom to dress, drink and dance as they pleased. Iran might have a female education minister, the first in over 40 years. 一些人认为，革命卫队可以强加一个新的社会契约，让伊朗人有更多的自由，可以随心所欲地穿着、饮酒和跳舞。伊朗可能会有一位女性教育部长，这是40多年来的第一位。 (An Islamist firing squad killed the last one, in 1980.) Political freedom, however, would shrink still more. (1980年，一个伊斯兰行刑队杀死了最后一个。)然而，政治自由将进一步缩小。 That process may have begun. The morality police often leave unveiled women alone. But repression against dissent and crime is as harsh as ever. Executions rose last year by more than 80%, to 576. If an authoritarian IRGC regime were to bring back stability while relaxing personal mores, many Iranians might accept it. 这个过程可能已经开始了。道德警察经常不理会不戴面纱的女人。但是镇压异议和犯罪仍像以往一样严酷。去年，死刑执行人数增加了80% 以上，达到576人。如果一个独裁的伊斯兰革命卫队政权能够在放松个人习俗的同时恢复稳定，许多伊朗人可能会接受。 But true reformists would surely bid for a secular civilian system, rather than rule by either the IRGC or the clergy. Earlier this year, a former president, Muhammad Khatami, called for “a fundamental transformation”. 但真正的改革派肯定会争取建立一个世俗的文官制度，而不是由革命卫队或神职人员统治。今年早些时候，前总统穆罕默德•哈塔米(Muhammad Khatami)呼吁进行“根本性转变”。 A popular former presidential candidate, Mir Hossein Mousavi, broke his silence after 12 years of house arrest to call for a referendum on whether to keep Iran as an Islamic republic. 备受欢迎的前总统候选人米尔 · 侯赛因 · 穆萨维(Mir Hossein Mousavi)在被软禁12年后打破沉默，呼吁就是否保留伊朗伊斯兰共和国的地位举行全民公决。 Whatever the shape of the transition, it could well be messy. Only one thing is certain, says Mr Fahas, who is close to clerics within the regime. Mr Khamenei will be “the last real faqih ”. In other words, rule by clerics as envisaged by the 1979 revolution’s founding father will be over. ■ 无论过渡的形式如何，都可能是混乱的。只有一件事是肯定的，法哈斯先生说，他与政权内部的神职人员关系密切。哈梅内伊先生将成为“最后一个真正的法齐赫”。换句话说，1979年革命开国元勋所设想的神职人员统治将会结束。￭ This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2023/05/25/who-will-be-irans-next-leader 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2023/05/25/who-will-be-irans-next-leader 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 A wishful wedding 一个美好的婚礼 Can Jordan fall in love with Saudi Arabia? 约旦会爱上沙特阿拉伯吗？ Dynastic alliances don’t always last 王朝联盟并不总是持久的 May 25th 2023 | Amman 2023年5月25日 | 安曼 W HEN BRITAIN had an empire, Jordan’s King Hussein took British and American wives, among others. Seeking to keep a constituency closer to home happy, his son Abdullah, the present king, married a Palestinian. Now Jordan’s crown prince, another Hussein, is to marry a Saudi architect. 当英国拥有一个帝国时，约旦国王侯赛因娶了英国和美国的妻子。他的儿子、现任国王阿卜杜拉(Abdullah)为了让离家更近的选区保持快乐，娶了一位巴勒斯坦人。现在，约旦的王储，另一个侯赛因，将要嫁给一个沙特建筑师。 A century ago the Hashemites, who have always occupied Jordan’s throne, were the Middle East’s leading dynasty but later became poor relations to their oil-richer rivals in the Gulf. Some fear that Saudi Arabia under its bearish crown prince, Muhammad bin Salman, better known as MBS , wants to turn Jordan into a kind of fief. Two years ago Jordan’s security courts accused unnamed foreign powers (guess who?) of trying to replace Abdullah with his half-brother, Hamzah, a charge vehemently denied. 一个世纪以前，一直占据约旦王位的哈希姆王朝曾是中东地区的领导王朝，但后来却与海湾地区石油资源丰富的对手关系不佳。一些人担心，在看跌的王储穆罕默德•本•萨勒曼(Muhammad bin Salman)的领导下，沙特阿拉伯希望把约旦变成某种封地。两年前，约旦安全法庭指控了一些未具名的外国势力(猜猜是谁?)试图用他同父异母的兄弟哈姆扎(Hamzah)取代阿卜杜拉的指控遭到强烈否认。 Might the wedding curb MBS ’s ambitions, since the bride is related to the Sudairis, an influential clan that has often married into the House of Saud? And might the new alliance encourage the Saudis to pay off Jordan’s grinding debts? 这场婚礼是否会抑制住 MBS 的雄心壮志，因为新娘与苏戴瑞家族(Sudairis)有关系，这个有影响力的家族经常与沙特王室联姻？新的联盟是否会鼓励沙特偿还约旦沉重的债务？ The Jordanian royal family, including the crown prince’s mother, Queen Rania, has recently been careful not to display its wealth too showily. The wedding may be relatively modest. 包括王储的母亲拉尼娅王后在内的约旦王室最近一直小心翼翼地避免过于炫耀自己的财富。婚礼可能相对简单。 Gulf-watchers will note whether MBS attends. He has scant regard for many of his relatives, having imprisoned a bunch of them. He has a habit of embracing with one arm and casting adrift with the other. 海湾观察人士将注意到 MBS 是否会出席。他很少关心他的许多亲戚，因为他把他们中的一些人关进了监狱。他有一只手拥抱，另一只手随波逐流的习惯。 Earlier this month he hugged President Bashar al-Assad of Syria on his first visit to Saudi Arabia in over 12 years. Some say he then denied him a private audience and refused to promise any cash to rebuild the devastated country. 本月早些时候，他拥抱了12年来首次访问沙特阿拉伯的叙利亚总统巴沙尔 · 阿萨德。一些人说，他随后拒绝与他进行私下会面，并拒绝承诺提供任何资金用于重建这个遭受重创的国家。 One person who cherished Jordan’s link to Saudi Arabia is in jail. For years Bassem Awadallah ran King Abdullah’s court, before moving to the Saudi capital at Riyadh to advise MBS . But after he came back to Jordan he fell out with its regime and was sentenced to 15 years in prison there, allegedly for plotting with Hamzah. King Abdullah may need more than a marriage to make the Saudis more generous. 一个珍视约旦与沙特阿拉伯联系的人被关进了监狱。多年来，巴西姆•阿瓦达拉(Bassem Awadallah)一直负责阿卜杜拉国王(King Abdullah)的法庭，后来他前往沙特首都利雅得为 MBS 提供咨询服务。但他回到约旦后，与约旦政权闹翻，被判处15年监禁，据称是因为与哈姆扎密谋。阿卜杜拉国王可能需要的不仅仅是一场婚姻，才能让沙特更加慷慨。 “We’re becoming irrelevant,” laments a Jordanian former official. ■ “我们正在变得无关紧要,”一位约旦前官员感叹道。 ￭ This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2023/05/25/can-jordan-fall-in-love-with-saudi-arabia 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2023/05/25/can-jordan-fall-in-love-with-saudi-arabia 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Middle East & Africa 中东和非洲 Sections 部分 Britain 英国 Europe 欧洲 Electric cars could be crucial for the EU to meet its climate goals 电动汽车可能对欧盟实现其气候目标至关重要 Electric cars :: But only if the charging infrastructure is ramped up much faster 电动汽车: 但前提是充电基础设施建设速度要快得多。 Who are the militias raiding Russia’s Belgorod region? ❗ 🔄 Raid on Belgorod :: They have links to Ukrainian military intelligence and, in some cases, to the far right 突袭别尔哥罗德: 他们与乌克兰军事情报部门有联系，在某些情况下，还与极右翼有联系 Women take over France’s powerful trade unions 女性接管了法国强大的工会 French unions :: Amid speculation that a departing (male) leader could go into politics 法国工会: 在猜测一位即将离任的(男性)领导人可能从政期间。 Europe has shaken off Putin’s gas embargo 欧洲已经摆脱了普京的天然气禁运 Charlemagne :: Now it needs to think about how to deal with China 查理曼: 现在它需要考虑如何与中国打交道。 Middle East & Africa 中东和非洲 Sections 部分 Britain 英国

Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Electric cars 电动车 Electric cars could be crucial for the EU to meet its climate goals 电动汽车可能对欧盟实现其气候目标至关重要 But only if the charging infrastructure is ramped up much faster 但前提是充电基础设施建设速度要快得多 May 22nd 2023 | BERLIN 2023年5月22日 | 柏林 O N THE LAST weekend of April the Berlin showroom of NIO , a Chinese maker of electric vehicles ( EV s), was a happy place. On the first floor a family was celebrating a child’s birthday party. On the ground floor car enthusiasts inspected an SUV , a saloon and a racing car on display. The price tags were high (around €50,000, or $54,000, for the passenger car and almost €75,000 for the SUV ; the racing car was not for sale) but they seemed considerably lower than those of German rivals such as Mercedes or BMW —until you read the fine print. The battery is not included. It must be bought for €12,000 or €21,000, depending on capacity; or rented. 四月的最后一个周末，中国电动汽车制造商 NIO 在柏林的展厅里，是一个令人愉快的地方。在一楼，一家人正在庆祝一个孩子的生日聚会。在一楼，汽车爱好者们检查了一辆 SUV，一辆轿车和一辆赛车。价格很高(乘用车大约50,000英镑，或54,000美元，SUV 大约75,000英镑; 赛车是非卖品) ，但它们似乎比梅赛德斯(Mercedes)或宝马(BMW)等德国竞争对手的价格低得多——直到你读到附加条款。不包括电池。必须以12,000英镑或21,000英镑购买，视容量而定; 或租赁。 For many Europeans an EV is still unaffordable (an average petrol-powered passenger car sells for around €28,000), but sales of EV s keep growing rapidly . Fully battery-powered cars accounted for 12.1% of cars registered in the European Union ( EU ) last year, compared with 9.1% for EV s in 2021 and just 1.9% in 2019, according to the European Automobile Manufacturers’ Association ( ACEA ). A wider category, alternatively powered vehicles ( APV s), which lumps together pure electric and plug-in and non-plug-in hybrids, made up more than half the EU car market during the last quarter of 2022, with over 1.3m vehicles registered in total. It was the first time that APV s surpassed purely hydrocarbon-powered cars. 对许多欧洲人来说，电动汽车仍然是买不起的(一辆普通的汽油动力乘用车售价约为2.8万欧元) ，但电动汽车的销量仍在迅速增长。根据欧洲汽车制造商协会(ACEA)的数据，去年在欧盟注册的汽车中，全电池动力汽车占12.1% ，而电动汽车在2021年为9.1% ，2019年仅为1.9% 。2022年第四季度，纯电动车、插电式混合动力车和非插电式混合动力车的替代动力车(APV)占据了欧盟汽车市场的一半以上，注册总量超过130万辆。这是 APV 首次超越纯碳氢动力汽车。 “The EU is the global front-runner in the adoption of EV s,” stated a report last November by McKinsey, a consultancy. The union’s member states are themselves responsible for more than a quarter of the world’s EV production, and are big importers of them too. Forward-thinking carmakers and early-adopting consumers could create a world-leading EV ecosystem, which could generate new jobs and speed up progress towards climate goals—or so McKinsey enthuses. 咨询公司麦肯锡(McKinsey)去年11月发布的一份报告称: “欧盟在电动汽车的采用方面处于全球领先地位。”。欧盟成员国自身对全球超过四分之一的电动汽车产量负有责任，同时也是电动汽车的主要进口国。有远见的汽车制造商和早期采用电动汽车的消费者可以创造一个世界领先的电动汽车生态系统，从而创造新的就业机会，加快实现气候目标的进程。 The biggest stumbling-block to an electric-car future, though, is the charging infrastructure, which is not keeping pace with the increase in vehicle sales. According to ACEA, between 2016 and 2022 sales of electric cars in Europe grew almost three times faster than the number of charging-points that were installed there. 然而，电动汽车未来最大的绊脚石是充电基础设施，它没有跟上汽车销量的增长。根据 ACEA 的数据，2016年至2022年间，欧洲电动汽车销量的增长速度几乎是安装充电站数量的三倍。 Power to the people 权力属于人民 For electric vehicles to play their full part in the EU ’s ambitious goal of carbon neutrality by 2050, the EU will need to increase the number of public charging-points from around 300,000 to at least 3.4m and up to 6.8m by 2030, says another McKinsey report. That is a tall order. Up to 14,000 public charging-points need to be installed per week until 2030, says ACEA. The number is currently just 2,000 per week. 麦肯锡(McKinsey)的另一份报告称，要让电动汽车在欧盟雄心勃勃的2050年碳中和目标中发挥全部作用，欧盟将需要把公共充电站的数量从大约30万个增至至少340万个，到2030年增至680万个。这要求太高了。ACEA 说，到2030年，每周需要安装多达14,000个公共充电站。目前这个数字仅为每周2000人。 The EU will also need to ensure that chargers are more evenly distributed, to harmonise payment systems, and to provide many more fast-charging points for lorries. At the moment half of all EU charging-points are in the Netherlands (90,000) and Germany (60,000). A big country like Romania, which is six times larger than the Netherlands, has only 0.4% of all the EU’ s charging-points. 欧盟还需要确保充电器的分布更加均匀，协调支付系统，并为卡车提供更多的快速充电点。目前，欧盟一半的充电站位于荷兰(9万个)和德国(6万个)。像罗马尼亚这样的大国，面积是荷兰的六倍，只有欧盟所有充电站的0.4% 。 The imbalance in the charging infrastructure reflects the cost of EV s. Electric cars have a much higher market share in northern and western European countries, where average post-tax incomes are €32,000 a year, than in southern and eastern Europe, where the average is less than half that. 充电基础设施的不平衡反映了电动汽车的成本。电动汽车在北欧和西欧国家的市场份额要高得多，这些国家的平均税后年收入为3.2万欧元，而在南欧和东欧，这一数字还不到这一数字的一半。 Yet because of lower global prices for lithium and other materials needed for battery production, as well as government subsidies, the economies of scale of rapidly increasing production and stiff competition, especially from Chinese producers, prices for EV s are falling faster than some forecast. “By 2025 or 2026 most carmakers will be able to build petrol-powered cars and EV s for the same price,” predicts Daniel Röska of Bernstein Research. At the moment it costs about €3,500 less to build a petrol-powered Golf, a popular Volkswagen model, than an equivalent-size EV . 然而，由于锂和其它电池生产所需材料的全球价格下跌，以及政府补贴、产量快速增长的规模经济和激烈竞争(尤其是来自中国生产商的竞争) ，电动汽车价格的下跌速度超过了一些人的预期。伯恩斯坦研究公司(Bernstein Research)的丹尼尔•罗斯卡(Daniel Röska)预测: “到2025年或2026年，大多数汽车制造商将能够以同样的价格生产汽油动力汽车和电动汽车。”。目前，制造一辆汽油动力的高尔夫(一款流行的大众车型)比同等尺寸的电动汽车要便宜约3500英镑。 The charging infrastructure is likely to remain a sticking-point for longer. In March the European Commission announced a new law aimed at appeasing worries about gaps. 充电基础设施可能在更长时间内仍将是一个症结所在。今年3月，欧盟委员会(European Commission)宣布了一项新的法律，旨在平息人们对差距的担忧。 It sets targets for electric recharging and hydrogen refuelling, such as a requirement that for each battery-powered car in an EU member state, a power output of at least 1.3 kilowatt (k W ) must be provided by publicly accessible chargers. And for every 60km along the trans-European road network, a fast recharging station with a total of at least 150k W of capacity should be installed from 2025 onwards. 它设定了电力充电和氢燃料补给的目标，比如要求欧盟成员国的每辆电池动力汽车，必须由公众可获得的充电器提供至少1.3千瓦(kW)的电力输出。从2025年起，全欧公路网络沿线每60公里应安装一个总容量至少为150千瓦的快速充电站。 This is not nearly enough, says the European carmakers’ lobby. “Already today, a lack of charging and refuelling stations is severely hampering the market uptake of zero-emission vehicles,” comments Sigrid de Vries, ACEA ’s director-general. She predicts that “a significant infrastructure gap” will continue to limit the transition of Europe’s car industry. In response, some carmakers started building their own charging networks a while ago, led by Tesla. 欧洲汽车制造商的游说团体表示，这远远不够。ACEA 总干事 Sigrid de Vries 评论说: “如今，充电站和加油站的缺乏已经严重阻碍了零排放汽车的市场占有率。”。她预计，“巨大的基础设施缺口”将继续限制欧洲汽车业的转型。作为回应，一些汽车制造商不久前开始建立自己的充电网络，以特斯拉(Tesla)为首。 In January Mercedes announced it will put in place some 10,000 chargers worldwide by the end of the decade. 今年1月，梅赛德斯宣布将在2020年之前在全球推出约1万个充电器。 Fit for 55, a climate package with 13 proposals presented by the European Commission in July 2021, increased the ambition for the fight against greenhouse-gas ( GHG ) emissions to a reduction of emissions by 55% (compared with levels in 1990) by 2030 from the original 40% reduction proposed back in 2011. Transport is one of the keys to success. It accounts for 22% of the EU’ s total emissions. Within that, 70% comes from road transport; and road transport is the only sector where emissions have steadily risen since 1990. (The EU has reduced its overall carbon footprint by 30% since 1990, mainly by burning less coal to generate electricity.) In a report in October the commission said the EU was “on track to deliver on its climate ambition”, but that “swift action” was required to meet the 2030 goals as well as the 2050 net-zero ambition. That was why the Fit for 55 proposals were put forward. 2021年7月，欧盟委员会提交了一份包含13项提案的气候方案《适合55岁人群》(Fit for 55)。该方案将抗击温室气体(GHG)排放的雄心壮志，从2011年最初提出的40% 的减排目标，提升至2030年前减排55% (相比于1990年的水平)。交通是成功的关键之一。它占欧盟总排放量的22% 。其中，70% 来自道路运输; 道路运输是自1990年以来排放稳步上升的唯一部门。(自1990年以来，欧盟的碳足印总量减少了30% ，主要是通过减少燃煤发电。)欧盟委员会在10月份的一份报告中表示，欧盟“正在实现其气候雄心”，但要实现2030年的目标以及2050年的净零目标，就必须“迅速采取行动”。这就是为什么提出了55个合适的建议。 These proposals are still working their way through the union’s lawmaking process. 这些提案仍在工会的立法程序中进行。 Going for green 绿灯 Fit for 55 might sound like a low-impact yoga class for the middle-aged, but its range of plans could make Europe the world’s climate-policy leader, provided they are followed up. They include a ban on the sale of new internal-combustion engines ( ICE ) by 2035, and new rules that beef up the targets for emission reductions by 2030 for sectors covered by Europe’s emissions trading system ( ETS ). The ETS is a cap-and-trade scheme in which permits to emit carbon are allocated to aviation, energy-intensive industries and power generators, and can then be traded between them. The proposals also add transport and domestic heating to the existing ETS scheme. Moreover, new levies at the EU ’s borders for carbon-intensive imports, such as industrial materials, are part of the proposals, as well as the establishment of a “social climate fund” that will be used to help vulnerable households and small firms cope with the price increases of bringing heating and transport into the ETS . 适合55岁人群的瑜伽课听起来像是针对中年人的低强度瑜伽课程，但它的一系列计划如果得到跟进，可能会使欧洲成为世界气候政策的领导者。其中包括在2035年前禁止销售新型内燃机(ICE) ，以及针对欧洲排放交易体系(ETS)覆盖的行业制定新的规则，加大2030年前减排目标的力度。碳排放交易系统是一个总量管制和交易计划，其中碳排放许可被分配给航空、能源密集型行业和发电企业，然后可以在它们之间进行交易。这些建议还在现有的排放交易计划中增加了运输和家庭供暖。此外，在欧盟边境对工业材料等碳密集型进口产品征收新的关税，以及建立“社会气候基金”，将用于帮助弱势家庭和小企业应对将供暖和运输纳入碳排放交易体系的价格上涨。 At the end of April EU ministers approved six central laws of the Fit for 55 package, including the carbon border-adjustment mechanism and the social climate fund. Whether the EU will manage to meet its ambitious climate goals will now depend on member states’ willingness to deliver on the plans and to abstain from watering down any legislation that does not sit well with their voters, says Elisabetta Cornago of the Centre for European Reform, a think-tank in Brussels. 4月底，欧盟各国部长批准了“55个一揽子计划”中的6项核心法律，包括碳边界调整机制和社会气候基金。布鲁塞尔智库欧洲改革中心(Centre for European Reform)的伊丽莎白•科纳戈(Elisabetta Cornago)表示，欧盟能否实现其雄心勃勃的气候目标，现在将取决于成员国是否愿意兑现计划，以及是否愿意放弃削弱任何不符合其选民利益的立法。 Germany’s recent last-minute attempts to block the ban of new ICE powered cars from 2035 does not augur well. Germany insisted on allowing some new ICE cars to be built after 2035, so long as they are fuelled with carbon-neutral e-fuels, such as ethanol. Critics fear this will open the door to cheating. 德国最近在最后一刻试图阻止从2035年开始禁止新的 ICE 动力汽车，这不是一个好兆头。德国坚持允许在2035年之后生产一些新的内燃机汽车，只要它们使用乙醇等碳中性电子燃料。批评人士担心，这将打开作弊的大门。 Likewise, Italy’s right-wing government is now pushing back against the EU ’s plans to improve the insulation and energy efficiency of public and residential buildings. Buildings account for roughly 40% of the EU ’s energy consumption and 36% of its carbon emissions. Yet Italy’s housing stock is older and more dilapidated than that of many other countries, so meeting the targets will require extensive and costly renovations. 同样，意大利右翼政府现在也在反对欧盟提高公共和住宅建筑隔热和能源效率的计划。建筑物约占欧盟能源消耗的40% ，碳排放量的36% 。然而，意大利的住房存量比许多其他国家更老，更破旧，因此要实现这些目标，需要大规模和昂贵的翻修。 Italy’s construction association says that some 2m buildings will need renovation over the next ten years, at the eye-watering cost of €40bn-60bn a year, if it is to meet the EU ’s green homes plans. Italy’s government also backed Germany in its last-minute reversal on the end of the ICE , and criticises the proposal to slash industrial emissions. 意大利建筑协会表示，如果要满足欧盟的绿色住宅计划，未来10年将有大约200万栋建筑需要翻新，每年的成本高达400亿至600亿欧元，令人瞠目结舌。意大利政府还支持德国在洲际交易所会议结束前的最后一刻改变立场，并批评了削减工业排放的提议。 “Fit for 55 is entirely on track; that is, the main laws have literally just been voted and now need to be implemented,” says a Brussels official. Transport is in better shape than other sectors in terms of progress to net zero. 一位布鲁塞尔官员表示: “《适合55岁人群》完全在正轨上; 也就是说，主要法律刚刚投票通过，现在需要实施。”。就实现净零增长而言，运输业的状况好于其它部门。 Seven laws of the Fit for 55 package still need to be approved. But if they pass soon, the EU could stake a strong claim to be considered the world leader in the fight against climate change. ■ 适合55套的七条法律仍然需要批准。但如果它们很快获得通过，欧盟可能会强烈要求被视为抗击气候变化的世界领导者。￭ 八点一刻杂志网 www.badianyike.com For more coverage of climate change, sign up for The Climate Issue , our fortnightly subscriber-only newsletter, or visit our climate-change hub . 要获得更多关于气候变化的报道，请注册气候问题，我们每两周一次的订阅通讯，或者访问我们的气候变化中心。 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/europe/2023/05/22/electric-cars-could-be-crucial-for-the-eu-to-meet-its-climate-goals 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/europe/2023/05/22/electric-cars-could-be-crucial-for-the-eu-to-meet-its-climate-goals 下载的 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Raid on Belgorod 突袭别尔哥罗德 Who are the militias raiding Russia’s Belgorod region? ❗ 🔄 They have links to Ukrainian military intelligence and, in some cases, to the far right 他们与乌克兰军事情报机构有联系，在某些情况下，还与极右翼有联系 May 25th 2023 | KYIV 2023年5月25日 I N THE LATE afternoon of May 22nd footage emerged of two grinning pro-Ukrainian fighters in the midst of a cross-border raid into Russia. 5月22日下午晚些时候，两名亲乌克兰的武装分子在越境突袭俄罗斯的过程中露齿而笑的镜头浮出水面。 “The key to the border has been broken in half,” says one, quoting from “Everything is Going to Plan”, a cult anthem by Civil Defence, a Russian rock band. But the attack that the two men had helped engineer showed that, for Russia, things were very much not going to plan. “边境的钥匙已经被打破了一半,”其中一个人说，他引用了俄罗斯摇滚乐队“民防”(Civil Defence)的一首邪教圣歌《一切都按计划进行》(Everything is Going to Plan)中的话。但是这两个人协助策划的袭击表明，对俄罗斯来说，事情根本不在计划之内。 It included: an illegal crossing from Ukraine into Belgorod, a Russian region on the border, by tanks and infantry fighting vehicles; the death of at least one border guard; farm buildings on fire; a downed helicopter; traffic jams of fleeing locals; and the reported evacuation of a nuclear-arms facility several kilometres into Russian territory. 这些事件包括: 坦克和步兵战车从乌克兰非法越境进入俄罗斯边境地区别尔哥罗德; 至少有一名边境警卫死亡; 农场建筑起火; 一架直升机坠毁; 逃离的当地人交通堵塞; 据报道，一个核武器设施被疏散到俄罗斯境内几公里处。 Who was responsible for the incursion? 入侵是谁干的？ Members of two militias that oppose Vladimir Putin—the Russian Volunteer Corps and the Free Russia Legion—claimed to have “liberated” several border villages. The groups appear to be based in Ukraine, from where they mount their attacks. 反对弗拉基米尔•普京(Vladimir Putin)的两个民兵组织——俄罗斯志愿兵团(Russian Automotive Corps)和自由俄罗斯军团(Free Russia Corps)——的成员声称，他们已“解放”了几个边境村庄。这些组织似乎以乌克兰为基地，从那里发动袭击。 Russia says that the militias are a front for the Ukrainian army; Ukraine insists that they are composed of Russian dissidents. The reality is somewhere in between. Both militias do indeed recruit disaffected Russian nationals. Both also co-ordinate their activities with HUR , Ukraine’s military-intelligence agency. Yet the groups have no formal connection to the Ukrainian government. That allows Ukrainian officials to distance themselves from the militias’ operations, however implausibly. 俄罗斯说，这些民兵组织是乌克兰军队的一个前线; 乌克兰坚持说，他们是由俄罗斯持不同政见者组成的。现实介于两者之间。这两个民兵组织的确招募了心怀不满的俄罗斯国民。双方还与乌克兰军事情报机构 HUR 协调行动。然而，这些组织与乌克兰政府没有正式联系。这使得乌克兰官员能够与民兵组织的行动保持距离，尽管这种距离令人难以置信。 The Kremlin sought to downplay the embarrassing security breach. 克里姆林宫试图淡化这一令人尴尬的安全漏洞。 Russian officials described it as an attempt by Ukraine to “deflect attention” from what they claimed was their capture of the long-contested town of Bakhmut on May 20th (Ukraine says it still holds buildings and trenches on Bakhmut’s south-western edge). 俄罗斯官员称这是乌克兰试图“转移注意力”，因为他们声称5月20日他们占领了争夺已久的巴赫穆特河(乌克兰表示在巴赫穆特的西南边缘仍有建筑物和战壕)。 On May 23rd the Russian defence ministry claimed to have killed or expelled all the militia fighters from Belgorod, though it provided no evidence. 5月23日，俄罗斯国防部声称已经杀死或驱逐了别尔哥罗德所有的民兵战士，尽管没有提供任何证据。 A Ukrainian intelligence source offers a more realistic assessment. Two ideas underpinned the operation in Belgorod, he says. The first was to undermine Mr Putin’s domestic authority: if a strongman can’t guarantee security, what is the point of him? 一个乌克兰情报来源提供了一个更现实的评估。他表示，有两个想法支撑着他在别尔哥罗德的业务。第一是削弱普京先生的国内权威: 如果一个强人不能保证安全，那他还有什么意义？ The second was to pull Russian military reserves away from critical sections of the front line, in the run-up to a long-planned Ukrainian counter-offensive. 第二步是将俄罗斯后备军撤离前线的关键区域，以备乌克兰长期计划的反攻。 This is not the first foray by Ukraine-backed militias into Russian territory. 这不是乌克兰支持的民兵组织首次进入俄罗斯领土。 The Free Russia Legion and Russian Volunteer Corps were formed last year, under opaque circumstances, but came to prominence this March when they spearheaded an incursion from Ukraine into Bryansk, another border region. 自由俄罗斯军团和俄罗斯志愿者团是去年在不透明的情况下组建的，但今年3月他们带头从乌克兰入侵另一个边境地区布良斯克，引起了人们的注意。 Russia claimed that two civilians were killed in that attack, but offered no proof. 俄罗斯声称有两名平民在那次袭击中丧生，但没有提供任何证据。 The two militias differ in organisation and ideology. The Free Russia Legion is more chaotic and closer to HUR ; the Russian Volunteer Corps is better disciplined but has far-right leanings. Andrei Chernak, a spokesman for HUR , says that the “existential” danger Ukraine faces means that it cannot be too choosy about whom it collaborates with. Survival is Ukraine’s only focus, he said. “We are prepared to work with everyone.” ■ 这两个民兵组织和意识形态不同。自由俄罗斯军团更加混乱，更加接近 HUR; 俄罗斯志愿军团纪律更加严明，但是有极右倾向。HUR 的发言人 Andrei Chernak 说，乌克兰面临的“生存”危险意味着它不能太挑剔与谁合作。他说，生存是乌克兰唯一的焦点。“我们准备与每个人合作。”￭ This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/europe/2023/05/25/who-are-the-militias-raiding-russias-belgorod-region 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/europe/2023/05/25/who-are-the-militias-raiding-russias-belgorod-region 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 French unions 法国工会 Women take over France’s powerful trade unions 女性接管了法国强大的工会 Amid speculation that a departing (male) leader could go into politics 人们猜测，一位即将离任的(男性)领导人可能会从政 May 25th 2023 | PARIS 2023年5月25日 | 巴黎 I N MANY RESPECTS , this has been a dreadful year for French trade unionism. A four-month struggle against pension reform has come to little. New rules that raise the retirement age from 62 years to 64 have been forced through parliament, without a direct vote, and written into law. 在许多方面，今年对法国工会来说是可怕的一年。反对养老金改革的斗争已经持续了四个月，但收效甚微。将退休年龄从62岁提高到64岁的新规定已被迫在议会通过，未经直接投票，并写入法律。 Yet France’s unions are upbeat. Why? 然而法国的工会却很乐观，为什么呢？ One reason is that all eight French unions have stuck together throughout. Another is that the public-sector workers who usually fill the streets have been joined by young people and private-sector employees. 其中一个原因是，法国所有八个工会始终团结在一起。另一个原因是，通常走上街头的公共部门工作人员也有年轻人和私营部门雇员。 Only 10.8% of French workers belong to a union, compared with 23.5% in Britain and 16.3% in Germany. But French unions derive their strength from their ability to pack protest rallies, as well as the strong statutory role they enjoy within firms. 只有10.8% 的法国工人加入了工会，而英国和德国的这一比例分别为23.5% 和16.3% 。但是，法国工会的力量来自于他们组织抗议集会的能力，以及他们在公司内部所享有的强大的法定地位。 The most arresting reason involves a change of leadership. In March, for the first time in its 128-year history, the CGT , a union with links to the Communist Party, elected a female leader, Sophie Binet. Aged 41, the one-time student unionist is as relaxed in a television studio as she is behind a megaphone. She also has a knack for the one-liner. 最引人注目的原因是领导层的变更。今年3月，与共产党有联系的工会总工会在其128年的历史上首次选出了女性领导人索菲•比奈(Sophie Binet)。这位曾经的学生会成员现年41岁，在电视演播室里就像站在扩音器后面一样放松。她也很会说俏皮话。 In April she mocked an address by President Emmanuel Macron, designed to calm matters after rioting and saucepan-banging protests, by suggesting that it “could have been written by Chat GPT ”. 今年4月，她嘲笑了美国总统埃马纽埃尔•马克龙(Emmanuel Macron)的一份演讲，暗示该演讲“可能是由 ChatGPT 撰写的”。马克龙的演讲旨在平息骚乱和敲打平底锅的抗议活动。 Green, feminist and from a white-collar union branch, Ms Binet has her hands full dealing with what has traditionally been a muscular union centred on heavy industry. But this is also what makes her an interesting pick. 绿色、女权主义和来自白领工会分支的比奈，正忙于应对传统上以重工业为中心的强大工会。但这也使她成为一个有趣的选择。 Ms Binet thinks she can still get the government to shelve the new pension rules. A poll suggests that 60% of the French want the protests to continue. Unions have called a 14th one-day strike for June 6th. The opposition is trying to get parliament to annul the new pension age. 比奈认为，她仍然可以让政府搁置新的养老金规定。一项民意调查显示，60% 的法国人希望抗议活动继续下去。工会已经为6月6日召开了第14次为期一天的罢工。反对党正试图让议会取消新的退休年龄。 The feminisation of the labour movement is also under way at the CFDT , France’s biggest union. After ten years, Laurent Berger will stand down on June 21st and hand over to his deputy, Marylise Léon. In this case, though, it is the departing Mr Berger who is the source of speculation. 在法国最大的工会 CFDT，劳工运动的女性化也在进行之中。十年后的6月21日，Laurent Berger 将卸任并移交给他的副手 Marylise Léon。然而，在这种情况下，即将离任的伯杰先生才是猜测的源头。 The son of a shipbuilder and a familiar face at every protest march, he has earned credibility on the left for standing up to Mr Macron and holding the unions’ alliance together. Paris swirls with rumours that he is considering a political future. 作为一名造船工人的儿子，他在每次抗议游行中都有一张熟悉的面孔，他反对马克龙先生，维系工会联盟，因此赢得了左翼的信任。巴黎充斥着他正在考虑政治前途的谣言。 Asked by The Economist , Mr Berger insists he does not want to go into politics “if it’s just about giving your name to put on a poster”. Yet he is clearly thinking hard about how to create a political space between the radicalised left and what he calls Mr Macron’s “hyper-centre”. 在接受《经济学人》采访时，伯杰先生坚持说，“如果只是为了把你的名字印在海报上”，他就不想从政。然而，他显然正在努力思考如何在激进的左翼和他所称的马克龙先生的“超中心”之间创造一个政治空间。 Mr Berger has a new book out about the politics of work. The French left, he says, needs to move on from the idea that “work is misery, alienation, exploitation” and think about how to make it fairer, and more valued. 伯杰先生出版了一本关于工作政治的新书。他表示，法国左派需要摆脱“工作是痛苦、异化和剥削”的观念，考虑如何让工作更公平、更有价值。 If Mr Berger does go into politics, he could be a formidable challenger to any centrist successor to Mr Macron. The union leader deplores the way the president imposed pension reform, outrage that taps into a strong popular feeling. 如果伯杰先生真的进入政界，他可能成为马克龙任何中间派继任者的强大挑战者。这位工会领袖对总统强制推行养老金改革的方式表示遗憾，这种愤怒利用了强烈的民意。 Yet he is thoughtful about how work culture needs to change. Mr Berger plans to take some time out to think about the left’s future, as well as his own. “Am I going to disappear?” he asks. “Evidently not.” ■ 然而，对于工作文化需要如何改变，他是深思熟虑的。伯杰先生计划抽出一些时间来思考左翼的未来，同时也思考他自己的未来。“我会消失吗?”他问道。“显然没有”￭ This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/europe/2023/05/25/women-take-over-frances-powerful-trade-unions 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/europe/2023/05/25/women-take-over-frances-powerful-trade-unions 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Charlemagne 查理曼大帝 Europe has shaken off Putin’s gas embargo 欧洲已经摆脱了普京的天然气禁运 Now it needs to think about how to deal with China 现在，它需要考虑如何与中国打交道 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 W EAPONS COME in all shapes and sizes, from the Javelins that blow up Russian tanks to the F -16s Ukrainian aces may soon be flying. As his invasion of Ukraine got bogged down last year, Vladimir Putin, Russia’s president, reached for a weapon few imagined he would dare to wield: the throttling of deliveries from Russia’s vast gasfields to its main customers in Europe. 武器有各种形状和大小，从炸毁俄罗斯坦克的标枪到 F-16乌克兰 A 战斗机可能很快就会飞起来。随着去年入侵乌克兰陷入僵局，俄罗斯总统弗拉基米尔•普京(Vladimir Putin)拿出了一件几乎没人想到他敢使用的武器: 限制从俄罗斯巨大天然气田向其欧洲主要客户的输送。 Particularly for Germany and other countries that had piped the stuff straight into their homes and factories, doom-mongers predicted dire consequences—think double-digit falls in GDP , surging unemployment, not to mention freezing households. Yet Mr Putin’s weapon of mass economic destruction has turned out to be a dud. The crisis has all but passed, and far less harm came of it than expected. 特别是对于德国和其他直接将这些物质输送到家庭和工厂的国家来说，末日论者预言了可怕的后果ーー想想 GDP 出现两位数下降、失业率飙升，更不用说家庭被冻结了。然而，普京先生的大规模经济破坏性武器已经被证明是一个哑弹。危机几乎已经过去，造成的伤害比预期的要小得多。 Once they are done breathing a sigh of relief, policymakers should reflect on what this means as they ponder their next geopolitical challenge: how to “de-risk” the continent’s trade with China. 一旦他们松了一口气，政策制定者在思考下一个地缘政治挑战时，就应该反思这意味着什么: 如何“降低”非洲大陆与中国贸易的风险。 If Putin’s super-weapon fizzled, how much should Europe pay to rid itself of dependence on China for its imports of everything from rare earths to mobile phones? 如果普京的超级武器失败了，欧洲应该支付多少钱来摆脱对中国的依赖，中国从稀土到手机的进口无所不包？ In retrospect, the dependence built up by dozy policymakers and short-sighted businesses on Russian gas was reckless: gas accounted for a quarter of energy consumption in Europe, and Russia made up a third of that. 回想起来，昏昏欲睡的政策制定者和目光短浅的企业对俄罗斯天然气的依赖是鲁莽的: 天然气占欧洲能源消费的四分之一，俄罗斯占三分之一。 Yet the impact of the Kremlin turning off the tap (not entirely, as some Russian gas is still being piped west through Turkey and Ukraine, of all places) fell well short of the “gastastrophe” feared by some. 然而，克里姆林宫关闭自来水的影响(不完全是这样，因为俄罗斯的一些天然气仍然通过土耳其和乌克兰向西输送，而不是所有地方)远远没有达到一些人所担心的“灾难”程度。 Prices for natural gas in Europe have tumbled from over €300 ($324) per megawatt hour last summer to €30 in recent days. That is high, but back within the normal historical range. 欧洲天然气价格已经从去年夏天的每兆瓦时300欧元(合324美元)暴跌至最近几天的每兆瓦时30欧元。这个数字很高，但回到了正常历史范围内。 Even if prices jump once thermostats are cranked back up come winter, few expect them to spike very much. 即使到了冬天，一旦调节器重新启动，价格就会上涨，但很少有人认为价格会大幅上涨。 How has Europe gone from running out of gas to wondering where it will store the stuff? Benjamin Moll, Georg Zachmann and Moritz Schularick, three economists, recently compared the doom-mongers’ vision with reality in Germany. 欧洲是如何从天然气短缺转变为不知道该把天然气储存在哪里的？三位经济学家本杰明•莫尔(Benjamin Moll)、格奥尔格•扎克曼(Georg Zachmann)和莫里茨•舒拉里克(Moritz Schularick)最近将末日论者的愿景与德国的现实进行了比较。 Far from falling into an abyss, Europe’s largest economy suffered only the mildest of technical recessions. Some have put this down to luck, notably a mild winter in much of Europe reducing the demand for heating. 这个欧洲最大的经济体非但没有陷入深渊，反而遭受了最温和的技术性衰退。一些人将此归因于运气，尤其是欧洲大部分地区的暖冬降低了供暖需求。 In fact, the economists find, the weather was in line with recent years. If anything, other factors compounded the effect of missing Russian gas. French nuclear plants turned out to need unexpected maintenance at the worst possible time, for example. 事实上，经济学家们发现，这里的天气与近年来一致。如果说有什么不同的话，那就是其他因素加剧了俄罗斯天然气丢失的影响。例如，法国的核电站需要在最糟糕的时间进行意想不到的维护。 Rather, millions of firms and people in Europe have turned out to be unwitting heroes of making do. “Market economies have a tremendous ability to adapt to changing circumstances,” says Mr Moll, a professor at the London School of Economics. 相反，欧洲数以百万计的公司和员工在不知不觉中成为了勉强度日的英雄。伦敦政治经济学院(London School of Economics)教授莫尔先生表示: “市场经济具有巨大的能力，能够适应不断变化的环境。”。 Households turned down the heating, at least in countries where politicians did not cap energy prices in a bid to placate voters. Factories once dependent on gas found ways to switch to other fuels. 家庭减少了供暖，至少在那些政客们不会为了安抚选民而限制能源价格的国家是这样。曾经依赖天然气的工厂找到了转向其他燃料的方法。 The most power-hungry bits of industry, such as those producing paper, cement, aluminium and some chemicals, were sometimes shuttered. Instead, those products were imported: effectively, an alternative way to bring energy to Europe’s shores. 最耗电的工业部门，如造纸、水泥、铝和一些化学品的生产部门，有时会被关闭。相反，这些产品是进口的: 实际上，是将能源带到欧洲海岸的一种替代方式。 The supply of energy also adapted. New piped gas was found, from Norway, Algeria or Azerbaijan. Given sky-high prices, ships laden with liquefied natural gas ( LNG ) flocked in. The installation of a new facility to unload such boats was thought to need years, yet Germany pulled it off in ten months. Mothballed coal-fired plants were revived, and renewables installed. Countries far and wide contributed to Europe’s energy pivot, too. 能源供应也适应了。在挪威、阿尔及利亚或阿塞拜疆发现了新的管道天然气。由于天价，满载液化天然气的船只蜂拥而至。安装一个新的设施来卸载这些船只被认为需要数年，但德国在十个月内就完成了。封存的燃煤电厂得以恢复，可再生能源得以安装。欧洲的能源重心转移也得益于广泛的国家。 It can be difficult for a factory in Spain to make do without gas, say. But a utility in Vietnam or China (where demand was muted by covid-19 lockdowns) might have more options, and its cargoes of LNG could be diverted to Europe. The effects can be brutal: Pakistan faced power shortages as its utilities could not outbid gas-starved Europeans. 比如说，西班牙的一家工厂可能很难在没有天然气的情况下生产。但越南或中国的公用事业公司(由于2019冠状病毒疾病封锁，需求受到抑制)可能有更多选择，它们的液化天然气货物可以转运到欧洲。其影响可能是残酷的: 巴基斯坦面临电力短缺，因为其公用事业公司的出价无法超过天然气短缺的欧洲人。 From Nord Stream to No Stream 从北溪到无溪 Europe will not soon come to depend on Russian gas again—not least because the Nord Stream pipeline that brought lots of it was blown up by an unknown party in September. 欧洲不会很快再次依赖俄罗斯的天然气，尤其是因为输送了大量天然气的北溪(Nord Stream)管道在去年9月被一个不知名的团体炸毁。 But the episode has spooked politicians into thinking about which other nefarious powers might one day hold them to ransom. Prompted in no small part by America, Europe wants to ensure it is not beholden to China. 但这一事件让政治家们开始思考，有朝一日哪些邪恶势力可能会勒索他们。在美国的大力推动下，欧洲希望确保自己不会受制于中国。 What good is defusing the Russian gas weapon if it is replaced with a similar dependence on Chinese solar panels? 如果俄罗斯的天然气武器被类似的依赖中国的太阳能电池板所取代，那么拆除它又有什么好处呢？ One coping mechanism is to replace imports with stuff made in Europe. This idea, popular with trade sceptics in France, gained ground during the pandemic, when the EU ran short of facemasks and paracetamol. Voters were promised that production would be “reshored”. Thus mask factories popped up in France (now gathering dust); a paracetamol plant is in the offing, backed by millions in state aid. 一种应对机制是用欧洲制造的产品取代进口产品。这个想法在法国的贸易怀疑论者中很流行，但在疫情期间，当欧盟缺少口罩和扑热息痛时，这个想法得到了支持。选民们得到承诺，生产将“回流”。因此，在法国出现了口罩工厂(现在正在积灰) ; 一家扑热息痛工厂即将建成，得到了数百万国家援助的支持。 The Russian gas imbroglio has increased the list of products statists think Europe should make at home. Targets have been set, Soviet-style, for various sectors. The largesse for microchip and battery plants can already be counted in billions. 俄罗斯天然气纠纷增加了统计学家认为欧洲应该在国内生产的产品清单。已经为各个部门设定了苏联式的目标。对微芯片和电池工厂的慷慨捐助已经可以用数十亿来计算了。 The other approach is to keep buying from abroad, but diversify. Businesses that would herd to a single country’s suppliers, often Chinese ones, can be nudged to new shores. 另一种方法是继续从国外购买，但要多样化。那些将蜂拥至一个国家的供应商(通常是中国的供应商)的企业，可能会被推向新的海岸。 Under the impetus of more liberal (ie, less French) forces in Brussels, this is being mooted: public tenders for renewables projects, for example, will be penalised if products are sourced from a country that supplies more than 65% of the EU market. That will induce firms to look beyond the usual Chinese factories. Because the effect is gradual and harnesses market forces, it will be far less costly. 在布鲁塞尔更加自由(比如，少一些法国)的力量的推动下，这个问题正在酝酿之中: 例如，如果可再生能源项目的公开招标产品来自一个供应欧盟市场65% 以上的国家，将受到惩罚。这将促使企业把目光投向通常的中国工厂之外。由于这种效应是渐进的，并且利用市场力量，因此成本将大大降低。 The Russian gas embargo that failed is a useful reminder that economies are more adaptable than politicians think. But recession was averted in part thanks to expensive bungs from government. All the more reason for the right lessons to be learned. 失败的俄罗斯天然气禁运提醒人们，经济体的适应能力比政治家们想象的要强。但经济衰退得以避免，部分原因在于政府的高额补贴。那就更有理由吸取正确的教训了。 It pays to think where your stuff comes from. But some ways of de-risking are smarter than others. ■ 想想你的东西是从哪里来的是值得的，但是一些降低风险的方法比其他方法更聪明。 ￭ Read more from Charlemagne, our columnist on European politics: 阅读更多来自 Charlemagne 的文章，我们的欧洲政治专栏作家: Meet the lefty Europeans who want to shrink the economy (May 18th) 见见希望缩减经济规模的左翼欧洲人(5月18日) Once Russia’s best friend in the West, Austria is facing trouble (May 11th) 奥地利曾经是俄罗斯在西方最好的朋友，现在却面临着麻烦(5月11日) Emmanuel Macron hopes to reinvent himself in 100 days (May 4th) 埃马纽埃尔•马克龙(Emmanuel Macron)希望在100天内(5月4日)彻底改头换面 Also: How the Charlemagne column got its name 还有: 查理曼大帝的专栏是如何得名的 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/europe/2023/05/25/europe-has-shaken-off-putins-gas-embargo 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/europe/2023/05/25/europe-has-shaken-off-putins-gas-embargo 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Europe 欧洲 Sections 部分 International 国际的 Britain 英国 To survive, Britain’s NHS must stop fixating on hospital care 为了生存，英国的 NHS 必须停止对医院护理的执着 Ward mentality :: Community care is the route to better health 病房心态: 社区护理是改善健康的途径 Britain’s semiconductor strategy shows the bind the country is in 英国的半导体战略显示出该国所处的困境 Dear as chips :: But a modest approach may be the sensible one 但是一个适度的方法可能是明智的 With Hollywood on strike, foreign shows enjoy the limelight 随着好莱坞的罢工，外国电视节目受到关注 Best supporting actor :: The strike spells disruption—and perhaps opportunity 最佳男配角: 罢工意味着破坏ーー也许还有机会。 Britain’s economy may grow by more than expected, but inflation is stickier 英国经济的增长可能超过预期，但通货膨胀更为严重 Bittersweet :: That makes the choice facing the central bank easier 苦乐参半: 这让央行更容易做出选择。 British voters want more immigrants but less immigration 英国选民希望有更多的移民，而不是更少的移民 Bagehot :: The government’s immigration policy is incoherent because voters are incoherent 白芝浩: 政府的移民政策是不连贯的，因为选民是不连贯的。 Europe 欧洲 Sections 部分 International 国际的

Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Ward mentality 病房心态 To survive, Britain’s NHS must stop fixating on hospital care 为了生存，英国的 NHS 必须停止对医院护理的执着 Community care is the route to better health 社区护理是改善健康的途径 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 B RITONS STILL love the idea of the National Health Service ( NHS ). Its core principles—to provide health care that is comprehensive, universal and free at the point of delivery—are ones the public believes in. As it approaches its 75th birthday (in England) on July 5th, the service is still capable of providing outstanding care. 英国人仍然热爱国家医疗服务体系(NHS)的理念。它的核心原则ーー提供全面、普遍和免费的医疗服务ーー是公众所信奉的原则。随着7月5日(英格兰)75岁生日的临近，这项服务仍然能够提供出色的护理。 But the NHS is also in grave difficulties.
