The fever dream of winter is receding somewhat, but few will forget how many patients died waiting for ambulances and lying in hospital corridors. 但英国国民医疗服务体系(NHS)也面临着严重的困难。冬季的狂热梦想正在逐渐消退，但很少有人会忘记有多少病人在等待救护车和躺在医院走廊里死去。 Hospital waiting lists exceed 7m; efforts to curb them have been stymied by the largest wave of strikes in the service’s history. One in 11 posts in the NHS is vacant. 医院等候名单超过700万人; 限制他们的努力已经受阻于服务历史上最大的罢工浪潮。英国国民医疗服务体系中有十一分之一的职位是空缺的。 These problems cannot just be pinned on the aftermath of the covid-19 pandemic. Britain has fewer hospital beds per person than almost any other rich country, and fewer CT scanners. Its health outcomes lag behind its peers’. Among 18 rich countries, Britain has some of the worst five-year-survival rates for killer diseases such as lung, colon and breast cancers, and fares poorly on outcomes for heart attacks and strokes. Life expectancy in Britain lags behind most other rich countries. 这些问题不能仅仅归咎于2019冠状病毒疾病大流行的后果。英国的人均医院病床比几乎任何其他富裕国家都少，CT 扫描仪也更少。它的健康结果落后于同行。在18个富裕国家中，英国是肺癌、结肠癌和乳腺癌等致命疾病五年生存率最低的国家之一，心脏病和中风的后果也很糟糕。英国人的预期寿命落后于大多数其他富裕国家。 More than seven in ten of Britons now agree that their beloved NHS is “broken”. Politicians are increasingly willing to talk about the problems of the system. But there is less consensus about what should be done to fix things. Some argue that throwing more money at the NHS would be enough. Others think the time has come for root-and-branch reform. Others still believe that the existing system can be adapted and improved. Who is right? 超过70% 的英国人现在认为他们心爱的国民保健服务体系已经“崩溃”了。政客们越来越愿意谈论体制问题。但对于应该采取什么措施来解决问题，人们的共识较少。一些人认为，向 NHS 投入更多资金就足够了。其他人则认为，彻底改革的时机已经到来。其他人仍然相信现有的系统可以适应和改进。谁是对的？ It is true that more money will be needed. Since 2010 the NHS has done better than other departments but, save for a pandemic-related spike, the flow of money from central government has slowed (see chart 1). That has badly dented the finances of many NHS trusts, the organisational units which run health-care services. They balanced their books by raiding their capital budgets and by forgoing investment in medical equipment, buildings maintenance and digital records. 确实需要更多的资金。自2010年以来，英国国民健康保险制度(NHS)的表现优于其他部门，但是，除了与大流行有关的激增外，来自中央政府的资金流动已经放缓(见图表1)。这严重影响了许多 NHS 信托公司的财务状况，这些公司是运营医疗保健服务的组织单位。他们通过削减资本预算和放弃医疗设备、建筑维护和数字记录方面的投资来平衡账目。 A report in 2020 by the National Audit Office, which scrutinises public spending, found that across the OECD , a club of mostly rich countries, Britain invested the least capital per person in health care in 2015. The French and German systems respectively spent 21% and 39% more on health care per person than Britain did between 2010 and 2019, according to research by the Health Foundation, a think-tank. 2020年，负责审查公共支出的英国国家审计署(National AuditOffice)发布的一份报告发现，在经合组织(OECD)这个主要由富裕国家组成的俱乐部中，2015年英国在医疗保健方面的人均投资最少。根据智库健康基金会(Health Foundation)的研究，2010年至2019年间，法国和德国的人均医疗支出分别比英国高出21% 和39% 。 Nurses, junior doctors and others who have suffered a decade of real-terms pay cuts have taken to the picket lines. The underfunding has been most apparent in general practice, the first point of contact for most patients and the most cost-effective part of the system. 护士、初级医生和其他遭受了十年实际薪酬削减的人已经走上了警戒线。资金不足在一般实践中最为明显，它是大多数患者的第一个接触点，也是该系统最具成本效益的部分。 “General practitioners are paid £160 per year per patient: that is less than it costs to insure a gerbil,” points out James Morrow, a GP in Sawston, in Cambridgeshire. Many GP s have quit; many more intend to (see chart 2). 剑桥郡索斯顿的全科医生詹姆斯•莫罗(James Morrow)指出: “全科医生每位病人的年薪是160美元，比给一只沙鼠投保的成本还低。”。许多全科医生已经辞职; 更多的人打算辞职(见表2)。 But spending more money cannot be the only answer. At £160bn ($198bn) the annual budget the government gives NHS England to commission health services is already larger than the economies of all but four African countries. Health care is the biggest line item in the government’s budget: 38p of every pound the state spends on public services goes on the NHS . 但是花更多的钱并不是唯一的答案。英国政府给予 NHS 英格兰委托医疗服务的年度预算为1600亿欧元(合1980亿美元) ，已经超过了除4个非洲国家以外的所有经济体。医疗保健是政府预算中最大的项目: 国家在公共服务上的每一英镑支出中有38便士用于国民保健服务。 The King’s Fund, a think-tank, has calculated that if the 50 years from 2012 were to follow the trajectory of the previous 50 years, then Britain would be spending almost a fifth of GDP on the NHS and employing one-eighth of the working population. But by the 2060s Britain’s ageing population will resemble an ice-cream cornet rather than a pyramid. A narrowing cone of younger people, rather than a broad base, would not be able to provide that amount of labour and taxes. 英国国王基金会(King’s Fund)估计，如果从2012年开始的50年按照过去50年的轨迹发展，那么英国将花费国民生产总值(GDP)近五分之一的资金用于国民保健服务(NHS) ，并雇佣八分之一的劳动人口。但到本世纪60年代，英国的老龄化人口将变成一个冰淇淋小喇叭，而不是一个金字塔。由年轻人组成的一个不断缩小的圆锥体，而不是一个广泛的基础，将无法提供那么多的劳动力和税收。 Resources are already being wasted. A report by the Institute for Fiscal Studies, a think-tank, found last year that, despite getting more money and more staff, the NHS is less productive than it was before the pandemic. 资源已经被浪费了。智囊机构财政研究所(Institute for Fiscal Studies)去年发布的一份报告显示，尽管获得了更多资金和更多员工，但英国国民医疗服务体系的生产力不如疫情爆发前。 Some conclude that what the NHS needs is a radical overhaul. “It’s electoral suicide to go against the NHS ,” says Professor Stephen Smith, who used to run one of its trusts. “But we can’t go on like this.” 一些人得出结论，英国国民保健制度需要彻底改革。“与 NHS 作对简直是选举自杀,”史蒂芬 · 史密斯教授说，他曾经管理过 NHS 的一个信托基金。“但我们不能再这样下去了” No time for patients 没时间接待病人 One option for root-and-branch reform is to charge more patients at the point of use. 根本和分支改革的一个选择是在使用点向更多的病人收费。 Last September minutes leaked from a secret meeting between the chiefs of Scotland’s National Health Service showing that they had discussed the possibility of a “two-tier” system in which the wealthy would pay for their care. 去年9月，苏格兰国家医疗服务体系(National Health Service)负责人秘密会议的会议记录显示，他们讨论了建立一个“双层”医疗体系的可能性，在这个体系中，富人将为他们的医疗费用买单。 The introduction of small co-payments for overstretched services like general practice and accident & emergency ( A&E ) departments has also been mooted. 对于像全科医生和急诊部门这样超负荷的服务，引入小额共同支付也已经提出了讨论。 To the charge that rationing has no part in a universal health-care system, proponents riposte that it already exists. Prescriptions are free for some patients, for example, but not for others. And whereas other health-care systems rely on price to manage demand, the NHS uses hidden mechanisms: administrative hoops, snarling receptionists and endless waiting lists. 对于定量配给在全民医疗保健系统中没有一席之地的指责，支持者反驳说它已经存在。例如，对于某些病人，处方是免费的，但对于其他病人则不是。而其他的医疗保健系统依靠价格来管理需求，NHS 则使用隐藏的机制: 行政管理、咆哮的接待员和没完没了的等待名单。 Co-payments might curb demand, but they would do so by deterring patients from seeking care. Some people, particularly the poor, would not seek treatment when they need it. 共同支付可能会抑制需求，但它们会阻止患者寻求治疗。有些人，特别是穷人，在需要的时候不会寻求治疗。 In dentistry, where patients are already charged for appointments (with some exemptions), the government has ramped up patient charges while reducing its own funding over the past decade. 在牙科领域，病人已经需要支付预约费用(除了一些例外) ，政府在过去十年里增加了病人的费用，同时减少了自己的资金。 England now spends the lowest share of its health budget on dentistry of any country in Europe. The underfunded dental contract has pushed thousands of dentists into private practice. 英格兰现在在牙科方面的花费是欧洲所有国家中最少的。资金不足的牙科合同已经迫使成千上万的牙医进入私人诊所。 That has created a two-tier system in which the rich go private and those who cannot afford or find an NHS dentist resort to DIY treatments involving pliers and alcohol. 这就形成了一个双层体系，富人私下里看病，而那些负担不起或找不到 NHS 牙医的人则需要用钳子和酒精自己动手治疗。 Another suggestion for fundamental reform is that Britain should switch to a social-insurance model, similar to that operating in France, Germany or the Netherlands. At the moment the NHS is funded out of general taxation. Under a social-insurance model, employees and employers pay contributions into a not-for-profit fund which is then used to purchase health care. Advocates suggest that by earmarking money for the NHS , this system might make people feel more responsible for their own health. And by taking responsibility for financing health care away from the government, it might also reduce the temptation for politicians to meddle. 另一个关于根本性改革的建议是，英国应该转向社会保险模式，类似于法国、德国或荷兰的模式。目前，英国国家医疗服务体系的资金来自一般税收。在社会保险模式下，雇员和雇主向非营利基金缴纳保费，然后用于购买医疗保健。支持者建议，通过为 NHS 拨款，这个系统可能会让人们感到对自己的健康负有更大的责任。而且，通过从政府手中接过医疗保健融资的责任，这也可能减少政治家干预的诱惑。 Yet meta-analyses suggest that there is no relationship between a country’s choice of funding system and its health outcomes. And all health systems have their own challenges. In the Netherlands GP s spend almost three times as much of their time on admin as their British counterparts, who already feel they are drowning in paperwork. 然而，荟萃分析表明，一个国家对资金体系的选择与其卫生结果之间没有关系。所有的卫生系统都有自己的挑战。在荷兰，全科医生花在管理上的时间几乎是英国全科医生的三倍，英国全科医生已经觉得自己被文书工作淹没了。 Making changes of this magnitude would be a huge political gamble, too. A recent survey by Ipsos, a pollster, found that 90% of Britons believe the NHS should be free at the point of delivery, and 84% think the NHS should be funded primarily through taxation. Such consensus is not found on other issues. Trying to switch to a new model would be “like throwing rocks at Everest”, says Alastair McLellan, who edits the Health Service Journal, a website. “The NHS is a fixed fact. Clever policymakers work with that, not against it.” 做出如此重大的改变也将是一场巨大的政治赌博。民意调查机构益普索(Ipsos)最近的一项调查发现，90% 的英国人认为 NHS 在交付时应该是免费的，84% 的人认为 NHS 应该主要通过税收获得资金。在其他问题上没有达成这样的共识。阿拉斯泰尔•麦克莱伦(Alastair McLellan)是《健康服务杂志》(Health Service Journal)的编辑，他表示，尝试转向一种新模式“就像向珠穆朗玛峰扔石头”。“ NHS 是一个不变的事实。聪明的政策制定者与之合作，而不是反对它。” If ripping up the NHS ’s founding principles is not an answer, radicalism is nonetheless required. That is because the NHS is far too focused on one element of the health-care system—hospitals. Aneurin Bevan, the Labour politician who was the architect of the NHS , called hospitals the “vertebrae of the health system”. It is more accurate to think of them as sponges. 如果撕毁 NHS 的创立原则不是一个答案，激进主义仍然是必要的。这是因为 NHS 过于关注医疗保健系统的一个组成部分——医院。NHS 的设计者、工党政治家阿内林•贝文(Aneurin Bevan)将医院称为“医疗体系的脊椎”。更准确地说，它们是海绵。 In the three years to 2018-19, hospitals’ share of total NHS spending increased from 63% to 65%; the share accounted for by primary and community services fell from 20% to 19%. 在截至2018-2019年的三年中，医院在 NHS 总支出中所占的比例从63% 上升到65% ; 初级和社区服务所占的比例从20% 下降到19% 。 By current prices, 47% of all central- and local-government spending on health care in Britain goes on hospitals, compared with 44% of funding from comparable schemes in France, 36% in the Netherlands and 33% in Germany. Within the OECD , when comparing government-funded or compulsory schemes, only America spends a bigger share of its GDP on hospitals (see chart 3). 按照目前的价格计算，英国中央和地方政府在医疗保健方面的支出中，有47% 用于医院，相比之下，法国同类计划的资金支出中，有44% 用于医院，荷兰为36% ，德国为33% 。在经济合作与发展组织(OECD)中，比较政府资助或强制性计划，只有美国在医院上的花费占其 GDP 的比重更大(见表3)。 A hospital-focused model worked well enough when the NHS was mainly there to provide urgent treatment for infections and injuries. Those days are long gone. The latest available data showed that half of all GP appointments and 70% of inpatient bed days were taken by those with long-term conditions, such as diabetes. Family doctors and community nurses are best-placed to manage these chronic conditions, yet only 8% of the NHS budget goes to general practice, down from 11% in 2005-06. Hospitals are plainly not the places to prevent things which contribute to ill-health, like obesity. Britain is the third-fattest country in Europe; if everyone was a healthy weight, a recent study found, the NHS could save up to £14bn a year. 当 NHS 主要为感染和伤害提供紧急治疗时，以医院为中心的模式运作良好。那些日子早就过去了。最新的可用数据显示，半数的全科医生预约和70% 的住院病床天数是由那些长期的条件，如糖尿病。家庭医生和社区护士是处理这些慢性病的最佳人选，然而只有8% 的 NHS 预算用于全科医生，低于2005-06年的11% 。显然，医院不是预防肥胖等导致不健康的疾病的场所。英国是欧洲第三胖的国家; 最近的一项研究发现，如果每个人都有健康的体重，NHS 每年可以节省140亿英镑。 It gets worse. Hospitals are primarily paid on the basis of “activity”, which at times incentivises them to perform unnecessary treatments and not collaborate with other bits of the NHS . An estimated 10% of the cost of inpatient care in hospitals is spent looking after those in the final year of life, often on ineffective tests and treatments. 还有更糟的。医院的报酬主要基于“活动”，这有时会激励他们进行不必要的治疗，而不与 NHS 的其他部分合作。据估计，医院住院治疗费用的10% 用于照顾生命最后一年的病人，这些人往往接受无效的检查和治疗。 Meanwhile, general practice and community services are often funded by fixed budgets in exchange for broadly defined services. They have little incentive to reduce referrals to other services. 与此同时，一般做法和社区服务往往由固定预算供资，以换取定义广泛的服务。他们几乎没有动力减少转介到其他服务。 The system does not just suck patients into hospitals but also keeps them there for too long, in part because of a shortage of carers. More than one in ten beds are occupied by people who do not need them. 这个系统不仅把病人吸引到医院，而且让他们在医院里呆的时间太长，部分原因是缺乏护理人员。超过十分之一的床位被不需要它们的人占用。 To speed up delays in discharging patients, some hospitals are even giving money to cash-strapped councils to pay for social care. “If everything is focused on acute hospitals that have already spent nearly half of the entire NHS budget, the NHS will fail,” says Joe Harrison, who runs an acute trust in Milton Keynes. 为了加快病人出院的时间，一些医院甚至向现金短缺的地方议会提供资金，以支付社会医疗费用。“如果一切都集中在急性医院，已经花费了近一半的 NHS 预算，NHS 将失败，说:”乔哈里森，谁运行的米尔顿凯恩斯急性信托。 Such warnings are not new. Nor are calls to shift money away from crisis management in hospitals and towards community care and prevention. 这样的警告并不新鲜。要求将资金从医院危机管理转向社区护理和预防的呼吁也没有得到响应。 “We are often not invited to meetings as it is seen as predictable what we will say,” says Kamila Hawthorne, the chair of the Royal College of GP s. “We’re constantly banging the drum on funding.” That might invite scepticism that anything will change. But two big building-blocks are moving into place. 英国皇家全科医生学院(Royal College of GP)院长卡米拉•霍桑(Kamila Hawthorne)表示: “我们往往没有受到邀请参加会议，因为人们认为，我们会说些什么是可以预测的。”。“我们一直在大力推销资金。”这可能会引发怀疑，认为一切都会改变。但是两个巨大的组成部分正在移动到位。 One gives regions more freedom to improve the health of their own populations. The NHS has long suffered from top-downitis, with the centre issuing a blizzard of orders and performance metrics. 一是给予各地区更多的自由，以改善本地区人口的健康状况。英国国民保健服务体系(NHS)长期以来一直受到自上而下之风的困扰，该中心发布了大量订单和绩效指标。 In his book “Zero”, Jeremy Hunt, a former health secretary and now the chancellor of the exchequer, recalls asking one hospital how many instructions it received related to patient safety: the answer was 108 documents a year. 前卫生大臣、现任财政大臣的杰里米•亨特(Jeremy Hunt)在他的著作《零》(Zero)中回忆道，他曾问一家医院，它收到了多少与患者安全有关的指示，答案是每年108份文件。 Such bureaucracy is hugely counterproductive—a classic case of hitting the target but missing the point. 这种官僚作风极大地适得其反，是达到目标但没有抓住要点的典型案例。 The latest organisational shake-up of the NHS is therefore promising. In 2022 the Health and Care Act created 42 Integrated Care Systems ( ICS s), each covering an average of 1.5m people. They bring together NHS bodies, local authorities and charities to plan and deliver health and care services in a more joined-up way. Each ICS also chooses longer-term goals to work towards, such as reducing health inequality. 因此，英国国民健康保险制度(NHS)最新的组织结构调整颇有希望。2022年，《健康与护理法案》(Health and Care Act)创建了42个综合护理系统(ICS) ，每个系统平均覆盖150万人。它们将国民保健服务机构、地方当局和慈善机构聚集在一起，以更加协调的方式规划和提供卫生保健服务。每个 ICS 还选择较长期的目标来努力，如减少健康不平等。 Bits of the NHS have already worked out the value of co-ordination. “It’s just completely bloody obvious that we all need to work together,” says Sir Jim Mackey, the boss of Northumbria Healthcare Foundation Trust, which has been working with other partners for decades. But ICS s should make that more common. To take one example, on average homeless people use eight times as many health services as other people (mainly A&E and ambulances). Instead of trying to manage this demand, West Yorkshire’s ICS works to reduce homelessness—for instance, by helping tenants at risk of eviction to improve their mental health. “You have to start with the needs of the people, not structures,” says Rob Webster, its chief executive. 英国国民健康保险制度(NHS)的一些部门已经发现了协作的价值。“很明显，我们需要共同努力,”诺森比亚医疗基金会信托基金的老板吉姆 · 麦基爵士说，该基金会几十年来一直与其他合作伙伴合作。但是 ICS 应该会让这种情况变得更加普遍。举个例子，平均而言，无家可归者使用的医疗服务是其他人(主要是急症室和救护车)的8倍。西约克郡的 ICS 不是试图管理这种需求，而是致力于减少无家可归现象ーー例如，通过帮助面临被驱逐风险的租户来改善他们的心理健康。该公司首席执行官罗伯•韦伯斯特(Rob Webster)表示: “你必须从人们的需求着手，而不是从结构着手。”。 To be truly beneficial, ICS s will need to be made more “hard-edged”, says Axel Heitmueller of Imperial College Health Partners, a partnership of NHS providers. Data can already be used to measure a small set of population-health outcomes, such as healthy life expectancy. To incentivise improvements on these measures, he reckons ICS s should be able to “earn back” a proportion of the savings they make from helping people to get healthier and be allowed to reinvest this money. 帝国理工学院健康伙伴(Imperial College Health Partners)的阿克塞尔•海特穆勒(Axel Heitmueller)表示，为了真正实现效益，ICS 将需要变得更加“锋利”。帝国理工学院健康伙伴是英国国民健康保险制度(NHS)提供商的合作伙伴。数据已经可以用来衡量一小组人口健康结果，如健康预期寿命。他认为，为了激励这些措施的改进，ICS 应该能够“赚回”一部分节省下来的钱，帮助人们变得更健康，并被允许对这些钱进行再投资。 The second crucial building-block for reform is technology. There is an awful lot still to do in this area. Less than half of ICS s have a data platform to help them manage their populations’ health. Privacy concerns and bungled implementation have impeded efforts to join up patient records—and to realise the potential of the NHS, which is unusual in holding cradle-to-grave information on a national population, as one of the most valuable repositories of health data in the world. 改革的第二个关键组成部分是技术。在这个领域还有很多事情要做。不到一半的 ICS 拥有一个数据平台来帮助他们管理人口健康。隐私方面的担忧和拙劣的实施阻碍了整合患者记录的努力ーー以及实现 NHS 作为世界上最有价值的健康数据库之一的潜力。 NHS 在掌握全国人口从摇篮到坟墓的信息方面非同寻常。 But the NHS is taking steps towards a future of greater productivity and more personalised care. It has already had success rolling out virtual wards—for example, using pulse oximeters to monitor the breathing efficiency of recently discharged patients. 但英国国民保健服务体系(NHS)正朝着提高生产率和更个性化护理的未来迈进。它已经成功地推出了虚拟病房ーー例如，使用脉搏血氧计监测最近出院病人的呼吸效率。 It is carrying out breast-cancer scans in shopping centres and cataract surgery in car parks. In partnership with Our Future Health, a programme that aims to track the health of 5m Britons, blood samples are now being taken in the back rooms of Boots, a high-street pharmacist. 它正在购物中心和停车场的白内障手术进行乳腺癌扫描。通过与“我们的未来健康”(Our Future Health)合作，目前正在商业街药剂师 Boots 的密室里采集血液样本。“我们的未来健康”旨在跟踪500万英国人的健康状况。 In future the results will allow more targeted screening for cancer and the creation of individual risk scores. Genomic sequencing should help the NHS to spot diseases earlier, to the benefit of patients and taxpayers. 在未来，研究结果将允许更有针对性的癌症筛查和创建个人风险评分。基因组测序应该有助于 NHS 更早地发现疾病，从而使患者和纳税人受益。 Patients should soon be able to access personalised health advice on the NHS app, and receive nudges to eat better and exercise more, as already happens in Singapore, says Seb Rees of Reform, a think-tank. In future the app could become the new front door to the NHS , with options for self-referrals, self-monitoring and home testing. Through Patients Know Best, a software company, patients can already get access via the app to an individual patient record which shows them their results and their medical records. 智囊机构 Reform 的塞布•里斯(Seb Rees)表示，患者应该很快就能在 NHS 应用程序上获得个性化的健康建议，并得到改善饮食和增加锻炼的推动，就像新加坡已经发生的那样。在未来，这个应用程序可能会成为 NHS 的新大门，提供自我推荐、自我监控和家庭测试的选项。通过一家名为“病人知道最好”的软件公司，病人已经可以通过这个应用程序访问病人的个人记录，其中显示了他们的结果和医疗记录。 Fran Husson, a patient with multiple conditions, uses this service to make personalised plans for her care in conjunction with her consultant. “It has been transformative,” she says. Fran Husson，一个患有多种疾病的病人，利用这项服务与她的顾问一起为她的治疗制定个性化的计划。“这是一种变革,”她表示。 The NHS has a long list of problems to tackle. Britons are getting older: that will drive up demand for health care. Culture is critically important: the NHS suffers from too much respect for clinical hierarchies, too much fear of blame when things go wrong and too little focus on the patient. Retention of staff is poor. Politicians are still liable to meddle, and to funnel money to the latest crisis. NHS 有一长串的问题需要解决。英国人正在变老: 这将推高对医疗保健的需求。文化是至关重要的: 英国国民医疗服务体系(NHS)存在着对临床等级制度的过分尊重，在事情出错时过于害怕受到指责，以及对患者关注太少的问题。员工保留率很低。政客们仍然容易干预，并将资金导向最近的危机。 But community care, decentralisation and technology offer the NHS its best chance of delivering better results. That would also restore Britons’ faith in their most treasured institution. ■ 但社区护理、地方分权和技术为英国国民保健服务(nHS)提供了取得更好结果的最佳机会。这也将恢复英国人对他们最珍视的机构的信心。￭ For more expert analysis of the biggest stories in Britain, sign up to Blighty, our weekly subscriber-only newsletter. 想了解更多关于英国最大新闻的专家分析，请注册我们的每周订阅时事通讯《英国人》。 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/25/to-survive-britains-nhs-must-stop-fixating-on-hospital-care 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/25/to-survive-britains-nhs-must-stop-fixating-on-hospital-care 下载的 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Dear as chips 亲爱的 Britain’s semiconductor strategy shows the bind the country is in 英国的半导体战略显示出该国所处的困境 But a modest approach may be the sensible one 但一个温和的方法可能是明智的 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 I T WAS THE underwhelming announcement the industry had been waiting for. On May 19th the government unveiled a £1bn ($1.2bn) decade-long dollop of funding aimed at boosting Britain’s semiconductor sector. 这是业界一直在等待的平淡无奇的声明。5月19日，英国政府公布了为期10年的10亿欧元(12亿美元)注资计划，旨在促进英国半导体行业的发展。 Industrial policy is back in vogue, especially when it comes to microchips, which power everything from washing machines to missiles. 工业政策重新流行起来，尤其是在微芯片方面，从洗衣机到导弹，微芯片为一切提供动力。 Spooked by shortages during the covid-19 pandemic, and worried that so much of the industry’s capacity is in Taiwan, which China regards as a rogue province, governments have been waving some very big chequebooks. 由于在2019冠状病毒疾病疫情期间受到短缺的惊吓，加上担心该行业如此多的产能在台湾——中国大陆将台湾视为一个无赖省份——各国政府一直在挥舞着一些巨额支票簿。 America has announced $53bn of subsidies and bribes over the next five years to boost domestic firms and lure cutting-edge chipmakers from Asia. The European Union has likewise promised €43bn ($46.2bn) in handouts, in the hope of doubling its market share by 2030. Other countries have big-spending plans of their own. 美国已宣布在未来五年内提供530亿美元的补贴和贿赂，以提振国内企业并吸引亚洲的尖端芯片制造商。欧盟(EU)同样承诺提供430亿欧元(合462亿美元)的救助资金，希望到2030年将其市场份额提高一倍。其它国家也有自己的大规模支出计划。 Britain’s announcement looks anaemic by comparison. The opposition Labour party accused the government of lacking ambition, and also pointed out that the money is backloaded, with just £200m due to be spent by 2025 and the rest doled out by 2033. Simon Thomas, the boss of Paragraf, a chip firm based in Cambridge, pointed out that even the full £1bn would not be enough to fund the cost of a single modern chip factory or “fab”. 相比之下，英国的声明显得苍白无力。反对党工党(Labour party)指责政府缺乏雄心壮志，并指出，这些资金已经积压了很多，到2025年将只有2亿美元用于支出，其余部分将在2033年发放。总部位于剑桥的芯片公司 Paragraf 的老板西蒙•托马斯(Simon Thomas)指出，即使是全部10亿欧元，也不足以支撑一家现代化芯片工厂或“晶圆厂”的成本。 But Britain’s government is in a bind. Its stated aims are to build up Britain’s chip industry, mitigate the risk of supply-chain disruption and bolster national security. Yet it cannot hope to match the spending power of supersized economies like America’s and China’s. 但英国政府陷入了困境。它宣称的目标是建立英国的芯片产业，减轻供应链中断的风险，并加强国家安全。然而，它无法指望与美国和中国这样的超大型经济体的消费能力相匹敌。 The chip industry is sufficiently globalised and full of high-tech bottlenecks that it is doubtful whether even a superpower can corral an entire supply chain within its borders. 芯片行业已充分全球化，并充满高科技瓶颈，因此，即便是一个超级大国，能否将整个供应链围在自己的国境内，也值得怀疑。 In any case, while Britain has a smattering of domestic chip firms, it lacks a big manufacturer, such as Intel, Samsung or TSMC , that could be a plausible national champion. 无论如何，尽管英国国内只有少数几家芯片公司，但它缺少一家像英特尔(Intel)、三星(Samsung)或台积电(TSMC)这样的大型制造商，而这些制造商可能成为全国冠军企业。 Instead, the idea—at least in theory— is to focus on cheaper interventions. That includes more money for research and development, and a focus on companies that design chips but leave the business of manufacturing to others. 相反，他们的想法(至少在理论上是这样的)是把重点放在更廉价的干预上。这包括增加研发经费，重点关注那些设计芯片但将制造业务留给他人的公司。 One well-known example of that “fabless” strategy is Arm, a British-headquartered firm whose chip designs power the world’s smartphones. 这种“无厂房”战略的一个著名例子是总部位于英国的 ARM 公司，该公司的芯片设计为全球智能手机提供动力。 Another aim is to position the country to benefit from technologies that might become big in the future. Ministers are keen, for instance, to promote compound semiconductors. 另一个目标是使国家能够从未来可能变大的技术中受益。例如，部长们热衷于推广化合物半导体。 These make use of elements other than silicon, such as germanium or selenium, which have different electrical properties. The strategy also mentions photonics, which uses light rather than electricity to do computational work. Britain has small firms active in both areas. 它们使用硅以外的元素，如锗或硒，这些元素具有不同的电学性质。该策略还提到了光子学，它使用光而不是电来做计算工作。英国在这两个领域都有活跃的小公司。 National security is to be pursued partly via diplomacy. On May 18th, on a visit to Japan, Rishi Sunak, the prime minister, announced a new partnership to bolster supply chains. (Japan boasts several big suppliers of chip-making equipment.) 国家安全在一定程度上要通过外交手段来实现。5月18日，在访问日本期间，日本首相 Rishi Sunak 宣布建立新的合作伙伴关系以加强供应链。(日本拥有数家大型芯片制造设备供应商。) Some commentators think the government’s understated approach has merit. 一些评论人士认为，政府的低调做法有其道理。 Malcolm Penn, who runs Future Horizons, a chip-industry consultancy, points out that the worldwide wave of subsidies to the industry now amounts to around $50bn a year, or nearly half the industry’s capital expenditure. 芯片行业咨询公司 Future Horizons 的负责人马尔科姆•佩恩(Malcolm Penn)指出，目前全球对芯片行业的补贴浪潮每年达到约500亿美元，接近该行业资本支出的一半。 That wall of money, he says, risks creating a long-term glut in capacity that could eventually leave state-backed factories struggling. 他表示，巨额资金可能导致产能长期过剩，最终可能导致政府支持的工厂陷入困境。 And the British government’s fingers have been burned before. In the 1970s it funded the development of a firm called Inmos, whose chips anticipated many modern trends. That was not enough to save it. The firm was eventually swallowed up by STMicroelectronics, a Dutch firm. 英国政府的手指曾经被烧伤过。在20世纪70年代，它资助了一家名为 Inmos 的公司的发展，该公司的芯片预见了许多现代趋势。这还不足以拯救它。该公司最终被一家名为“意法半导体”的荷兰公司吞并。 Subsidies powered the building of chip factories in Scotland and Wales during the 1980s and 1990s. Most have now closed. 上世纪80年代和90年代，补贴为苏格兰和威尔士芯片工厂的建设提供了动力。 Can the government’s latest, deliberately modest strategy move the needle? Even its defenders concede it is unlikely to transform things. But, says Mr Penn, it is probably more sensible than trying to match the financial firepower of economies ten times Britain’s size. 政府最新的、刻意保守的战略能否扭转乾坤？就连它的捍卫者也承认，这不太可能改变局面。但是，佩恩先生说，这可能比试图匹配十倍于英国的经济规模的财政火力更为明智。 “Just bribing someone to build a factory: that’s probably not the best strategy,” he says. ■ 他说: “只是贿赂某人来建工厂: 这可能不是最好的策略。”￭ For more expert analysis of the biggest stories in Britain, sign up to Blighty, our weekly subscriber-only newsletter. 想了解更多关于英国最大新闻的专家分析，请注册我们的每周订阅时事通讯《英国人》。 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/25/britains-semiconductor-strategy-shows-the-bind-the-country-is-in 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/25/britains-semiconductor-strategy-shows-the-bind-the-country-is-in 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Best supporting actor 最佳男配角 With Hollywood on strike, foreign shows enjoy the limelight 随着好莱坞的罢工，外国电视节目受到关注 The strike spells disruption—and perhaps opportunity 罢工意味着破坏，也许还意味着机会 May 21st 2023 | PINEWOOD STUDIOS 2023年5月21日 | 松林制片厂 I NDIANA JONES has fought his way through jungles, snakepits and booby-trapped temples. But his latest cinematic adventure, due from Disney next month, took place just off the M 25 motorway near Slough. Pinewood Studios, where “Indiana Jones and the Dial of Destiny” was shot, is not the only place in Britain to star at the movies. 印第安纳 · 琼斯曾在丛林、蛇穴和布满陷阱的寺庙中奋战。但是他最近的一次电影冒险，将于下个月在迪斯尼公司拍摄，就发生在斯劳附近的 M25高速公路上。电影《夺宝奇兵》(Indiana Jones and the Dial of Destiny)就是在这个松林制片厂拍摄的，它并不是英国唯一一个出演电影的地方。 Barbieland, the bright-pink setting for the forthcoming “Barbie” movie, was created on the outskirts of Watford, where Warner Bros has its giant Leavesden Studios. “Andor”, the latest instalment in the Star Wars saga, was shot in locations around England and Scotland. 即将上映的电影《芭比娃娃》(Barbie)的背景是粉红色的芭比园(Barbieland) ，它位于沃特福德郊区，华纳兄弟(Warner Bros)在那里拥有自己的巨型华纳兄弟利维斯登制片厂。《安多尔》是《星球大战》系列的最新一部，在英格兰和苏格兰各地拍摄。 Britain is one of the world’s largest centres of video production. In the year to April, 1,788 new TV series were ordered by a broadcaster or streaming platform based there—more than in any country bar America and Japan, according to research by Ampere Analysis (see chart). As well as these local commissions, the bulk of which are “unscripted” (a category that includes reality TV , sport and the like), Britain hosts many high-profile—and valuable—scripted productions by American studios, which come for the talent, tax breaks and high-tech facilities. 英国是世界上最大的视频制作中心之一。安培分析(Ampere Analysis)的研究显示，在截至今年4月的一年里，有1788部新电视剧被当地的广播公司或流媒体平台订购，超过除美国和日本以外的任何国家。除了这些地方委员会(其中大部分是“非剧本”的(包括真人秀节目、体育节目等)之外，英国还有许多由美国电影公司制作的高调且有价值的剧本作品，它们是为了人才、税收优惠和高科技设施而来。 As an industrywide arms race for streaming content forced Hollywood studios to go abroad in search of production capacity, Britain was one of the winners. But its popularity with American studios means it will also be among the losers of the writers’ strike that has brought Hollywood to a halt. The Writers Guild of America ( WGA ) downed pens on May 2nd, complaining that the cut-throat economics of streaming had eroded writers’ pay and conditions. New scripts, and thus most new scripted productions, are on hold. 随着全行业对流媒体内容的军备竞赛迫使好莱坞电影公司到海外寻找制作能力，英国成为了赢家之一。但它在美国电影公司中的受欢迎程度意味着它也将成为导致好莱坞停滞的编剧罢工的失败者之一。5月2日，美国编剧工会(WGA)放弃了写作，抱怨流媒体的残酷经济侵蚀了编剧的薪水和工作条件。新的脚本，因此大多数新的脚本产品，被搁置。 Though there is no strike in Britain, filming will be disrupted. 虽然英国没有罢工，但是电影拍摄将会中断。 American-made shows shot on British soil may continue only if their scripts are finalised—a tougher condition than it sounds, as big productions typically have writers on hand to fiddle with the script during filming. 在英国本土拍摄的美国制作的电视剧只有在剧本定稿后才能继续拍摄，这比听起来要困难得多，因为大制作通常会有编剧在场，在拍摄期间对剧本进行修改。 British-made shows commissioned by American firms can go ahead if contracts are already signed and under British law. But any new deals are off-limits—at least for anyone who wants to work in Hollywood again. 如果合同已经签订并且符合英国法律，美国公司委托的英国制作的节目可以继续进行。但任何新交易都是禁区ーー至少对那些想再次在好莱坞工作的人来说是这样。 The Writers’ Guild of Great Britain warns local writers against working for American studios during the strike: “You risk being blacklisted by the WGA , which could seriously damage your long-term career.” 英国作家协会警告当地作家不要在罢工期间为美国电影公司工作: “你有可能被 WGA 列入黑名单，这会严重损害你的长期职业生涯。” Production is already drying up. Even before the strike was announced, there was “trepidation about committing to projects this year”, says David Gray, managing director of Lux Machina, a high-end visual-effects company. 生产已经枯竭。高端视觉效果公司 Lux Machina 的董事总经理戴维•格雷(David Gray)表示，甚至在宣布罢工之前，人们就“对今年承诺的项目感到不安”。 When filming wraps on titles that are already in production, like Warner’s next season of “House of the Dragon”, currently under way at Leavesden, the dearth of new projects will be felt more keenly. Mr Gray recalls that the previous WGA strike, which lasted for 100 days in 2007-08, knocked the British film business off course for a couple of years. The current strike seems nowhere near a resolution. 在拍摄已经在制作中的电影时，比如华纳正在 Leavesden 拍摄的下一季《龙之屋》(House of the Dragon) ，新项目的匮乏会让人感觉更加强烈。格雷先生回忆说，上一次 WGA 罢工在2007-08年持续了100天，使英国电影业偏离了几年的轨道。目前的罢工似乎远未达成解决方案。 But there are two ways in which British film-makers may benefit. First, production costs stand to fall. 但英国电影制作人可能从两个方面获益: 第一，制作成本将持续下降。 The content-commissioning frenzy of recent years has inflated the price of everything from studio space to hairdressing, as Apple, Amazon and Co have arrived with their Silicon Valley-size chequebooks. 随着苹果(Apple)、亚马逊(Amazon)和公司(Co)带着他们硅谷规模的支票簿来到这里，近年来的内容委托热潮推高了从工作室空间到美发的所有东西的价格。 It has been a bonanza for the people who do those jobs, but excruciating for anyone who needs their services. As demand from American studios cools, the likes of ITV and the BBC will find that their more modest budgets go further. 对于从事这些工作的人来说，这是一笔巨大的财富，但对于任何需要他们服务的人来说，却是痛苦的。随着美国电影公司的需求降温，像英国独立电视台(ITV)和英国广播公司(BBC)这样的公司将发现，它们更为有限的预算将走得更远。 Second, locally made shows will command a higher price internationally. As the strike goes on, American streamers will run out of new content, without which they may lose subscribers. 其次，本土制作的节目在国际上的价格会更高。随着罢工的继续，美国流媒体将耗尽新的内容，如果没有这些内容，他们可能会失去订户。 Though the dispute prevents British writers from working for American studios, there is no ban on the studios buying shows that are already made. 尽管这一争端阻止了英国作家为美国电影公司工作，但并没有禁止电影公司购买已经制作好的电视节目。 If the strike continues, “the acquisitions market will heat up,” says Fred Black of Ampere Analysis, who expects British firms to push their shows harder to American distributors. Hollywood’s studio chiefs might not fear the unions. 如果罢工继续下去，“收购市场将会升温,”安培分析公司的弗雷德 · 布莱克说，他预计英国公司会更加努力地向美国经销商推销他们的节目。好莱坞的制片厂老板可能不会害怕工会。 They may yet be brought to their knees by re-runs of “Midsomer Murders”. ■ 他们可能会因为“骇人命案事件簿”的重播而屈服。 ￭ For more expert analysis of the biggest stories in Britain, sign up to Blighty, our weekly subscriber-only newsletter. 想了解更多关于英国最大新闻的专家分析，请注册我们的每周订阅时事通讯《英国人》。 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/21/with-hollywood-on-strike-foreign-shows-enjoy-the-limelight 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/21/with-hollywood-on-strike-foreign-shows-enjoy-the-limelight 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Bittersweet 苦乐参半 Britain’s economy may grow by more than expected, but inflation is stickier 英国经济的增长可能超过预期，但通货膨胀更为严重 That makes the choice facing the central bank easier 这使得央行面临的选择更加容易 May 24th 2023 | 2023年5月24日 B RITAIN’S ECONOMIC outlook is a bit rosier. That at least is the verdict of the IMF , a global financial watchdog, which on May 23rd upgraded its forecast for the country. Instead of falling into a recession this year, as it was predicting in April, the IMF now believes that Britain will eke out growth. The fund attributed the improvement to a trifecta of falling wholesale energy prices, improved financial stability and warmer relations with the EU , exemplified by Rishi Sunak’s recent deal to reduce trade frictions in Northern Ireland. 英国的经济前景略为乐观。这至少是国际货币基金组织的结论，一个全球金融监督机构，在5月23日上调了对该国的预测。国际货币基金组织(IMF)现在相信，英国今年不会像4月份预测的那样陷入衰退，而是会勉强维持经济增长。IMF 将这一改善归功于批发能源价格下跌、金融稳定性改善以及与欧盟关系回暖的三重效应，例如里希•苏纳克(Rishi Sunak)最近达成的减少北爱尔兰贸易摩擦的协议。 As a result the IMF reckons that Britain no longer looks like the sick man of Europe. The fund’s growth forecast of 0.4% for 2023 is hardly the stuff of champagne and cartwheels, but it is still better than the slight contraction it predicts for Germany. 因此，国际货币基金组织认为，英国看起来不再像欧洲的病夫。该基金对2023年0.4% 的增长预测很难让人满意，但仍好于其对德国经济轻微收缩的预测。 The fund thinks that Britain will also do better than France and Italy over the next few years. Those politicians who pointed out the fallibility of the IMF when it was predicting a recession now hail the technocrats’ prophecies as evidence that pessimism about Britain is overdone. 国际货币基金组织认为，未来几年英国的表现也将好于法国和意大利。那些指出国际货币基金组织(IMF)在预测经济衰退时不可靠的政治家们，现在却把技术官僚的预言称赞为对英国悲观过头的证据。 At first glance inflation figures published on May 24th appear to be similarly encouraging for the government and its supporters. 乍一看，5月24日公布的通货膨胀数据似乎同样令政府及其支持者感到鼓舞。 For the first time since the Russian invasion of Ukraine, the pace of price growth fell into single digits: the consumer-price index was 8.7% higher in April than 12 months previously. That is a big fall compared with the 10.1% rate in March. 自从俄罗斯入侵乌克兰以来，价格增长速度首次降到个位数: 4月份的消费者价格指数比12个月前高出8.7% 。与3月份的10.1% 相比，这是一个大幅下降。 In fact, there was little—if any—good news in the figures. The drop in the headline rate largely reflected “base effects”. Energy prices jumped in April 2022 as a cap on retail prices was adjusted to reflect higher wholesale prices following the start of the war in Ukraine. 事实上，这些数字几乎没有什么好消息。总体利率的下降在很大程度上反映了“基数效应”。2022年4月，能源价格大幅上涨，原因是零售价格上限被调整，以反映乌克兰战争爆发后批发价格的上涨。 This April’s fall in the year-on-year comparison reflects a more favourable starting-point. 今年4月的同比降幅反映出一个更有利的起点。 The drop was also less than economic forecasters had expected. Economists working at banks and the like had predicted that the rate of inflation would drop even more steeply, to 8.2%. Higher food prices, increasing by 19% compared with April 2022, were partly responsible. 这一降幅也低于经济预测人士的预期。在银行等机构工作的经济学家曾预测，通货膨胀率将进一步急剧下降，降至8.2% 。与2022年4月相比，食品价格上涨了19% ，这是部分原因。 So was a jump in the price of communication: many mobile-phone and broadband suppliers update their prices for annual contracts in April. 通信价格的上涨也是如此: 许多移动电话和宽带供应商在4月份更新了年度合同的价格。 Most concerning for the Bank of England, so-called core inflation, which strips out food and energy to give a less volatile picture of underlying inflationary pressure, rose to 6.8% in April, up from 6.2% in March. 最令英格兰银行担忧的是，所谓的核心通货膨胀，即剔除食品和能源以给出潜在通货膨胀压力不那么波动的图景，从3月份的6.2% 上升到4月份的6.8% 。 That means the core-inflation rate is at its highest level since 1992. A similar rise occurred in the rate of services inflation, a decent indicator of the domestically generated inflation that the bank has the greatest power to affect. 这意味着核心通胀率处于1992年以来的最高水平。服务业通货膨胀率也出现了类似的上升，这是银行最有能力影响的国内通货膨胀的良好指标。 Investors promptly raised their bets on an increase in interest rates when the bank holds its next meeting in June. Markets also predicted that the main policy rate will peak at 5.25% in November, compared with a peak of 4.5% before the latest data were released. 在美联储6月份召开下一次会议时，投资者迅速加大了对加息的押注。市场还预测，主要政策利率将在11月达到5.25% 的峰值，而最新数据公布前的峰值为4.5% 。 The yield on government borrowing jumped to levels just below those seen in the chaotic aftermath of the disastrous mini-budget in September. 政府借款收益率跃升至略低于9月份灾难性的小型预算案造成的混乱后果的水平。 The combination of better-than-expected growth prospects and worse-than-forecast inflation does make the bank’s decisions much easier: the economy both needs and can cope with higher interest rates. 高于预期的增长前景和低于预期的通胀确实让央行的决策变得容易得多: 经济既需要也能够应对更高的利率。 But rising mortgage costs and stubbornly high inflation mean that it will be a while before Britons share the IMF ’s sunnier view of the country’s prospects. ■ 但不断上涨的抵押贷款成本和居高不下的通胀率意味着，英国人要想认同 IMF 对英国前景的乐观看法，还需要一段时间。￭ For more expert analysis of the biggest stories in Britain, sign up to Blighty, our weekly subscriber-only newsletter. 想了解更多关于英国最大新闻的专家分析，请注册我们的每周订阅时事通讯《英国人》。 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/24/britains-economy-may-grow-by-more-than-expected-but-inflation-is-stickier 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/24/britains-economy-may-grow-by-more-than-expected-but-inflation-is-stickier 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Bagehot 白芝浩 British voters want more immigrants but less immigration 英国选民希望有更多的移民，而不是更少的移民 The government’s immigration policy is incoherent because voters are incoherent 政府的移民政策是不连贯的，因为选民是不连贯的 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 T HE BIGGEST lie in British politics is that voters want honest debate. Whenever a policy problem emerges, sensible types call for the trade-offs to be laid out before an informed voting public who will carefully weigh the options. 英国政治中最大的谎言就是选民想要诚实的辩论。无论何时出现政策问题，明智的人都会要求在知情的投票公众面前进行权衡，让他们仔细权衡各种选择。 Anyone who has sat through a focus group or gone canvassing with a politician knows this is nonsense. When faced with an either/or question, British voters usually give a decisive answer: “yes”. 任何经历过焦点小组讨论或与政治家一起游说的人都知道，这是无稽之谈。当面对非此即彼的问题时，英国选民通常会给出一个决定性的答案: “是的”。 Nowhere is this more true than immigration. A majority of voters think migration is too high, according to most polls. Almost nine out of ten Conservative voters think this; a plurality of Labour voters agree. 没有什么地方比移民更能说明这一点了。大多数民意调查显示，大多数选民认为移民太多了。几乎十分之九的保守党选民这样认为; 大多数工党选民同意这一点。 At the same time, British voters say they want more nurses, doctors and fruit-pickers. Carers, academics, computer whizzes and students are welcome, too. 与此同时，英国选民表示，他们希望有更多的护士、医生和水果采摘者。护理人员、学者、计算机专家和学生也受到欢迎。 Big-hearted Britons thought the country was completely right to let swathes of refugees from Ukraine and Hong Kong into the country. Britons may not much like immigration, but they are keen on immigrants. 心胸宽广的英国人认为，英国允许大批来自乌克兰和香港的难民进入英国是完全正确的。英国人可能不太喜欢移民，但他们热衷于移民。 If so, then the Tories have come up with an impeccably botched policy response. A Conservative government that has pledged to cut immigration at the past four elections has instead overseen an increase to a record level. 如果是这样，那么保守党已经提出了一个无可挑剔的拙劣政策回应。保守党政府曾在过去四次选举中承诺减少移民，但现在却将移民人数增加到了创纪录的水平。 Net migration hit 606,000 in Britain last year, according to figures published on May 25th, as people took advantage of a more liberal post-Brexit immigration regime. 根据5月25日公布的数据，去年英国的净移民达到60.6万人，这是因为人们利用了英国退欧后更为自由的移民制度。 The British government has thrown open the country’s doors while complaining about the people who walk through them. It is utterly incoherent. But when it comes to immigration, so are voters. 英国政府打开了国门，同时抱怨走进来的人们。完全语无伦次。但在移民问题上，选民也是如此。 Public opinion on immigration was not always so confused. Attitudes used to move in lockstep with numbers. In the 1940s and 1950s Britain accepted workers from across the Commonwealth, who could enter the country as they pleased. 公众对移民的看法并不总是那么混乱。过去人们的态度总是与数字一致。在1940年代和1950年代，英国接受来自英联邦各地的工人，他们可以随心所欲地进入英国。 By the 1960s eight out of ten people wanted lower immigration; hard-nosed and rather racist legislation followed. Likewise, when immigration increased during the 1990s and 2000s, so did concern. 到20世纪60年代，80% 的人希望降低移民比例; 紧随其后的是精明而且带有种族主义色彩的立法。同样，在上世纪90年代和本世纪头十年，当移民增加时，人们的担忧也随之增加。 This trend reached its apex in 2016, when, with just a month to go until the Brexit referendum, the government announced a then-record net influx of 330,000 people. Britain voted to leave the EU , with immigration cited as one of the main reasons. 这一趋势在2016年达到顶峰，当时距离英国退出欧盟公投只有一个月的时间，政府宣布当时创纪录的净流入人数达到33万人。英国投票决定脱离欧盟，移民是主要原因之一。 This tidy relationship has broken down. Immigration has increased sharply since the Brexit vote but concern about it has, if anything, gone down in the past decade. 这段亲密的关系已经破裂了。自英国退欧公投以来，移民人数大幅增加，但过去10年，人们对移民问题的担忧(如果有的话)有所下降。 In 2012 a quarter of voters thought immigrants boosted Britain’s economy; half thought immigrants harmed it, according to British Future, a think-tank. Now those proportions have reversed. 根据智库 British Future 的调查，2012年，四分之一的选民认为移民推动了英国经济的发展; 一半的选民认为移民损害了英国经济。现在这些比例反过来了。 The number of people who cite immigration as the number-one problem facing the country has plunged, while issues such as lousy health care and high inflation top the worry-list. 将移民问题列为国家面临的头号问题的人数已大幅下降，而糟糕的医疗保健和高通胀等问题则是最令人担忧的问题。 Attacking immigration was once an easy win for politicians. In 2015 almost 70% of voters wanted immigration reduced. Now, only 42% do. At the same time, a hard-core minority of people now want migration to increase. In 2015 only one in ten wanted this. Now about a quarter do. 攻击移民曾经是政客们轻而易举的胜利。2015年，近70% 的选民希望减少移民。现在，只有42% 的人这么认为。与此同时，少数核心人群希望移民增加。2015年，只有十分之一的人希望这样。现在大约四分之一。 James Dennison and Alexander Kustov, a pair of academics, label this phenomenon a “reverse backlash”. Politicians have tried to placate voters tempted by anti-immigrant populist parties and ignored others in the process. 两位学者詹姆斯•丹尼森(James Dennison)和亚历山大•库斯托夫(Alexander Kustov)将这种现象称为“逆向反弹”。政客们试图安抚那些受到反移民民粹主义政党诱惑的选民，但在这个过程中却忽视了其他人。 Once-silent liberal voters have started demanding to be heard. 一度沉默的自由派选民开始要求被倾听。 (Intriguingly, about half of people think the British public has become less tolerant overall, even though most polling points to the opposite; when discussing immigration, Britons think in irregular verbs: “I am tolerant; you are prejudiced; he is a complete bigot.”) (有趣的是，大约一半的人认为英国公众总体上变得不那么宽容了，尽管大多数民意调查的结果恰恰相反; 在讨论移民问题时，英国人用不规则动词思考: “我宽容; 你有偏见; 他是个彻头彻尾的偏执狂。”) Conservatives are split on how to deal with this change. For some, the increasingly liberal views of British voters when it comes to immigration should be seized on. 保守派在如何应对这一变化上存在分歧。对一些人来说，应该抓住英国选民在移民问题上日益自由化的观点。 Dominic Cummings, the architect of the Vote Leave campaign in 2016, argued that voters would be happy with high levels of immigration as long as it was controlled. Judging by the positive shift in attitudes on immigrants, he was right. 多米尼克•卡明斯(Dominic Cummings)是2016年退欧运动的设计师，他认为，只要移民受到控制，选民就会对高水平的移民感到满意。从对移民态度的积极转变来看，他是对的。 If the government can stop people crossing the English Channel in small boats (some 45,000 arrived last year in this manner) voters will not care about the larger numbers of migrants arriving through official channels. There are few benefits of Brexit. 如果政府能够阻止人们乘坐小船穿越英吉利海峡(去年约有4.5万人以这种方式抵达英吉利海峡) ，选民们就不会关心更多通过官方渠道抵达的移民。Brexit 几乎没有什么好处。 But Britain’s immigration policy could be one. 但英国的移民政策可能就是其中之一。 For other Conservative advisers—including those currently in Downing Street—immigration simply must come down if the government is to have any chance of surviving. In their view, the liberal turn is a mirage. 对于其他保守党顾问(包括目前在唐宁街工作的顾问)来说，如果政府想要生存下去，移民就必须减少。在他们看来，转向自由主义只是海市蜃楼。 When voters eventually notice that immigration has, in fact, hit an all-time high they will be furious. People have mistaken a drop in salience with an increase in liberalism. 当选民最终注意到移民事实上已经达到了历史最高点时，他们会非常愤怒。人们把显著性的下降与自由主义的抬头搞错了。 This hypothesis is about to be tested in real life: if voters want control rather than reductions, what if more than half a million arrive every year? Rishi Sunak, the prime minister, thinks he knows the answer to that question, and has pledged to reduce the numbers. 这一假设即将在现实生活中得到验证: 如果选民希望控制而不是削减，那么如果每年有50多万人来投票呢？印度总理 Rishi Sunak 认为他知道这个问题的答案，并承诺减少人数。 Welcome. Now get out 欢迎，出去吧 Taking numbers down a little is easy. Unless another war breaks out in Europe, there will be fewer refugees next year. Bringing them down a lot is harder. If the British government wants fewer people to come, it can change the law and suffer the consequences. 把数字降低一点很容易。除非欧洲爆发另一场战争，否则明年的难民人数将会减少。把他们拉下马就更难了。如果英国政府希望来的人更少，它可以修改法律，并承担后果。 Suella Braverman, the home secretary, has already tightened rules on the number of international students who can bring dependents, even though voters are broadly comfortable with people coming to Britain to study and universities rely on their fees. 英国内政大臣苏埃拉•布雷弗曼(Suella Braverman)已经收紧了对可以带家属来英国留学的国际学生人数的限制，尽管选民普遍对来英国学习的人感到满意，而且大学依赖他们的学费。 The government could crack down on fruit-pickers, but farmers in Lincolnshire would scream. Few voters would thank a government that turns away nurses. Cutting immigration comes at a cost that voters show no willingness to pay. 政府可以打击水果采摘者，但林肯郡的农民会尖叫。很少有选民会感谢一个拒绝护士的政府。削减移民的代价是选民不愿意付钱。 Rolling out the welcome mat and then shouting at anyone who wipes their feet on it may be an imperfect approach. But from the government’s point of view, it will have to do. Voters do not want to live with the consequences of their opinions. 铺开欢迎垫，然后对任何在上面擦脚的人大喊大叫，这可能是一种不完美的做法。但从政府的角度来看，它必须这么做。选民们不想承担他们的观点所带来的后果。 When voters are hypocrites, politicians must be too. ■ 当选民是伪君子时，政治家也一定是伪君子。 ￭ Read more from Bagehot, our columnist on British politics: 阅读更多来自我们的英国政治专栏作家白芝浩的文章: Truss Tour: 2023 (May 17th) 桁架之旅: 2023(5月17日) How housing became the new divide in British politics (May 11th) 住房是如何成为英国政治中新的分水岭的(5月11日) The Tories v the institutions (May 4th) 托利党诉制度(5月4日) Also: How the Bagehot column got its name 还有: 白芝浩专栏是如何得名的 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/25/british-voters-want-more-immigrants-but-less-immigration 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/britain/2023/05/25/british-voters-want-more-immigrants-but-less-immigration 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Britain 英国 Sections 部分 Business 生意 International 国际的 The cost of the global arms race 全球军备竞赛的代价 Farewell, peace dividend :: What a “war tax” means for the global economy 永别了，和平红利: “战争税”对全球经济意味着什么 Britain 英国 Sections 部分 Business 生意

Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Farewell, peace dividend 永别了，和平红利 The cost of the global arms race 全球军备竞赛的代价 What a “war tax” means for the global economy “战争税”对全球经济意味着什么 May 23rd 2023 | BRUSSELS and SAN FRANCISCO 2023年5月23日 | 布鲁塞尔和旧金山 A T THE END of the cold war America’s president, George H.W. Bush, popularised the idea that cutting defence spending would boost the economy. “We can reap a genuine peace dividend this year and then year after year, in the form of permanently reduced defence budgets,” he declared in 1992. The world took note. 冷战结束乔治·赫伯特·沃克·布什美国总统普遍认为削减国防开支可以促进经济发展。他在1992年宣称: “我们可以在今年和年复一年地收获真正的和平红利，其形式是永久性削减国防预算。”。全世界都注意到了。 America went from shelling out 6% of its GDP on defence in 1989 to roughly 3% in ten years (see chart 1). Then came the 9/11 attacks and conflicts in Afghanistan and Iraq. 1989年，美国国防开支占 GDP 的6% ，10年间，这一比例下降到3% 左右(见图表1)。然后是911袭击和阿富汗和伊拉克的冲突。 Now with Russia’s invasion of Ukraine, talk of war between America and China over Taiwan, and tensions concerning Iran’s nuclear ambitions, countries are tooling up as never before in this century. 现在，随着俄罗斯入侵乌克兰，美国和中国之间关于台湾战争的讨论，以及伊朗核野心引发的紧张局势，各国正在本世纪前所未有地装备起来。 Last year defence spending worldwide increased by nearly 4% in real terms to over $2trn, according to the Stockholm International Peace Research Institute, a think-tank (see chart 2). 根据智囊团斯德哥尔摩国际和平研究所，去年全球国防开支实际增长了近4% ，超过2万亿美元(见表2)。 The share prices of defence firms are performing better than the overall stockmarket (see chart 3). Many NATO allies, notably Germany, plan to meet or exceed spending of 2% of GDP on defence—the alliance’s target. Other countries are planning to splurge, too. Japan plans to raise defence outlays by two-thirds through to 2027, turning it into the world’s third-largest spender. 国防企业的股票价格表现好于整个股票市场(见表3)。许多北约盟国，尤其是德国，计划达到或超过国防支出占 GDP 的2% ，这是北约的目标。其它国家也在计划挥霍。日本计划到2027年将国防开支提高三分之二，使其成为世界第三大军费开支国。 We estimate that total new defence commitments and forecast spending increases, if implemented, will generate over $200bn in extra defence spending globally each year. It could be a lot more. Imagine that countries which currently spend less than 2% of GDP per year meet that level and that the remainder increase spending by half a percentage point of GDP . Global defence outlays would rise by close to $700bn a year. 我们估计，新的国防承诺总额和预测支出增长如果得到落实，每年将在全球产生超过2000亿美元的额外国防支出。可能还有更多。想象一下，如果一个国家目前每年的支出不到 GDP 的2% ，就可以达到这个水平，其余国家的支出可以增加 GDP 的0.5个百分点。全球国防支出每年将增加近7000亿美元。 Russia’s invasion of Ukraine “risks wiping out the peace dividend we have enjoyed for the past three decades”, declared Kristalina Georgieva, the head of the IMF , in a speech in April. The West is sending ever more weapons, of ever growing sophistication, in order to help Ukraine launch a counter-offensive against Russia. It has equipped the best part of nine new armoured brigades with modern battle tanks and more besides. 国际货币基金组织(IMF)总裁克莉丝汀娜·乔吉瓦在今年4月的一次讲话中表示，俄罗斯入侵乌克兰“有可能抹去我们过去30年享有的和平红利”。为了帮助乌克兰对俄罗斯发动反攻，西方国家正在向乌克兰输送越来越多越来越先进的武器。它为九个新装甲旅最好的部分配备了现代化的战斗坦克，此外还有更多的装备。 Soon it will start training Ukrainian pilots to fly American-made F-16 fighter jets. 不久将开始训练乌克兰飞行员驾驶美国制造的 F-16战斗机。 The number of NATO countries hitting the 2% target rose from three in 2014 to seven last year. The club now says this should be “a floor, not a ceiling”, a notion that is likely to be enshrined at its summit in Lithuania in July. Some countries are moving well beyond. 达到2% 目标的北约国家从2014年的3个增加到去年的7个。俱乐部现在表示，这应该是“一个底线，而不是一个天花板”，这个概念很可能会在7月的立陶宛峰会上得到体现。一些国家正在远远超越。 Poland aims to reach 4% this year, and eventually to double the size of its army. France speaks of shifting to a “war economy”. 波兰的目标是今年达到4% ，并最终将其军队规模扩大一倍。法国谈到转向“战争经济”。 An arms race is intensifying on the other side of the globe, too. Taiwan is extending military service from four months to a year. Under the AUKUS deal, America and Britain will help supply Australia with nuclear-powered submarines; they will also aim to develop other weapons, including hypersonic missiles. In the past decade India’s defence budget has grown by about 50% in real terms, as has Pakistan’s. 在地球的另一端，军备竞赛也在加剧。台湾正将服役期从四个月延长至一年。根据 AUKUS 协议，美国和英国将帮助向澳大利亚提供核动力潜艇; 他们还将致力于发展其他武器，包括高超音速导弹。在过去的十年中，印度的国防预算实际增长了50% ，巴基斯坦也是如此。 In the Middle East, Gulf states are again shopping heavily in the arms bazaar. 在中东，海湾国家再次在军火市场大举采购。 China’s defence budget has grown by about 75% in real terms in the past ten years. It wants to “basically complete modernisation” of its forces by 2035, and become a “world class” military power by 2049. America thinks China wants the capability to invade Taiwan as early as 2027. 在过去十年中，中国的国防预算实际增长了约75% 。希望到2035年“基本实现军队现代化”，到2049年成为“世界级”军事强国。美国认为中国希望最早在2027年拥有入侵台湾的能力。 Some in America question whether its approach is adequate in a world riven by rivalry. Despite some recent increases, America’s defence budget has shrunk by about 5% since 2012. Squeezes on spending emerged in the aftermath of the financial crisis of 2007-09. Yet even before the acute tension of current times, Congress gave authority to a commission to consider American defence spending. 一些美国人质疑，在一个竞争四分五裂的世界里，这种做法是否足够。尽管最近出现了一些增长，美国的国防预算自2012年以来已经缩减了大约5% 。2007年至2009年金融危机之后，政府开支出现了紧缩。然而，甚至在当前的紧张局势出现之前，国会就授权一个委员会考虑美国的国防开支。 In 2018 the body recommended raising it by 3% to 5% in real terms every year for at least five years. Overall America’s advantage over its rivals has eroded in the past century, reckons Andrew Krepinevich, an American strategist. 2018年，该机构建议至少连续五年每年实际增长3% 至5% 。美国战略家安德鲁 · 克雷皮尼维奇(AndrewKrepinevich)认为，在过去的一个世纪里，美国相对于竞争对手的总体优势已经被侵蚀。 During the first, second and cold wars America’s adversaries had much smaller economies than America did. No longer. Today China’s GDP alone is nearly 80% of America’s. 在第一次、第二次和冷战期间，美国的对手经济规模远小于美国。不再是了。今天，仅中国的 GDP 就接近美国的80% 。 In the decades after the cold war, the thinking was that to spend less on armies meant to spend more on infrastructure and public services and to lower debt or taxes. 在冷战结束后的几十年里，人们的想法是，减少军费开支意味着增加基础设施和公共服务开支，并降低债务或税收。 Since the 1960s the world has “released” about $4trn a year of spending at current prices in this way, equivalent to the global government budget for education. Now the peace dividend is turning into a “war tax”. How heavy will it be? 自上世纪60年代以来，世界每年以这种方式“释放”的支出约为4万亿美元，相当于全球政府的教育预算。现在，和平红利正在变成一种“战争税”。它会有多重？ Working out accurately who is spending what can be difficult. For international comparison, defence spending is usually reckoned as a share of GDP , at market exchange rates. By this measure global military expenditures appear close to a post-cold-war low, at around 2.5%. 准确地计算出谁在花什么可能是困难的。作为国际比较，按照市场汇率，国防开支通常被认为是 GDP 的一部分。按照这个标准衡量，全球军费开支似乎接近冷战后的最低水平，约为2.5% 。 But market exchange rates greatly underestimate the true size of defence establishments in countries such as China and Russia, where a given dollar of military spending can pay for a lot more weapons and soldiers. 但市场汇率大大低估了中国和俄罗斯等国国防设施的真实规模，在这些国家，给定一美元的军费开支可以支付更多的武器和士兵。 The proportion will also increase in the coming years if great-power rivalry grows as expected. In a more insecure world, countries will arm themselves because their neighbours are doing so or because their allies encourage them to. 如果大国之间的竞争像预期的那样增长，这一比例在未来几年也将上升。在一个更不安全的世界里，各国将武装自己，因为它们的邻国正在这样做，或者因为它们的盟友鼓励它们这样做。 Splurging more on weapons raises several questions. What will countries buy, might money be squandered and could the global economy be damaged? 在武器上挥霍更多引发了几个问题。各国将购买什么，是否会浪费资金，是否会损害全球经济？ America, by far the world’s largest defence spender, is devoting growing sums to research and development of future weapons. 迄今为止，美国是世界上最大的国防开支国，正在投入越来越多的资金用于未来武器的研究和开发。 This includes hypersonic missiles, to catch up with China and Russia; “directed energy” such as powerful lasers to shoot down drones and missiles; and artificial intelligence and robotics. 这包括用于追赶中国和俄罗斯的高超音速导弹; 用于击落无人机和导弹的强大激光等“定向能量”; 以及人工智能和机器人技术。 It is also buying as many munitions as its factories can produce—from 155mm artillery shells to anti-ship missiles. 它还在尽可能多地购买其工厂能够生产的军需品，从155毫米炮弹到反舰导弹。 The war in Ukraine has exposed the extraordinary quantities of munitions needed in a conflict, as well as the inability of peacetime production lines to meet such demand. 乌克兰境内的战争暴露了冲突中需要的大量弹药，以及和平时期的生产线无法满足这种需求。 China is investing on all fronts and expenditures grew by 4.2% in real terms last year. The breakdown of its budget is opaque, not least because of “civil-military fusion” in technological development. It has developed a thicket of anti-access/area denial ( A2/AD ) weapons, with ground-attack and anti-ship missiles able to reach deep into the Pacific. It also has a lead in some types of hypersonic missiles (which are harder to intercept than ballistic ones). Its navy is already larger than America’s. 中国正在全方位投资，去年实际支出增长了4.2% 。其预算的分解是不透明的，尤其是因为技术开发中的“军民融合”。它已经开发了一系列的反介入/区域拒绝(A2/AD)武器，其中包括能够深入太平洋的地面攻击和反舰导弹。它在某些类型的高超音速导弹(比弹道导弹更难拦截)方面也处于领先地位。它的海军规模已经超过了美国。 America, Russia and China are investing in their nuclear arsenals, too. America is upgrading all legs of its “triad” of ground-, air- and submarine-launched nukes. 美国、俄罗斯和中国也在投资建设自己的核武库。美国正在升级其地面、空中和潜艇发射核武器“三合一”的所有部件。 Russia is working on esoteric weapons, such as the long-distance, nuclear-powered Poseidon torpedo designed to set off an underwater nuclear explosion that, propagandists boast, can cause destructive tidal waves. 俄罗斯正在研究一些深奥的武器，比如远程核动力的波塞冬鱼雷，该鱼雷旨在引发水下核爆炸，宣传人员夸口说，这种爆炸可以引发毁灭性的海啸。 China is quickly expanding its arsenal, from several hundred warheads to 1,500 by 2035, according to the Pentagon. 五角大楼表示，中国正在迅速扩大其核武库，到2035年将核弹头数量从几百枚增加到1500枚。 Equipment is also at the top of the shopping list for many smaller countries. Germany is buying stealthy new F-35 jets as well as command-and-control systems. Poland is spending heavily on land forces—buying tanks, howitzers, precision rockets and more from America and South Korea—as well as combat aircraft. 设备也是许多较小国家的首选购物清单。德国正在购买隐形的新型 F-35战斗机以及指挥控制系统。波兰在陆军和战斗机方面投入巨资，包括从美国和韩国购买坦克、榴弹炮、精确火箭等。 Japan is seeking, among many other things, long-range “counter-strike” missiles to hit back at China and North Korea. 日本正在寻求，在许多其他事情中，远程“反打击”导弹来反击中国和朝鲜。 This shopping spree comes with several risks. 这种疯狂购物有几个风险。 Given the bottlenecks, one danger is escalating costs, in an industry where prices can be hard to control because of long development processes, changing requirements and because defence firms operate at the technological edge, with all the associated overheads. 考虑到这些瓶颈，一个危险是成本不断上升。在这个行业，由于开发过程漫长、需求不断变化，以及防务公司处于技术边缘，需要支付所有相关的管理费用，价格可能难以控制。 American defence budgets can be subject to the whims of politicians seeking benefits for their districts. Congress has persistently refused to let the air force retire obsolescent aircraft, for instance. 美国的国防预算可能会受到政客们一时兴起的心血来潮的影响，这些政客们希望为自己的选区谋取利益。例如，国会一直拒绝让空军退役那些过时的飞机。 European countries, for their part, are poor at co-ordinating procurement at scale. McKinsey, a consultancy, notes that they operate many more models of weapons than America does: 15 types of main battle tanks compared with one in America; 20 fighter jets versus seven, and so on. 就欧洲国家而言，它们在大规模采购方面缺乏协调。咨询公司麦肯锡(McKinsey)指出，他们操作的武器型号比美国多得多: 15种主战坦克，而美国只有一种; 20架战斗机，而美国只有7架，以此类推。 Worse, the defence industry is especially prone to graft, notes Josie Stewart of Transparency International, an anti-corruption group. 反腐败组织透明国际(Transparency International)的乔茜•斯图尔特(Josie Stewart)指出，更糟糕的是，国防工业尤其容易受贿。 That is because of the secrecy that surrounds many of its dealings, its importance to national security and the “revolving door” of experts versed in its technicalities. An onrush of money could make everything worse. 这是因为其许多交易的保密性、对国家安全的重要性以及精通其技术细节的专家的“旋转门”。大量资金的涌入可能会让一切变得更糟。 There are wider concerns that big defence establishments, and the industries supplying them, will weigh down the global economy by stoking inflation or slowing growth, or both. 人们更广泛地担心，大型国防机构及其供应行业将加剧通胀或放缓增长，或两者兼而有之，从而拖累全球经济。 Kenneth Rogoff of Harvard has noted that “the need to front-load massive temporary expenditures can easily push up borrowing costs”. 哈佛大学(Harvard)的肯尼思•罗格夫(Kenneth Rogoff)指出，“预先加载大量临时支出的需要，很容易推高借贷成本”。 Expectations for inflation? 通货膨胀预期？ Certain fears may be misplaced. Take defence inflation in America—the increase in prices facing buyers of military equipment. It is running at about 5% year on year, the highest rate in decades. 某些恐惧可能是多余的。以美国的国防通胀为例ーー军事装备购买者面临的价格上涨。同比增长约5% ，为几十年来最高水平。 During previous military build-ups this sort of defence inflation has sharply increased. In the early 1980s, a period in which Ronald Reagan built up America’s military capabilities, it easily outpaced economy-wide price rises. 在以往的军事集结中，这种国防通货膨胀急剧上升。上世纪80年代初，罗纳德•里根(Ronald Reagan)在那个时期建立了美国的军事实力，其增长速度轻松超过了整个经济体的物价上涨速度。 In the Vietnam war it briefly hit 48% on an annual basis. 在越南战争期间，这一比例曾短暂地达到每年48% 。 Even so, there is little reason to believe that the new cold war will be sharply inflationary. Not even the fiercest hawks are calling for defence spending, as a share of GDP , to return to the levels of the 1960s or 1970s. Barring a hot war between big powers, global defence spending is unlikely to grow above the low single digits of global GDP —meaning that its impact on global aggregate demand, and thus inflation, will be similarly small. 即便如此，几乎没有理由相信，新冷战将导致大幅通胀。即使是最强硬的鹰派人士也没有呼吁国防支出占 GDP 的比例恢复到上世纪60年代或70年代的水平。除非大国之间爆发一场热战，否则全球国防支出的增长不太可能超过全球国内生产总值(gdp)的个位数——这意味着，国防支出对全球总需求的影响，以及对通胀的影响，都将同样微不足道。 Spending can remain historically low, in large part, because defence is more efficient than it used to be. Modern armies require ever fewer people—allowing military planners to cut headcounts (though service members can become more expensive). 在很大程度上，国防支出可能保持在历史低位，因为它比以前更有效率。现代军队需要的人越来越少ーー允许军事规划者削减人数(尽管服役人员可能会变得更加昂贵)。 Brazil spends 78% of its budget on people compared with less than 50% in the West. In place of people you have better machines. Many planners bemoan the growing cost of each platform, but they improve with each iteration. 巴西78% 的预算花在人身上，而西方只有不到50% 。取代人类的是更好的机器。许多规划者抱怨每个平台的成本不断增长，但是他们每次迭代都在改进。 “These days you can hit dozens of targets with a single bomber, rather than vice versa, which used to be the case,” argues James Geurts, a retired air-force colonel who advises Lux Capital, a venture-capital firm. “如今，你可以用一架轰炸机打击几十个目标，而不是像过去那样，反之亦然,”为风险投资公司 Lux Capital 提供咨询的退休空军上校詹姆斯 · 格尔茨(James Geurts)说。 Official data from America suggest that once you adjust for improvements in quality, the price of a missile has fallen in nominal terms by about 30% since the late 1970s. The price of military aircraft is about flat. 来自美国的官方数据显示，一旦根据质量改进进行调整，导弹的名义价格自上世纪70年代末以来已经下降了约30% 。军用飞机的价格基本持平。 Today a country can spend relatively modestly to acquire fearsome capabilities. As such, defence spending tends to fall relative to GDP , particularly in peacetime. 如今，一个国家可以通过相对适度的支出来获得令人生畏的能力。因此，国防支出相对于 GDP 往往会下降，特别是在和平时期。 Defence could continue to get better and cheaper in relative terms because of the changing nature of what Dwight Eisenhower, a general-turned-president, called the “military-industrial complex”. 相对而言，国防可能会继续变得更好、更便宜，因为德怀特·艾森豪威尔将军出身的美国总统所称的“军事工业复合体”的性质正在发生变化。 In the past defence departments exported technology to civilians—think of the global-positioning system and the internet. Increasingly, the opposite is happening, with military industries importing technology from outside. 过去，国防部门向平民出口技术ーー想想全球定位系统和互联网吧。随着军事工业从外部引进技术，情况正在日益相反。 Technological trajectories 技术轨迹 Cyber-security, drones and satellite technology straddle both the civilian and military worlds. SpaceX, founded by Elon Musk, has launched American military satellites. Ukrainian warriors make extensive use of his Starlink constellation of satellites. 网络安全、无人机和卫星技术跨越了民用和军事领域。由埃隆 · 马斯克创立的 SpaceX 公司已经发射了美国的军事卫星。乌克兰战士广泛使用他的星链星座卫星。 America’s defence department has identified 14 critical technologies deemed vital to national security. Perhaps ten or 11 of these areas are being led commercially. Tech firms such as Google and Microsoft help out with cyber-security, data-processing and artificial intelligence. 美国国防部确认了14项被认为对国家安全至关重要的关键技术。也许其中的10或11个领域正在商业领先。谷歌和微软等科技公司在网络安全、数据处理和人工智能方面提供帮助。 Several firms provide cloud computing. 有几家公司提供云计算。 It all amounts to a change of cultural mindset from tech firms that once shunned defence as morally tainted. A defence-tech ecosystem has sprung up in America, with lots of engineering expertise in Colorado; regulation wonks in Washington, DC ; aerospace types in Los Angeles; and investors in San Francisco. But it is not just an American phenomenon. About half of the biggest defence-and-aerospace firms founded within the past decade are headquartered in other parts of the world. 这一切都意味着科技公司文化观念的改变，这些公司曾经因为道德败坏而回避国防。国防科技生态系统已经在美国兴起，科罗拉多州拥有大量的工程专家，华盛顿特区的监管专家，洛杉矶的航空航天类型，以及旧金山的投资者。但这不仅仅是美国的现象。在过去十年中成立的最大的国防和航空航天公司中，大约有一半的总部设在世界的其他地方。 “Founders no longer want to do the next social-media startup,” says Paul Kwan of General Catalyst, an American investment firm. 美国投资公司 General Catalyst 的保罗•关(Paul Kwan)表示: “创始人不再想创建下一个社交媒体初创企业。”。 Big investors in Silicon Valley, including General Catalyst and the venture-capital outfit Andreessen Horowitz, are increasingly interested in national security, broadly defined. Tech firms smell opportunity. 硅谷的大投资者，包括 General Catalyst 和风险投资机构安德森·霍罗威茨，对广义的国家安全越来越感兴趣。科技公司嗅到了机会。 Palantir, which specialises in big-data analytics, recently released a new defence platform powered by AI to speed up decision-making. Defence was one of the few sectors where there were more venture-capital deals in 2022 than in 2021. 专注于大数据分析的 Palantir 最近发布了一个由人工智能驱动的新防御平台，以加快决策速度。国防是2022年风险投资交易比2021年多的少数几个行业之一。 There is also a growing effort to inject defence firms themselves with tech’s dynamism. A recent parliamentary report in Britain noted that “old ‘legacy’ systems complicate tasks as routine as ordering a pair of boots”. It can take ten to 20 years to produce a new plane. 还有一个越来越大的努力是为国防企业自身注入科技的活力。英国最近的一份议会报告指出，“旧的‘遗留’系统使日常工作复杂化，就像订购一双靴子一样”。制造一架新飞机可能需要10到20年的时间。 But rather than develop new planes every decade or so, says Jim Taiclet, the boss of Lockheed Martin, the world’s largest defence contractor, his firm aims to mimic Silicon Valley by offering software upgrades to improve performance every six to 12 months. 世界最大的国防承包商洛克希德 · 马丁公司的老板吉姆 · 泰克莱特(Jim Taiclet)说，与其每十年左右开发一架新飞机，他的公司旨在模仿硅谷，每六到十二个月提供软件升级以改善性能。 The fiscal consequences of the new defence boom may be modest if the industry becomes more efficient. The defence-versus-everything-else trade-off was acute in decades past: in 1944 America spent 53% of its GDP on military forces. But it is less so today. If the world doubled its military spending overnight (assuming no increases in taxes or debt) public spending would need to be cut by about 5% to balance the books. Not easy, but not that hard. 如果该行业变得更有效率，新一轮国防繁荣的财政后果可能不会太严重。在过去的几十年里，国防与其他一切的权衡是非常尖锐的: 1944年，美国将其 GDP 的53% 花在了军事力量上。但今天就不那么如此了。如果世界军费开支一夜之间翻了一番(假设税收和债务没有增加) ，公共开支需要削减5% 左右才能平衡收支。不容易，但也没那么难。 What of the impact on growth? Many historians argue that defence spending is a drain on the rest of the economy. Keeping a country secure has great economic value. But once you buy a missile, say, it tends to sit in storage rather than being put to productive use. 这对经济增长有何影响？许多历史学家认为，国防支出是对其他经济领域的消耗。维护国家安全具有重大的经济价值。但是，一旦你购买了一枚导弹，比如说，它往往会被储存起来，而不是投入生产性使用。 During the second world war productivity growth slowed in America, as people were pulled from the fields into munitions factories and military units. 第二次世界大战期间，美国的生产力增长放缓，因为人们被迫离开农田，进入军火工厂和军事单位。 Forced limits on military spending in postwar Japan and West Germany, by contrast, coincided with huge productivity improvements in both countries. 相比之下，战后日本和西德被迫限制军费开支的同时，两国的生产力都有了巨大提高。 This is only a partial story, however. Countries such as Israel and South Korea combine vibrant economies with big defence sectors. We analysed World Bank data from the 1960s to 2021, exploring the relationship between military spending and GDP growth. Both within a single country over many years, and between countries in a single year, we find practically no correlation between the two. Put simply, more guns need not mean less butter. 然而，这只是部分故事。以色列和韩国等国将充满活力的经济与庞大的国防部门结合在一起。我们分析了世界银行20世纪60年代至2021年的数据，探讨了军费开支与 GDP 增长之间的关系。无论是在一个国家内多年，还是在国家之间一年，我们发现两者之间实际上没有相关性。简而言之，更多的枪并不意味着更少的黄油。 More defence-related R&D could boost wider innovation. And more investment in defence capabilities could also have positive spillover effects on the rest of the economy. A recent paper by Enrico Moretti of the University of California, Berkeley, and colleagues finds that “government-funded R&D in general—and defence R&D in particular—[is] effective at raising a country’s total expenditures on innovation in a given industry.” 更多与国防相关的研发可以促进更广泛的创新。对国防能力的更多投资也可能对其他经济部门产生积极的溢出效应。加州大学伯克利分校(University of California，Berkeley)的恩里科•莫雷蒂(Enrico Moretti)及其同事最近发表的一篇论文发现，“总体而言，政府资助的研发(尤其是国防研发)有效地提高了一个国家在特定行业创新方面的总支出。” Of taxes and tanks 税收和坦克 Governments have plenty of competing demands for their cash: among them caring for ageing populations, fighting climate change and paying higher interest on their debts. Some fear that higher taxes are inevitable, or that the cost will be passed to future generations as borrowing. 各国政府对资金有着大量相互竞争的需求: 其中包括照顾老龄化人口、应对气候变化以及支付更高的债务利息。一些人担心，提高税收是不可避免的，或者成本将作为借款转嫁给未来几代人。 Many governments will face pressure to backpedal on commitments to higher military expenditures. According to a recently leaked intelligence report, Justin Trudeau, Canada’s prime minister, told NATO leaders that his country would never reach the 2% target. It is not yet clear how Japan or Poland will pay for their big rises in defence. 许多国家的政府将面临压力，要求它们在增加军费开支的承诺上做出让步。根据最近泄露的一份情报报告，加拿大总理贾斯汀 · 特鲁多(Justin Trudeau)告诉北约领导人，他的国家永远无法达到2% 的目标。目前尚不清楚日本或波兰将如何为其大幅增加的国防开支买单。 Goings-on in Washington, above all, will determine the size and duration of the boom. The mainstream still wants America to maintain primacy and fend off both Russia and China. 最重要的是，华盛顿正在发生的事情将决定繁荣的规模和持续时间。主流仍然希望美国保持主导地位，同时抵御俄罗斯和中国。 But many on the “America First” populist wing want to cut support for Ukraine and, in some cases, even for the Pentagon. A third group favours redirecting military spending away from Europe and the Middle East to concentrate on China. 但是许多“美国优先”的民粹主义者希望削减对乌克兰的支持，在某些情况下，甚至削减对五角大楼的支持。第三类人支持将军费开支从欧洲和中东转向中国。 And a fourth contains figures from the left who want less spending on defence and more on social matters. The first category, the internationalist hawks, seem to have the upper hand. Confronting America’s rival is one of the few issues where there is bipartisan support. 第四个是左翼人士，他们希望减少国防支出，增加社会事务支出。第一类，国际主义鹰派，似乎占了上风。对抗美国的竞争对手是少数得到两党支持的问题之一。 Several things might push up spending. A crisis might escalate—or even draw America into direct fighting—forcing a military surge. Harry Truman, for instance, oversaw one as president during the Korean war. Short of conflict, a future president may opt for a military build-up. 有几个因素可能会推高支出。一场危机可能升级，甚至将美国卷入直接战争，迫使美国增兵。例如，哈里•杜鲁门(Harry Truman)在朝鲜战争期间曾担任美国总统。如果没有冲突，未来的总统可能会选择军事集结。 Many credit Reagan’s decision to boost defence outlays as crucial to bankrupting the Soviet Union and winning the cold war. 许多人认为，里根增加国防开支的决定，对于让苏联破产和赢得冷战至关重要。 One way or another, a new era of rearmament beckons. 无论如何，一个重整军备的新时代正在召唤我们。 As General Mark Milley, chairman of America’s joint chiefs of staff, told the Senate recently: “Preventing great-power war through readiness and deterrence is very expensive, but not as expensive as fighting a war.” And the only thing more costly than that, as he explained, is losing one. ■ 正如美国参谋长联席会议主席马克•米利(Mark Milley)将军最近对参议院所言: “通过准备就绪和威慑来防止大国之间的战争代价非常高昂，但没有打一场战争那么高昂。”正如他解释的那样，唯一比这更昂贵的就是失去一个。￭ Correction (May 24th): A previous version of this article misstated Japan’s defence-spending plans, and the number of NATO countries that spend 2% of GDP on defence. Sorry. 更正(5月24日) : 本文之前的版本错误地陈述了日本的国防开支计划，以及北约国家国防开支占国内生产总值2% 的数量。对不起。 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/international/2023/05/23/the-cost-of-the-global-arms-race 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/international/2023/05/23/the-cost-of-the-global-arms-race 下载的 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个
