Science & ... 科学..。 Sections 部分 Economic & ... 经济..。 Culture 文化 Art made by artificial intelligence is developing a style of its own 由人工智能创造的艺术正在形成自己的风格 The robotic school :: AI models not only reflect but magnify what they see in the images they are fed 机器人学校: : 人工智能模型不仅反映，而且放大他们看到的图像，他们被喂养 In Javier Marías’s final novel, an agent confronts his conscience 在哈维尔 · 马里亚斯的最后一部小说中，一个经纪人直面自己的良心 Contemplative spy fiction :: The hero of “Tomás Nevinson” is plucked from retirement for a tough final mission 沉思间谍小说: 《托马斯 · 奈文森》的主人公从退休中被挑选出来执行一项艰巨的最终任务 “The Perfection Trap” decries what it calls a “hidden epidemic” “完美陷阱”谴责所谓的“隐性传染病” Social psychology :: Thomas Curran finds some unusual culprits for the scourge of perfectionism 社会心理学: Thomas Curran 发现了完美主义的罪魁祸首 Rome fell. Will the modern-day West follow suit? 罗马帝国灭亡了，现代的西方也会如此吗？ Great-power decline :: “Why Empires Fall” makes a diverting historical comparison, if not a wholly convincing one 大国衰落: 《帝国为何衰落》做了一个有趣的历史比较，如果不是一个完全令人信服的比较的话 As it spreads across the world, who owns English? 随着它传播到世界各地，谁拥有英语？ Johnson :: Or, for that matter, French or Portuguese? 约翰逊: 或者说，法语还是葡萄牙语? ？ August Wilson was and remains a bard of black life in America 奥古斯特 · 威尔逊过去和现在都是美国黑人生活的吟游诗人 American drama :: Plays such as “Fences” and “Ma Rainey’s Black Bottom” continue to find new fans on stage and screen 美国戏剧: 《篱笆墙》、《马蕾妮的黑屁股》等戏剧不断在舞台和银幕上找到新的粉丝。 Science & ... 科学..。 Sections 部分 Economic & ... 经济..。

Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 The robotic school 机器人学校 Art made by artificial intelligence is developing a style of its own 由人工智能创造的艺术正在形成自己的风格 AI models not only reflect but magnify what they see in the images they are fed 人工智能模型不仅反映，而且放大他们看到的图像，他们被喂养 May 24th 2023 | 2023年5月24日 F IRST PRIZE in the creative category of this year’s Sony World Photography Awards was presented in April to Boris Eldagsen, who impressed judges with his ethereal, vintage-style portrait of two women in black and white. 今年的索尼世界摄影大奖创意类一等奖于4月颁发给了鲍里斯 · 埃尔达森(Boris Eldagsen)。埃尔达森以他那两位穿着黑白两色的女性优雅复古风格的肖像给评委们留下了深刻印象。 Mr Eldagsen, however, turned down the prize and revealed that his image had been created not by a camera but by artificial intelligence ( AI ). The German artist said he had “applied as a cheeky monkey”, to see if he could fool the panel. 然而，Eldagsen 先生拒绝了这个奖项，并透露他的照片不是由照相机创造的，而是由人工智能(AI)创造的。这位德国艺术家表示，他已经“申请扮演一只厚脸皮的猴子”，看看自己能否骗过评审团。 “Generative” AI models make convincing mimics, whether they are producing vintage portraits or more modern images—like a purported photo of the pope in an improbable Balenciaga puffer jacket, which recently tricked half of Twitter. Yet their ability to produce original work is less clear . “ AI art has so far been exceptionally trivial,” a columnist in the Art Newspaper recently huffed. It is intrinsically derivative, based on the millions of training images that are consumed, digested and regurgitated to order. “Plagiarism is a feature of the AI process,” declared the Writers Guild of America, one of many creative-workers’ unions that sees generative AI models as mere copycats. “生成型”人工智能模型做出了令人信服的模仿，无论它们是在制作老式肖像还是更现代的图像ーー比如一张据称是教皇穿着不可思议的巴黎世家(Balenciaga)羽绒服的照片，该照片最近骗过了半数 Twitter 用户。然而，他们创作原创作品的能力却不那么明显。“迄今为止，人工智能艺术一直是异常琐碎的,”《艺术报》的一位专栏作家最近表示。它本质上是衍生的，基于数以百万计的训练图像被消费，消化和反刍的秩序。“剽窃是人工智能过程的一个特征,”美国编剧工会宣称。许多创意工作者工会都认为，生成性人工智能模型只不过是抄袭。 Perhaps so. Yet there are signs that AI models are developing an artistic style of their own—and not just in drawing hands with more than five digits, as was common in their early output. By identifying and accentuating the patterns they see in their training data , generative models are not so much mirrors of contemporary styles as they are magnifying lenses. Slowly, a robotic school of artwork is emerging. 也许吧。然而，有迹象表明，人工智能模型正在形成自己的艺术风格ーー而不仅仅是像早期产品那样，用五个数字以上的手画手。通过识别和强调他们在训练数据中看到的模式，生成模型与其说是当代风格的镜子，不如说是放大镜。慢慢地，一个机器人学校的艺术品正在出现。 Computers have been making art for some time. Five years ago Christie’s auctioned its first work created by artificial intelligence, “Edmond de Belamy”, a blurry approximation of an 18th-century oil painting. 电脑创造艺术已经有一段时间了。5年前，佳士得(Christie’s)拍卖了其首件人工智能创作的作品《埃德蒙德•德•贝拉米》(Edmond de Belamy) ，这是一幅模糊的近似于18世纪油画的作品。 Despite being unfinished and faintly alien-looking, the novelty fetched $432,500. “It may not have been painted by a man in a powdered wig,” Christie’s said, “but it is exactly the kind of artwork we have been selling for 250 years.” 尽管是未完成的，看起来有点像外星人，这个新奇的东西卖了432,500美元。克里斯蒂拍卖行表示: “这幅画可能不是一个戴着假发的男人画的，但它确实是我们250年来一直在销售的那种艺术品。” Technology has since come far. Open platforms such as Midjourney and DALL-E generate images in seconds based on simple written prompts. No artistic skill is required, though there is a knack to getting good results. “The image I produce isn’t my work. My work is the prompt,” says Nick St Pierre, a designer in New York who got into AI last year when he saw it coming for his job. The image above, which he made using Midjourney, took hundreds of iterations, ending up with this prompt: 自那以后，科技已经取得了长足的进步。Midjourney 和 DALL-e 等开放平台基于简单的书面提示，可以在几秒钟内生成图像。不需要任何艺术技巧，尽管有获得好结果的诀窍。“我创造的形象不是我的作品。纽约设计师尼克•圣皮埃尔(Nick St Pierre)表示: “我的工作是一种激励。”去年，当他看到人工智能正在向自己的工作靠拢时，他进入了人工智能领域。上面的图片是他用 Midjourney 制作的，经过数百次反复，最后得到了这样一个提示: 35mm, 1990s action film still, close-up of a bearded man browsing for bottles inside a liquor store. WATCH OUT BEHIND YOU!!! 35毫米，上世纪90年代的动作片特写镜头，一个大胡子男人在一家酒类商店里挑瓶子。小心身后! ! ！ (background action occurs)…a white benz truck crashes through a store window, exploding into the background…broken glass flies everywhere, flaming debris sparkles light the neon night, 90s CGI , gritty realism (背景动作发生) ... 一辆白色的奔驰卡车撞破了商店的橱窗，爆炸成为背景... 碎玻璃到处飞，燃烧的碎片闪烁着霓虹灯的夜晚，90年代的 CGI，坚韧的现实主义 The ability to order any image in any style, from action movies of the 1990s to papal paparazzi shots, seems incompatible with the idea of a distinctive AI look. Yet certain traits seem to predominate. One is nostalgia. The ability of AI models to recreate the styles they have been fed makes them adept at simulating the look of old media, or sometimes recreating past events in modern guise. 从上世纪90年代的动作片到教皇的狗仔队照片，任何风格的图像都可以订购，这似乎与独特的人工智能外观理念格格不入。然而，某些特征似乎占主导地位。一个是怀旧。人工智能模型能够重新创建它们所接受的样式，这使得它们擅长模拟旧媒体的外观，或者有时以现代伪装重新创建过去的事件。 One recent viral series imagined modern-day selfies taken in pre-modern times, with Vikings or cavemen grinning for the camera. 最近的一个病毒式的系列想象在前现代时期拍摄的现代自拍照，维京人或山顶洞人对着镜头露齿而笑。 As in previous eras of art, the prevailing subject matter is determined by whoever commissions the work. 和以前的艺术时代一样，流行的主题是由委托作品的人决定的。 Western galleries groan with pictures of rich white men and their property, not because they looked good in oil paint but because these were the themes dearest to the hearts of patrons. Midjourney’s output is big on anime warrior princesses for the same reason. 西方画廊里满是富有的白人男子和他们的财产的照片，这并不是因为它们在油画中看起来很好，而是因为这些是顾客心中最珍视的主题。出于同样的原因，Midjourney 的动漫产品在动漫武士公主方面表现突出。 AI models seem to favour particular techniques, too. Examining images produced by Midjourney earlier this year, Roland Meyer, a researcher at Ruhr University Bochum in Germany, noticed a common look. Many would “shine and sparkle, as if illuminated from within”. 人工智能模型似乎也偏爱特定的技术。今年早些时候，德国波鸿大学研究所的研究员罗兰•迈耶(Roland Meyer)通过研究 Midtrip 公司生产的图像，发现了一个普遍的现象。许多人会“闪闪发光，仿佛从内部被照亮”。 They combined this distinctive lighting with a colour palette that contrasted warm earthy tones with blue or green metallic ones. 他们结合了这种独特的照明与色彩调色板，对比温暖的泥土色调与蓝色或绿色金属的。 Many also displayed what photographers call “high dynamic range”, meaning a wide variation in light levels within the image, with pools of deep shadow and other areas brightly lit. 许多照片还展示了摄影师所说的“高动态范围”，即照片中光线水平的广泛变化，有深色阴影和其他明亮区域。 These traits are not coincidental. Mr Meyer attributes the distinctive “glow” to the way AI models handle lighting. Rather than using digital art techniques such as ray tracing, in which a computer calculates how light will fall on an object from a particular angle, AI imagines how things will look based on the images in its training data, which have been illuminated from many different angles. For this reason, even photo-realistic AI images seem to be lit in a way more akin to painting than to naturalistic photography, Mr Meyer says. 这些特征并非巧合。Meyer 先生将这种独特的“发光”归功于人工智能模型处理照明的方式。人工智能没有使用射线追踪等数字艺术技术(计算机计算光线如何从特定角度照射到物体上) ，而是根据其训练数据中的图像来想象物体的外观，这些图像已经从许多不同角度照射到物体上。出于这个原因，即使照片逼真的人工智能图像似乎在某种程度上更接近于绘画，而不是自然主义摄影，迈耶先生说。 Painting by numbers 数字绘画 The favoured colour schemes, meanwhile, often pair teal or turquoise with orange or magenta. That reflects a taste popularised on Instagram and from there across the internet, which is likely to have been well-represented in training data. 与此同时，人们青睐的颜色搭配常常是青绿色或绿松石色与橙色或品红色。这反映了一种在 Instagram 和互联网上流行起来的品味，这种品味很可能在培训数据中得到了很好的体现。 (High dynamic range has been made similarly ubiquitous online by modern smartphone cameras, which produce such effects by taking several images at different exposures and stitching them together.) (高动态范围已经被现代智能手机摄像头在网上同样普及，它通过在不同的曝光下拍摄几张照片并将它们缝合在一起来产生这样的效果。) With updates to AI models come changes to their oeuvre. David Holz, the founder and boss of Midjourney, rejects the idea that there is a single AI style. The latest version of the program, released in March, has reined in some of the tendencies of the previous one, he says. “The look of our images has radically changed five times now over the past 12 months and will continue to do so for quite some time,” Mr Holtz insists. 随着人工智能模型的更新，他们的全部作品也发生了变化。Midjourney 创始人兼老板大卫•霍尔兹(davidholz)否认存在单一的人工智能风格。他说，三月份发布的最新版本已经抑制了前一版本的某些倾向。“在过去的12个月里，我们的照片的外观已经发生了5次根本性的变化，而且这种变化还将持续相当长的一段时间,”霍尔茨先生坚持说。 “People want diversity.” “人们想要多样性。” As the models evolve and improve, they will remain reliant on man-made training data. “No collection is ever neutral,” says James Coupe, head of photography at the Royal College of Art in London, where he is setting up an AI lab. “Image archives are indexes of a society’s changing ideological investments and political commitments.” And whatever the training data, “we are going to end up with a style that is a kind of lowest common denominator of art.” He compares AI -generated images to a project in the 1990s by a pair of Russian conceptual artists, Vitaly Komar and Alexander Melamid. They surveyed public tastes in 14 countries and produced artworks based on the results. Nearly everyone went for banal 19th-century-style landscapes. 随着模型的发展和改进，它们仍将依赖于人造的训练数据。伦敦皇家艺术学院(Royal College of Art)摄影负责人詹姆斯•库佩(James Coupe)表示: “没有哪个收藏品是中立的。”库佩正在伦敦建立一个人工智能实验室。“形象档案是一个社会不断变化的意识形态投资和政治承诺的指标。”无论培训数据如何，“我们最终都会形成一种类似于艺术最小公分母的风格。”他将人工智能生成的图像与上世纪90年代两位俄罗斯概念艺术家维塔利•科马尔(Vitaly Komar)和亚历山大•梅拉米德(Alexander Melamid)的一个项目进行了比较。他们调查了14个国家的公众品味，并根据调查结果制作了艺术品。几乎每个人都喜欢19世纪平淡无奇的风景画。 By identifying and condensing prevailing styles in their training data, generative- AI models are playing a similar role. Mr Meyer thinks their ability to summarise visual styles or eras could make them powerful tools of analysis for art historians. Both their weakness and their strength is that, as he puts it, they are “cliché detectors”. ■ 通过在训练数据中识别和浓缩流行的风格，生成性人工智能模型正在发挥类似的作用。Meyer 先生认为他们总结视觉风格或时代的能力可以使他们成为艺术历史学家分析的强大工具。他们的弱点和优点都在于，用他的话说，他们是“陈词滥调的探测器”。￭ For more on the latest books, films, TV shows, albums and controversies, sign up to Plot Twist , our weekly subscriber-only newsletter 更多关于最新的书籍，电影，电视节目，专辑和争议，注册到情节扭曲，我们的每周订阅者唯一的时事通讯 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/24/art-made-by-artificial-intelligence-is-developing-a-style-of-its-own 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/24/art-made-by-artificial-intelligence-is-developing-a-style-of-its-own 下载的 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Contemplative spy fiction 沉思间谍小说 In Javier Marías’s final novel, an agent confronts his conscience 在哈维尔 · 马里亚斯的最后一部小说中，一个经纪人直面自己的良心 The hero of “Tomás Nevinson” is plucked from retirement for a tough final mission 《托马斯 · 奈文森》的主人公从退休中被挑选出来执行一项艰巨的最后任务 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 Tomás Nevinson. By Javier Marías. Translated by Margaret Jull Costa. Knopf; 656 pages; $32.50. Hamish Hamilton; £22.00 托马斯 · 内文森(Tomás Nevinson)著，哈维尔 · 马里亚斯(Javier Marías)著，玛格丽特 · 朱尔 · 科斯塔(Margaret Jull Costa)译，克诺夫出版社，656页，32.50美元，哈米什 · 汉密尔顿(Hamish Hamilton)出版社，22.00美元 W HEN JAVIER MARÍAS died last September at the age of 70, he left a richly beguiling, stylistically bold and, above all, original body of work. Whether exploring academic life, marriage or the murky world of espionage , the Spanish author’s novels, written over five decades, blend intellectual inquiry with playful and artful high jinks. 当哈维尔 · 马里亚斯于去年九月去世，享年70岁时，他留下了一部富有诱惑力、风格大胆、最重要的是原创性的作品。无论是探索学术生活，婚姻还是黑暗的间谍世界，这位西班牙作家的小说，写了50多年，融合了智力探索与顽皮和艺术的狂欢。 Marías’s final book revisits characters and themes from his penultimate one, “Berta Isla” (published in 2017). He saw “Tomás Nevinson” as a “companion piece” to its predecessor—but it is a better novel. The eponymous hero comes fully alive as he navigates shadowy machinations and ethical quandaries. 马里亚斯的最后一本书重温了倒数第二本书《贝尔塔岛》(Berta Isla，2017年出版)中的人物和主题。他把《托马斯 · 奈文森》看作是前作的“伴侣作品”ーー但它是一部更好的小说。这位与他同名的英雄在阴谋诡计和道德困境中生龙活虎。 Tomás Nevinson’s days in the British Secret Service are over. Back in his home town of Madrid, the half-English, half-Spanish former spy is trying to rebuild his family life after years of “enforced exile”. 托马斯 · 奈文森在英国特勤局的日子结束了。回到家乡马德里，这位半英半西的前间谍正试图在多年“被迫流亡”后重建家庭生活。 But when his old handler and manipulator, Bertram Tupra, tracks him down and tempts him with one last assignment, Tomás is roused from retirement, reactivated and once again obliged to “dovetail lies and false identities”. 但是，当他的老上司和操纵者伯特伦 · 图普拉(Bertram Tupra)找到他，并用最后一个任务诱惑他时，托马斯从退休中醒来，重新被激活，再次被迫“接合谎言和假身份”。 His mission requires him to go undercover in north-west Spain and find out which of three women is a terrorist with ETA , a Basque separatist group, who is now lying low after taking part in bombings in Barcelona and Zaragoza. 他的任务要求他去西班牙西北部卧底，找出三个女人中哪一个是巴斯克分离主义组织埃塔(ETA)的恐怖分子。埃塔在参与巴塞罗那和萨拉戈萨的爆炸案后，目前正在低调行事。 Posing as a teacher of English, Tomás embarks on a charm offensive, befriending the women—a fellow schoolteacher, a restaurant owner and a trapped wife—and forging more intimate relations with one of them. 托马斯假扮成一名英语教师，展开了一场魅力攻势，与这些女性——一名教师同事、一名餐馆老板和一名陷入困境的妻子——成为朋友，并与其中一名女性建立了更亲密的关系。 He watches their movements and reactions, all the while looking for clues that might betray a hidden identity and a role in past crimes. 他观察他们的行动和反应，同时寻找可能暴露隐藏身份和过去犯罪角色的线索。 But then Tupra, impatient for results, changes the rules. If Tomás cannot unmask and dispatch the terrorist, then all three women will have to die. Can he find the culprit in time—and will he have the unscrupulous courage to carry out his “blood mission”? 但随后，急于求成的 Tupra 改变了规则。如果托马斯不能揭露并消灭恐怖分子那三个女人都得死。他能否及时找到罪魁祸首，以及他是否有勇气去完成他的“血之使命”？ Those who prefer their espionage thrillers lean and taut may be put off by this hulking tome. But like the stories in Marías’s bravura trilogy, “Your Face Tomorrow” , “Tomás Nevinson” is a different kind of spy novel. It unfolds in meandering yet mesmerising sentences. Some describe scenes, others contribute to free-flowing monologues or digressive meditations. 那些喜欢他们的间谍惊悚小说的人可能会被这本笨重的大部头吓跑。但就像 Marías 大胆三部曲《明日你的脸》(Your Face Tomorrow)中的故事一样，《托马斯•内文森》(Tomás Nevinson)是一部与众不同的间谍小说。它以蜿蜒而迷人的句子展开。一些描述场景，另一些有助于自由流畅的独白或离题的沉思。 The author’s other trademark tropes, from literary allusions to historical references and philosophical debates, all feature. 作者的其他商标比喻，从文学典故到历史参考和哲学辩论，所有的特点。 Occasionally the narrative is weighed down by ponderous musings on topics such as hatred, justice and killing for the greater good. 有时，对于仇恨、正义和为了更大的利益而杀戮等话题的沉闷思考会拖累叙事。 But it is never long before readers are again enthralled by Marías’s high-stakes plot, his protagonist’s tangled web of deceit and the sharp scrutiny of guilt, doubt, betrayal and memory. 但是没过多久，读者们就再次被马里亚斯高风险的情节、他笔下主人公错综复杂的欺骗网络以及对内疚、怀疑、背叛和记忆的尖锐审视所吸引。 Now seamlessly translated by Margaret Jull Costa, this final work is a stimulating drama by a master of his craft. ■ 现在由玛格丽特 · 朱尔 · 科斯塔无缝翻译，这最后的工作是一个激动人心的戏剧大师的手艺。￭ For more on the latest books, films, TV shows, albums and controversies, sign up to Plot Twist , our weekly subscriber-only newsletter 更多关于最新的书籍，电影，电视节目，专辑和争议，注册到情节扭曲，我们的每周订阅者唯一的时事通讯 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/in-javier-mariass-final-novel-an-agent-confronts-his-conscience 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/in-javier-mariass-final-novel-an-agent-confronts-his-conscience 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Social psychology 社会心理学 “The Perfection Trap” decries what it calls a “hidden epidemic” “完美陷阱”谴责所谓的“隐性传染病” Thomas Curran finds some unusual culprits for the scourge of perfectionism Thomas Curran 发现了完美主义的罪魁祸首 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 The Perfection Trap. By Thomas Curran. Cornerstone Press; 304 pages; £22. To be published in America by Scribner in August; $28 完美陷阱。作者: Thomas Curran。基石出版社; 304页; 22。将于八月由 Scribner 出版社在美国出版，售价28美元 I N NATHANIEL HAWTHORNE’S short story “The Birth-Mark”, a chemist called Aylmer marries a young woman, Georgiana, whose sole imperfection is a red blemish on her left cheek. He considers it a “fatal flaw”; she pleads with him to use his skills to remove it. 在纳撒尼尔·霍桑的短篇小说《胎记》中，一位名叫艾尔默的化学家娶了一位名叫乔治亚娜的年轻女子，她唯一的缺陷就是左脸颊上的一个红斑。他认为这是一个“致命的缺陷”; 她恳求他利用他的技能来消除它。 Stumbling on his journal, however, Georgiana is astonished to find it is a catalogue of scientific mishaps. Might his loathing of her birthmark stem from his professional disappointment? At length Aylmer concocts a potion that has the desired effect—and promptly kills her. 然而，偶然发现他的日记，乔治亚娜惊讶地发现它是一个科学灾难的目录。他对她胎记的厌恶可能源于他对工作的失望吗？最后，艾尔默配制了一种药剂，达到了预期的效果，并迅速杀死了她。 Hawthorne’s 180-year-old tale illustrates the perils of perfectionism . It is Thomas Curran’s starting-point for a study of what he calls a “hidden epidemic”. He thinks the obsessive pursuit of ever higher standards, rather than propelling achievement, is mainly a scourge. 霍桑180年的故事说明了完美主义的危险。这是 Thomas Curran 研究他所谓的“隐性流行病”的起点。他认为，过分追求更高的标准，而不是推动成就，主要是一种灾难。 A social psychologist at the London School of Economics, he describes himself as “a recovering perfectionist”. 作为伦敦政治经济学院(London School of Economics)的社会心理学家，他形容自己是“一个正在恢复的完美主义者”。 Drawing on both academic research and his own experiences of “deficit thinking”, he makes a vigorous case, albeit one occasionally marked by cliché (he sees his younger self as a “chin-stroking, cardigan-wearing intellectual”). 利用学术研究和自己的“赤字思维”经验，他提出了一个强有力的理由，尽管这个理由有时带有陈词滥调的特点(他认为年轻时的自己是一个“摸着下巴、穿着羊毛衫的知识分子”)。 Mr Curran distinguishes between three sorts of perfectionism. The first, which looks inward, is the relentless self-scolding of the workaholic or punctilious student. A second version, directed towards others, is commonly found in bosses who have unrealistic expectations of their staff and decry their supposed failings (he cites Steve Jobs as an example). The third and most troublesome kind is the form imputed to society: “an all-encompassing belief that everybody, at all times, expects us to be perfect”. Its victims tend to feel lonely; often they harm themselves and harbour thoughts of suicide. Curran 先生区分了三种完美主义。第一个是内省，是对工作狂或一丝不苟的学生无情的自责。第二个版本是针对其他人的，通常发生在对员工抱有不切实际的期望并谴责他们所谓的失败的老板身上(他以史蒂夫•乔布斯(Steve Jobs)为例)。第三种，也是最麻烦的一种是归咎于社会的形式: “一种包罗万象的信念，每个人，在任何时候，都希望我们是完美的”。它的受害者往往感到孤独; 他们往往伤害自己，并怀有自杀的念头。 Having noted the ways in which this pathology inflames vulnerabilities and erodes resilience, Mr Curran suggests some causes. 柯伦先生注意到了这种病理是如何加剧脆弱性并侵蚀复原力的，他提出了一些原因。 These include a lack of job security, neurotic supervision by helicopter parents and the gaudy blandishments of advertising, which fuel consumption and anxiety. 这些问题包括缺乏工作保障、直升机父母神经质的监督以及花哨的广告诱惑，这些都加剧了消费和焦虑。 “The very fabric of this economy”, he claims, “is woven from our discontent.” Inevitably he blames social media, which inundate users with images of finely sculpted bodies, flawless outfits, ambrosial holidays and exquisite weddings. 他声称: “这个经济体的结构就是由我们的不满编织而成的。”他不可避免地将此归咎于社交媒体，这些媒体上充斥着精心雕琢的身体、完美的服装、奢华的节日和精致的婚礼的图片。 A less familiar culprit is Don Hamachek, an American psychologist, who in the 1970s coined the term “normal perfectionism”, thereby legitimising morbid self-criticism, says Mr Curran. He even reproaches Barack Obama for enjoining young people to learn from their mistakes. 柯伦先生说，一个不太为人熟知的罪魁祸首是美国心理学家唐 · 哈马切克(Don Hamachek) ，他在20世纪70年代创造了“正常完美主义”这个术语，从而使病态的自我批评合法化。他甚至指责巴拉克•奥巴马(Barack Obama)要求年轻人从错误中吸取教训。 Instead, he says, failures should be “allowed to simply wash through us as a joyous reminder of what it means to be a fallible human”. 相反，他说，应该“让失败简单地洗刷我们，作为一个快乐的提醒，提醒我们作为一个易犯错误的人意味着什么”。 The author’s greatest odium, though, is directed at meritocracy. In this he draws on the thinking of Michael Sandel, a philosopher at Harvard. Especially in “The Tyranny of Merit” (published in 2020), Professor Sandel has argued that using education as a giant sorting machine creates a toxic obsession with credentials, dividing society into winners and losers and depleting the common good. 不过，作者最大的不满是针对精英管理。在这方面，他借鉴了哈佛大学哲学家迈克尔•桑德尔(MichaelSandel)的思想。特别是在2020年出版的《功绩的暴政》(The Tyranny of Merit)一书中，桑德尔教授认为，把教育当作一台巨大的分类机器，会造成对学历的痴迷，将社会分为赢家和输家，并且会损害公共利益。 Like other critics of meritocracy, Mr Curran has a point—until you consider the alternatives. 像其他批评精英制度的人一样，柯伦先生说得有道理ーー除非你考虑其他选择。 Daily life, in his view, now resembles an endless tribunal. Young people suffer most from the constant scrutiny of scores and rankings. 在他看来，现在的日常生活就像是一场无休止的审判。年轻人最容易受到成绩和排名的不断审查。 Decrying a fixation on economic growth, he applauds countries, such as Bhutan and New Zealand, where decision-makers take account of citizens’ happiness. Mr Curran’s preferred fix is a universal basic income, which he says would “extinguish the fire of perfectionism”. 他谴责对经济增长的执着，称赞像不丹和新西兰这样的国家，这些国家的决策者考虑到了公民的幸福。柯伦先生更喜欢的解决办法是普及基本收入，他说这将“熄灭完美主义的火焰”。 Whatever its economic merits, his argument errs in treating perfectionism as a purely cultural phenomenon. Might it not also be a disposition embedded in the psyche? Tellingly, his guidance—“Keep going. 无论其经济价值如何，他的论点都错误地将完美主义视为一种纯粹的文化现象。这难道不是一种植根于心灵的倾向吗？很明显，他的指导是: “继续前进。 Do not yield”; “Keep practising that acceptance of fortune and fate”—is couched in the language of the perfectionist’s round-the-clock report card. ■ “不要屈服”; “坚持练习接受命运和命运”ーー用完美主义者24小时不间断的成绩单的语言表达。￭ For more on the latest books, films, TV shows, albums and controversies, sign up to Plot Twist , our weekly subscriber-only newsletter 更多关于最新的书籍，电影，电视节目，专辑和争议，注册到情节扭曲，我们的每周订阅者唯一的时事通讯 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/the-perfection-trap-decries-what-it-calls-a-hidden-epidemic 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/the-perfection-trap-decries-what-it-calls-a-hidden-epidemic 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Great-power decline 大国衰落 Rome fell. Will the modern-day West follow suit? 罗马帝国灭亡了，现代的西方也会如此吗？ “Why Empires Fall” makes a diverting historical comparison, if not a wholly convincing one “帝国为什么会衰落”做了一个有趣的历史比较，如果不是一个完全令人信服的比较 May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 Why Empires Fall . By John Rapley and Peter Heather. Yale University Press; 200 pages; $27. Allen Lane; £20 《为什么帝国会衰落》 ，约翰•拉普利和彼得•希瑟著，耶鲁大学出版社200页，27美元，艾伦•莱恩出版社出版，20美元 D ECLINISM IS IN fashion again. As relations between America and China worsen, studying the ends of earlier ages of hegemony becomes more popular. Books predicting the unstoppable rise of autocratic strongmen and the death of democracy proliferate. There is much talk of the “Thucydides trap ”: the inevitability of a clash between a rising power and an established one, as Athens challenged Sparta in the fifth century BC . 衰退主义再次流行起来。随着中美关系的恶化，研究早期霸权时代的终结变得越来越流行。预言独裁统治者势不可挡的崛起和民主政体灭亡的书籍层出不穷。有很多关于“修昔底德陷阱”的讨论: 当雅典在前5世纪挑战斯巴达时，一个新兴大国和一个老牌大国之间不可避免地会发生冲突。 This provocative short book adapts this approach with a novel twist. It draws a comparison between the West in 1999, the zenith of its confidence, and Rome exactly 1,600 years earlier, in 399—just decades before the empire’s collapse. 这本具有煽动性的短篇小说以一种新颖的方式改编了这种方法。它将1999年西方世界的信心巅峰与1600年前的399年罗马帝国的崩溃前几十年进行了对比。 The most interesting part of the story concerns Rome . John Rapley, a political economist, and Peter Heather, a historian, dissent from the analysis familiar since Edward Gibbon of an empire in gradual decline almost from its inception under Augustus. Rome, they argue, was as strong as ever at the turn of the fifth century AD . An admittedly extensive and sometimes rickety empire was held together mainly by co-opting talented outsiders, devolving extensive power to strong commanders and doing deals with potential enemies. 故事中最有趣的部分是关于罗马的。政治经济学家约翰•拉普利(John Rapley)和历史学家彼得•希瑟(Peter Heather)不同意自爱德华•吉本(Edward Gibbon)以来人们所熟悉的分析，即一个帝国几乎从奥古斯都(Augustus)时期开始就在逐渐衰落。他们认为，罗马在公元5世纪之交一如既往地强大。不可否认，一个庞大而有时摇摇欲坠的帝国主要是通过吸收有才华的外来者、将广泛的权力下放给强大的指挥官以及与潜在敌人做交易才得以维系起来的。 Yet within a decade Rome had pulled its legions out of Britain (the authors dub this withdrawal the first Brexit ). Less than a century later, the last western emperor, Romulus Augustulus, was gone. 然而，不到十年，罗马就从英国撤出了大批军队(作者称这次撤军为第一次英国退欧)。不到一个世纪之后，西方最后一位皇帝——罗慕路斯·奥古斯都——也走了。 What went wrong? The book dwells on foreign forces that became ever harder to resist. At the empire’s weakening periphery, local bigwigs began throwing in their lot with Goths , Ostrogoths, Visigoths, Huns, Vandals and the rest. The sister of one emperor even married a Visigoth leader (apparently voluntarily) and produced a son with serious imperial claims. As Gibbon laboured to explain 250 years ago, the eastern empire based in Constantinople continued for almost another millennium. 出了什么问题？这本书详述了越来越难以抗拒的外国势力。在帝国日渐衰弱的边缘地带，当地的大人物开始与哥特人、东哥特人、西哥特人、匈奴人、汪达尔人和其他人为伍。一位皇帝的妹妹甚至嫁给了一位西哥特领导人(显然是自愿的) ，并生下了一个有着严重帝国主义要求的儿子。正如吉本在250年前努力解释的那样，以君士坦丁堡为基地的东方帝国又延续了近一千年。 But it, too, was weakening and steadily losing territory, initially through costly wars against Persia and later with the rise of Islam and the Ottomans . 但它也在日渐衰弱，并逐渐丧失领土，最初是通过对波斯的代价高昂的战争，后来是随着伊斯兰教和奥斯曼帝国的崛起。 Over the centuries western Europe recovered, first under Charlemagne and later even more spectacularly when it spread its power (and its own empires) across most of the known world. 经过几个世纪的恢复，西欧首先在查理曼大帝的统治下复苏，后来更为引人注目的是，它将自己的权力(以及自己的帝国)扩展到了大多数已知的世界。 The question the authors pose is whether, despite its apparent dominance as recently as the turn of the 21st century, the West is now inexorably heading in the same direction as Rome in the fifth. 两位作者提出的问题是，尽管在21世纪之交，西方世界表面上占据主导地位，但它们现在是否正不可阻挡地朝着与罗马在第五世纪相同的方向前进。 They point to such issues as rising debt burdens, costly welfare states, depressed productivity growth, demographic decline and mass immigration. 他们指出，这些问题包括债务负担不断增加、福利国家成本高昂、生产率增长低迷、人口下降和大规模移民。 They see big challenges to the West from the growth of Asia, particularly China and India, and also from anti-Western powers such as Russia and the rise of Africa. 他们认为，亚洲的发展，特别是中国和印度的发展，以及俄罗斯等反西方大国和非洲的崛起，对西方构成了巨大挑战。 Yet the analogy with Rome’s decline and fall is ultimately unconvincing. The rest of the world is certainly catching up with the West, both economically and demographically. China’s economy may soon be bigger than America’s. 然而，将其与罗马帝国的衰落相提并论，最终是不能令人信服的。世界其他地区无疑正在经济和人口方面赶上西方。中国的经济规模可能很快就会超过美国。 Europe accounted for a quarter of the global population in 1914, but has less than a tenth today. Immigration, especially from Africa and Latin America, is politically testing. Populism is on the march. 1914年，欧洲人口占全球总人口的四分之一，但如今只有不到十分之一。移民，尤其是来自非洲和拉丁美洲的移民，正在经受政治考验。民粹主义正在发展。 All the same, on a global scale it is hard to envisage a serious military rival to the clout of the United States and its European allies. Russia is revanchist in Ukraine , but it is also in long-term decline. China is brittle and its growth is slowing sharply. India is politically rancorous. The West’s grip on the best technology and research is firm. 尽管如此，在全球范围内，很难想象会有一个与美国及其欧洲盟友的影响力相当的军事对手。俄罗斯在乌克兰是复仇主义者，但它也处于长期衰落之中。中国经济脆弱，增长急剧放缓。印度在政治上充满敌意。西方对最好的技术和研究的控制是坚定的。 And though Europe’s economic prospects may be cloudy, American productivity leaves most competitors in the dust. 尽管欧洲的经济前景可能不明朗，但美国的生产率却让大多数竞争对手望尘莫及。 Declinists like to cite George Bernard Shaw, whose dictum on the transience of great powers jokily invoked an English village: “Rome fell. Babylon fell. Hindhead’s turn will come.” It is tempting to think that way. 衰落主义者喜欢引用萧伯纳，他们关于大国短暂性的格言开玩笑地引用了一个英国村庄: “罗马陷落了。巴比伦沦陷了。迟早会轮到后脑勺的。”这样想是很诱人的。 But for now, at least, it is not obvious when, if ever, the West will follow suit. ■ 但至少就目前而言，还不清楚西方国家何时会效仿(如果有的话)。 ￭ For more on the latest books, films, TV shows, albums and controversies, sign up to Plot Twist , our weekly subscriber-only newsletter 更多关于最新的书籍，电影，电视节目，专辑和争议，注册到情节扭曲，我们的每周订阅者唯一的时事通讯 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/rome-fell-will-the-modern-day-west-follow-suit 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/rome-fell-will-the-modern-day-west-follow-suit 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Johnson 约翰逊 As it spreads across the world, who owns English? 随着它传播到世界各地，谁拥有英语？ Or, for that matter, French or Portuguese? 或者说，法国人还是葡萄牙人？ May 25th 2023 | 2023年5月25日 W HAT COUNTRY does French belong to? The answer seems obvious: France, as it says on the label. But there are roughly four times as many speakers of French outside France as there are within it. Who does Portuguese belong to? 法国属于哪个国家？答案似乎显而易见: 正如标签上所写的那样，是法国。但是法国境外讲法语的人数大约是法国境内讲法语人数的四倍。葡萄牙人是谁的？ You might now hesitate to blurt out “Portugal”, remembering that Brazil’s population is about 20 times bigger than Portugal’s. Maybe Portuguese belongs jointly to them both. But then 70m people live in African countries in which Portuguese is an official language. Perhaps it belongs to them, too. 你现在可能会犹豫要不要脱口而出“葡萄牙”，要知道巴西的人口是葡萄牙的20倍左右。也许葡萄牙语同时属于他们两个。但是，有7000万人生活在葡萄牙语为官方语言的非洲国家。也许它也属于他们。 The English can be under no illusion that the language of the same name is exclusively theirs. The small matters of the other nations in the British Isles, and of the superpower across the Atlantic, make clear that it is joint property. 英国人不会幻想同一个名字的语言只属于他们自己。不列颠群岛的其他国家和大西洋彼岸的超级大国的小事情清楚地表明，这是共同财产。 But these countries—along with Canada, Australia and other Anglophone peoples—must at some point come to terms with the fact that, even collectively, their language no longer belongs to them. 但是，这些国家ーー以及加拿大、澳大利亚和其他讲英语的国家ーー必须在某个时候接受这样一个事实: 即使是整体而言，他们的语言也不再属于他们。 Of the estimated billion people who speak English, less than half live in those core English-speaking countries. 在说英语的大约十亿人口中，只有不到一半生活在核心的英语国家。 Every day, the proportion of English-speakers born outside the traditional Anglosphere grows. Perhaps 40% of people in the European Union speak English , or about 180m—vastly more than the combined population of Britain, Canada, Australia and New Zealand. In India, calculations range from 60m to 200m. Most such estimates make it the second-biggest Anglophone country in the world. 每天，出生在传统英语圈以外的英语使用者的比例都在增长。欧盟(EU)大约有40% 的人会说英语，约为1.8亿人，远远超过英国、加拿大、澳大利亚和新西兰的总人口。在印度，计算范围从6000万到2亿不等。大多数这样的估计使它成为世界上第二大讲英语的国家。 English-speakers pride themselves on the spread of the language , and often attribute that to an open, liberal-minded attitude whereby it has happily soaked up words from around the world. In the coming century, though, English will do more than borrow words. 说英语的人为这种语言的传播感到自豪，他们常常把这归功于一种开放、自由的态度，因为这种态度让英语快乐地吸收了来自世界各地的词汇。然而，在下一个世纪，英语将不仅仅是借用单词。 In these non-Anglophone countries, it is becoming not just a useful second language, but a native one. 在这些非英语国家，它不仅成为一种有用的第二语言，而且成为一种母语。 Already it is easy to find children in northern Europe who speak as though they come from Kansas, the product of childhoods immersed in subtitled films and television in English, along with music, gaming and YouTube. 我们已经很容易在北欧找到这样的孩子，他们说起话来好像来自堪萨斯州，这是他们沉浸在带字幕的英语电影和电视，以及音乐、游戏和 YouTube 中的童年时代的产物。 Today, many learners still aim for an American or British standard. 今天，许多学习者仍然以美国或英国的标准为目标。 Textbooks instruct Indian English-speakers to avoid Indianisms such as “What is your good name?” for “What is your first name?”, or “I am working here for years” instead of “I have been working here for years.” A guide to avoiding Europeanisms has long circulated in European Union institutions, to keep French- or German-speakers from (for example) using “actual” to mean “current”, as it does in their languages. 教科书指导说英语的印度人避免使用“你的好名字是什么?”“你叫什么名字?”或者“我已经在这里工作很多年了”，而不是“我已经在这里工作很多年了”避免使用欧洲词汇的指南在欧盟机构中流传已久，以防止说法语或德语的人像他们的语言那样使用“实际的”来表示“当前的”。 Yet as hundreds of millions of new speakers make English their own, they are going to be less keen to sound British or American. 然而，随着数以亿计的新人将英语变成自己的语言，他们将不再那么热衷于听起来像英国人或美国人。 A generation of post-colonial novelists has been mixing native words and phrasings into their English prose, without translation, italics or explanation. Academic movements such as “English as a lingua franca” ( ELF ) have been developing the ideology that speakers—no longer referred to as “non-native” but rather “multilingual”—should feel free to ignore British or American norms. Karen Bennett of Nova University in Lisbon says the university website has been translated using words common in southern European English—like “scientific” for “academic”, or “rector” for “vice-chancellor”. 一代后殖民时代的小说家一直在将本土词汇和短语混合到他们的英语散文中，没有翻译、斜体字或解释。“英语作为通用语言”(ELF)等学术运动一直在发展这样一种意识形态: 说英语的人(不再被称为“非母语人士”，而是“多语种人士”)应该可以随意无视英国或美国的规范。里斯本新星大学(Nova University)的卡伦•贝内特(Karen Bennett)表示，该大学的网站被翻译成了南欧英语中常见的词汇，比如“学术”的“科学”，或“副校长”的“校长”。 The appropriate local dialect is not British or American but ELF . 适当的当地方言不是英国或美国，而是 ELF。 Given enough time, new generations of native speakers contribute not just words but their own grammar to the language they learn—from older speakers’ point of view, distorting it in the process. 如果有足够的时间，新一代的母语使用者不仅会为他们所学的语言贡献词汇，还会贡献他们自己的语法ーー从老一辈使用者的角度来看，这个过程会扭曲语言。 “I am working here for years” is a mistake today, but it is not hard to imagine it becoming standard in the future in culturally confident Anglophone Indian circles. 如今，“我在这里工作了很多年”是一个错误，但不难想象，在以英语为母语、文化自信的印度人圈子里，这种说法将来会成为标准说法。 If this disturbs you, remember that this column is written in a mangled version of Anglo-Saxon, learned badly by waves of Celts, Vikings, Normans and others until it became an unrecognisably different tongue. 如果这让你感到困扰，请记住，这篇专栏文章是用一种混乱的盎格鲁-撒克逊语写成的，被一波波的凯尔特人、维京人、诺曼人和其他人糟糕地学会了，直到它变成了一种难以辨认的不同语言。 And take comfort in the fact that such changes usually happen too slowly to affect comprehension in a single lifetime. Written language is less volatile than the spoken kind and exerts a stabilising force. 让我们感到欣慰的是，这些变化通常发生得太慢，不会在一生中影响我们的理解。书面语言不像口头语言那样易变，而且能起到稳定作用。 But if language is always evolving (true to the point of cliché), the adaptations are even more profound when they come as a result of new speakers hailing from different linguistic worlds. No language has ever reached more speakers than English. 但是，如果语言总是在进化(这是老生常谈的观点) ，那么当来自不同语言世界的新的使用者出现时，这种适应就更加深刻。说英语的人最多。 It is hard to predict how they will change it, but easy to rule out the notion that they will not change it at all. In the end, it will be theirs too. ■ 很难预测他们将如何改变它，但很容易排除他们根本不会改变它的概念。最终，它也会是他们的。￭ Read more from Johnson, our columnist on language: 阅读更多来自我们的语言专栏作家约翰逊的文章: The hazards of pronouncing foreign names on air (May 11th) 在直播中发外国名字的危害(5月11日) ChatGPT raises questions about how humans acquire language (Apr 26th) ChatGPT 提出了人类如何获得语言的问题(4月26日) A new language textbook in Mexico has caused a brouhaha (Apr 12th) 墨西哥一本新的语言教科书引起了轩然大波(4月12日) Also: How the Johnson column got its name 还有: 约翰逊专栏是如何得名的 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/as-it-spreads-across-the-world-who-owns-english 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/as-it-spreads-across-the-world-who-owns-english 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 American drama 美国戏剧 August Wilson was and remains a bard of black life in America 奥古斯特 · 威尔逊过去和现在都是美国黑人生活的吟游诗人 Plays such as “Fences” and “Ma Rainey’s Black Bottom” continue to find new fans on stage and screen 《围栏》、《马蕾妮的黑屁股》等剧目不断在舞台和银幕上找到新的粉丝 May 25th 2023 | NEW YORK 2023年5月25日 | 纽约 W HEN AUGUST WILSON first heard a recording of Bessie Smith singing “Nobody in Town Can Bake a Sweet Jelly Roll Like Mine”, he wrote later, “the universe stuttered and everything fell to a new place.” It was 1965, and he was a dish-washing poet of 20 with grand ambitions and a sense of injustice. 威尔逊第一次听到贝西 · 史密斯(Bessie Smith)唱“城里没有人能像我一样烤一个甜果冻卷”的录音时，他后来写道，“宇宙结巴了，一切都落到了一个新的地方。”那是1965年，他是一个20岁的洗碗诗人，有着远大的抱负和不公平的感觉。 In Smith’s proud, mournful voice, he heard a way to hold onto the past while moving forward; to rue fate yet embrace life. The blues , he thought, “is the best literature black Americans have”. Through the music he found what became his voice as a playwright. 在史密斯那傲慢而悲伤的声音中，他听到了一种在前进的同时紧紧抓住过去的方法: 既悔恨命运又拥抱生活。他认为，布鲁斯“是美国黑人拥有的最好的文学作品”。通过音乐，他找到了他作为剧作家的声音。 Wilson died of liver cancer in 2005, aged 60, but his plays have lives of their own. 威尔逊于2005年死于肝癌，享年60岁，但他的戏剧有自己的生命。 The ten parts of his American Century Cycle—which, between them, won two Pulitzers and heaps of acclaim for chronicling the black American experience in each decade of the 20th century—are still finding new audiences on stage and screen. 他的《美国世纪周期》(American Century Cycle)的十个部分仍在舞台和银幕上寻找新的观众。这十个部分共获得了两项普利策奖，并因记录了20世纪每十年美国黑人的经历而获得了大量赞誉。 This year, as usual, his work has been performed across America: “Radio Golf” in Philadelphia, “Fences” in Jackson, Mississippi, “Two Trains Running” in Miami. 今年，像往常一样，他的作品在美国各地演出: 费城的“ Radio Golf”，密西西比杰克逊的“ Fences”，迈阿密的“ Two Train Running”。 “How I Learned What I Learned”, his autobiographical one-man show, recently closed in Seattle; “Jitney” comes to London in July. Meanwhile, Netflix has announced a screen adaptation of “The Piano Lesson”, with John David Washington and Samuel L. 他的自传性个人秀“ How I Learned What I Learned”最近在西雅图结束; “ Jitney”将于7月在伦敦上映。与此同时，Netflix 公司宣布了一部改编自电影《钢琴课》的电影，由约翰 · 大卫 · 华盛顿和塞缪尔 · L。 Jackson reprising their roles from a Broadway hit (nominated for a pair of Tony awards at the ceremony on June 11th). 杰克逊重新扮演了他们在百老汇的热门角色(在6月11日的颁奖典礼上获得了两项托尼奖提名)。 These plays endure because Wilson achieved his goal of presenting black life “on its own terms…with all its richness and fullness”. Although the white world looms at the edges of these stories, it remains largely offstage. 这些戏剧之所以能够流传下来，是因为威尔逊实现了他的目标: “以自己的方式... ... 展现黑人生活的丰富多彩”。尽管白人世界在这些故事的边缘若隐若现，但它在很大程度上仍处于幕后。 Wilson was concerned with the rituals and beauty of ordinary black Americans as they forged a culture in unpropitious circumstances. 威尔逊关心的是普通美国黑人的仪式和美丽，因为他们在不利的环境下形成了一种文化。 His characters are not slaves, but nor are they entirely free—denied the jobs and loans claimed by the European families who arrived in America at the turn of the 20th century. They hustle and dream, but know the game is rigged. 他笔下的人物不是奴隶，但也不是完全自由的ーー没有20世纪初抵达美国的欧洲家庭所要求的工作和贷款。他们忙碌着，梦想着，但是他们知道游戏是被操纵的。 As a frustrated musician says in “Ma Rainey’s Black Bottom”, which in 2020 became a film starring Viola Davis and Chadwick Boseman : “Is you gonna be satisfied with a bone somebody done throwed you when you see them eating the whole hog?” 正如一位沮丧的音乐家在2020年由维奥拉 · 戴维斯(Viola Davis)主演的电影《我的黑屁股》(Ma Rainey’s Black Bottom)中所说的那样: “当你看到别人扔给你的一块骨头被他们吃掉整头猪时，你会满意吗?”《我的黑屁股》(Ma Rainey’s Black Bottom)是一部查德维克·博斯曼电影 Most of these dramas unfold in the Hill District of Pittsburgh, which, when Wilson was growing up there, was a slum known as “Little Harlem”. 这些戏剧大多发生在匹兹堡的希尔区，当威尔逊在那里长大的时候，那里是一个被称为“小哈莱姆”的贫民窟。 Though he was originally named Frederick August Kittel junior after his mostly absent white German father, Wilson said he never questioned his racial identity because no one else seemed to: “All the things that were closed to Negroes at that time were closed to me.” As the only black student in his Catholic high-school class, he was greeted every morning by racist invective. 尽管威尔逊最初的名字是小弗雷德里克 · 奥古斯特 · 基特尔(Frederick August Kittel) ，因为他的德国白人父亲基本上不在他身边，但威尔逊说，他从来没有质疑过自己的种族身份，因为似乎没有其他人这么做: “当时对黑人关闭的所有东西都对我关闭了。”作为天主教高中班里唯一的黑人学生，他每天早上都受到种族主义者的谩骂。 He dropped out of formal schooling at 15 when a teacher accused him of plagiarism, on the presumption that his essay was too good for him to have written it himself. 15岁时，一位老师指责他抄袭，认为他的论文太好，不可能是他自己写的，于是他从正规学校退学。 A song for all seasons 一首适合所有季节的歌 Wilson read everything he could at his local library, then got another education listening to the banter of community elders at a nearby pool hall. 威尔逊在当地的图书馆里尽可能多地阅读，然后在附近的台球厅里听着社区老人们的玩笑，接受了另一种教育。 He knew he was a writer, and sensed that it was a rare job he could do without being under someone’s thumb: “Nobody said, ‘Hey, you can’t do that.’ So I felt free.” He spent years scribbling poems and stories before turning to plays in earnest in his 30s. 他知道自己是一个作家，并且意识到这是一个罕见的工作，他可以做，而不是在别人的拇指下: “没有人说，‘嘿，你不能这样做。所以我感到自由他花了数年时间写诗和故事，直到30多岁才开始认真地看戏。 After a couple of false starts, he found his rhythm, writing “Jitney”, “Ma Rainey’s Black Bottom” and “Joe Turner’s Come and Gone” between 1982 and 1984. He learned to sit at his desk and wait for the characters to talk to him: “I could hear the music.” 在几次失败的开始后，他找到了自己的节奏，在1982年至1984年间创作了“ Jitney”、“ Ma Rainey’s Black Bottom”和“ Joe Turner’s Come and Gone”。他学会了坐在办公桌前，等待剧中人物对他说话: “我能听到音乐。” His plays forgo outrage for sadness. During the Black Power era of the 1960s, Wilson and a friend had founded a theatre troupe in Pittsburgh that aimed to “raise consciousness” with dramas that put white characters on trial and occasionally murdered them. 他的戏剧因悲伤而放弃了愤怒。在20世纪60年代的黑人权力时代，威尔逊和一个朋友在匹兹堡创立了一个剧团，旨在通过审判白人角色、偶尔谋杀他们的戏剧来“唤醒人们的意识”。 But he came to see that he could send a stronger message by reproducing the rhythms and nuances of black life on stage, along with its slang. With his anthropological ear for the vernacular, Wilson saw his history plays as a way for black Americans to finally tell their own story. 但后来他发现，他可以通过在舞台上重现黑人生活的节奏和细微差别，以及黑人的俚语，来传递一个更强烈的信息。威尔逊从人类学的角度对白话进行了分析，他把自己的历史剧视为美国黑人最终讲述自己故事的一种方式。 “If we’re going to be pointed toward a future,” he reckoned, “we must know our past.” “如果我们要指向未来,”他认为，“我们必须了解我们的过去。” The women in his plays are outnumbered by the men, but tend to be sturdier, more sensible and more memorable. Their wisdom seems to come from resignation: relegated to lives of laundry and cooking, they know better than to dream big. 在他的戏剧中，女性的数量远远超过男性，但她们往往更坚强、更理智、更令人难忘。他们的智慧似乎来自于顺从: 他们只能过着洗衣和做饭的生活，他们知道不能有太大的梦想。 The men are the tragic figures, grasping for dignity in emasculating times. “I been wandering a long time in somebody else’s world,” says a recently freed slave in “Joe Turner’s Come and Gone”, Wilson’s favourite of his plays. 男人是悲剧性的人物，在被阉割的时代里争取尊严。“我在别人的世界里游荡了很长一段时间,”威尔逊最喜欢的戏剧《乔 · 特纳的来来去去》(Joe Turner’s Come and Gone)中一个刚刚获得自由的奴隶说。 Although he dotes on the details of black lives, his history cycle has always enjoyed a wide following. Themes such as the trials of marriage, tussles between fathers and sons, and the hunger for redemption resonate with audiences of all backgrounds. 虽然他对黑人生活的细节非常着迷，但是他的历史周期总是拥有广泛的追随者。诸如婚姻的考验、父子之间的争斗以及对救赎的渴望等主题在各种背景的观众中引起了共鸣。 By proving the commercial viability of African-American theatre, he opened the door for other black artists, such as Lynn Nottage and Suzan-Lori Parks, both Pulitzer-prizewinners. “I just want to say for the record: you are our king,” Ms Parks told Wilson in 2005. 通过证明非裔美国人戏剧的商业可行性，他为其他黑人艺术家打开了大门，如林恩 · 诺塔奇和苏珊-洛里 · 帕克斯，他们都是普利策奖得主。“我只想郑重声明: 你是我们的国王,”帕克斯在2005年告诉威尔逊。 Appetite for his work is unabated. After acquiring the playwright’s extensive archive in 2020, the University of Pittsburgh made it available this year, citing demand from scholars, historians and students. The first authorised biography of Wilson will be published this summer. 人们对他的工作兴趣不减。在2020年获得这位剧作家的大量档案之后，匹兹堡大学今年以学者、历史学家和学生的需求为由提供了这些资料。威尔逊的第一部授权传记将于今年夏天出版。 Last year an arts centre opened in his restored childhood home. Denzel Washington, who directed and starred in a film version of “Fences” (2016) and was an executive producer of “Ma Rainey”, plans to adapt more of his work. “It is a privilege and honour”, Mr Washington has said, “to be a small part in keeping him alive.” 去年，一家艺术中心在他儿时修复的家中开业。丹泽尔·华盛顿曾执导及主演电影版《围栏》(2016) ，并担任《马蕾妮》的执行制片人，他计划改编更多作品。“这是一种特权和荣誉,”华盛顿先生说，“在保持他活着的一小部分。” Wilson was grateful for his success, but not surprised. He knew that he was doing something special, and miraculously finished his grand project just a few months before he died. 威尔逊对他的成功心存感激，但并不感到惊讶。他知道自己在做一些特别的事情，并且在他去世前几个月奇迹般地完成了他的宏伟计划。 He harnessed the raw vitality of the blues for the stage because he understood it was “life’s way of talking”, as Ma Rainey explains: “You don’t sing to feel better. You sing ’cause that’s a way of understanding life.” ■ 他在舞台上利用了蓝调的原始活力，因为他明白这是“生活的说话方式”，正如马雷尼解释的那样: “你唱歌不是为了感觉更好。你唱歌，因为那是理解生活的一种方式。”￭ For more on the latest books, films, TV shows, albums and controversies, sign up to Plot Twist , our weekly subscriber-only newsletter 更多关于最新的书籍，电影，电视节目，专辑和争议，注册到情节扭曲，我们的每周订阅者唯一的时事通讯 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/august-wilson-was-and-remains-a-bard-of-black-life-in-america 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/culture/2023/05/25/august-wilson-was-and-remains-a-bard-of-black-life-in-america 下载的 Previous 上一页 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Economic & ... 经济..。 Sections 部分 Obituary 讣告 Graphic detail 图像细节 Our model suggests that global deaths remain 5% above pre-covid forecasts 我们的模型表明，全球死亡率仍然比疫情前的预测高5% The new normal :: Attributing this increase to covid would make it the fourth-leading cause of death 新常态: : 将这种增长归因于冠状病毒疾病会使其成为第四大死亡原因 Economic & ... 经济..。 Sections 部分 Obituary 讣告

Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 The new normal 新常态 Our model suggests that global deaths remain 5% above pre-covid forecasts 我们的模型表明，全球死亡率仍然比疫情前的预测高5% Attributing this increase to covid would make it the fourth-leading cause of death 将这种增长归因于冠状病毒疾病会使其成为第四大死因 May 23rd 2023 | 2023年5月23日 O N MAY 5 TH the World Health Organisation declared an end to the covid-19 public-health emergency. Based on official mortality counts, this looked tardy. By April 2022, average weekly death tolls had already fallen to the level of March 2020. 5月5日，世界卫生组织宣布结束2019冠状病毒疾病公共卫生紧急状态。根据官方统计的死亡率，这看起来有些迟缓。到2022年4月，每周平均死亡人数已经下降到2020年3月的水平。 Such tallies exclude deaths caused by covid but not attributed to it, however. A better measure is excess mortality, the gap between the number of deaths from all causes and the amount pre-covid trends would imply. 然而，这些统计数字并不包括冠状病毒疾病造成的死亡，但不包括死亡原因。一个更好的衡量标准是超额死亡率，即各种原因造成的死亡人数与新冠疫情前趋势所意味的死亡人数之间的差距。 For countries that do not publish their total number of deaths, we have built a model to estimate the excess. 对于那些没有公布死亡总人数的国家，我们已经建立了一个模型来估计超额死亡人数。 This statistic also suggests that covid is killing at a slower, steadier pace than in 2020-21. Yet endemic covid remains surprisingly deadly. 这一统计数据还表明，与2020年至2021年相比，冠状病毒疾病正在以更慢、更稳定的速度消亡。然而，地方性冠状病毒疾病仍然令人惊讶地致命。 Amid high uncertainty, our central estimate for the world’s current total mortality rate exceeds projections from 2019 by 5%, or 3m lives per year. 在高度不确定性的情况下，我们对当前世界总死亡率的核心估计比2019年的预测高出5% ，即每年300万人。 In the past year 16% of estimated excess deaths were officially attributed to covid, down from 37% in 2021. This gap has grown as deaths from covid have shifted from rich countries to poor ones. 过去一年，官方估计的超额死亡中有16% 是由冠状病毒疾病引起的，低于2021年的37% 。随着冠状病毒疾病死亡人数从富裕国家转移到贫穷国家，这一差距有所扩大。 In 2020 the virus hit hardest in the rich world, where relatively old populations travel often and huddle indoors in cold weather. Such places also ramped up testing, raising the share of victims counted in official data. 2020年，这种病毒在发达国家受到的影响最为严重，那里相对较老的人口经常外出旅行，在寒冷的天气里蜷缩在室内。这些地方还加大了检测力度，提高了官方数据中受害者的比例。 But in 2021 wealthy states began to turn the tide. Because many of the vulnerable died early on, and covid survivors gained natural immunity, their populations became more resilient. 但在2021年，富裕国家开始扭转局面。由于许多弱势群体很早就死亡，冠状病毒疾病幸存者获得了天然免疫力，他们的种群变得更具弹性。 They devised and distributed effective vaccines and treatments, from steroids to new drugs like Paxlovid. 他们设计并分发了有效的疫苗和治疗方法，从类固醇到帕洛韦这样的新药。 Meanwhile, the disease worked its way around the world. Ultra-contagious variants reached rural areas and countries with tight border controls, like Vietnam. 与此同时，这种疾病在世界各地蔓延。传染性极强的变种病毒已经蔓延到了农村地区和边境管制严格的国家，比如越南。 Even China, which often locked down entire cities, realised it could not control the virus and scrapped its “zero-covid” policy in 2022. In most poor countries vaccine take-up remains low, and weak health-care systems raise death rates for those who fall ill. 即使是经常封锁整个城市的中国，也意识到自己无法控制病毒，于2022年取消了“零新冠”政策。在大多数贫穷国家，疫苗接种率仍然很低，薄弱的卫生保健系统提高了患病者的死亡率。 Because data on total mortality is scant outside the rich world, our model’s estimates have become less precise over time. Its confidence interval for the world’s current excess-death rate stretches from near zero all the way up to the estimated levels of mid-2020. However, excluding the recent surge in China, its best guess for the past year is around one of every 1m people per day. 由于在发达国家以外，关于总死亡率的数据很少，我们的模型随着时间的推移已经变得不那么精确了。它对当前全球超额死亡率的置信区间从接近于零一直延伸到2020年中期的估计水平。然而，如果不考虑中国最近的人口激增，该机构对过去一年的最佳猜测是，每天约有100万人口中就有一人在中国居住。 As a share of people aged at least 65, excess-death rates in rich countries are three times lower than elsewhere. But because such places have older populations, their overall mortality rate is similar. 富裕国家65岁以上老人的超额死亡率是其他国家的三倍。但是因为这些地方的人口老龄化，他们的总体死亡率是相似的。 Covid is not the sole cause of this change. Many countries’ health-care systems remain strained, meaning people miss out on care. Cases of other diseases that went untreated in 2020-21 are raising death rates today. 冠状病毒疾病不是导致这种变化的唯一原因。许多国家的医疗保健系统仍然紧张，这意味着人们错过了医疗保健。2020年至2021年期间没有得到治疗的其他疾病病例如今正在提高死亡率。 But if covid were indeed responsible for the full increase, it would be tied for the world’s fourth-leading cause of death. At current rates, it would kill more people in the next eight years than in the past three. ■ 但是，如果冠状病毒疾病真的要对死亡人数的全面增加负责，那么死亡人数将与全球第四大死因并列。按照目前的速度，在接下来的八年里，死于这种疾病的人数将超过过去三年。￭ Chart sources: WHO; UN; The Economist. Read our methodology here , and inspect all our code, data, and models on GitHub 图表来源: 世界卫生组织、联合国、经济学人。请阅读我们的方法，并检查我们在 GitHub 上的所有代码、数据和模型 This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/graphic-detail/2023/05/23/our-model-suggests-that-global-deaths-remain-5-above-pre-covid-forecasts 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/graphic-detail/2023/05/23/our-model-suggests-that-global-deaths-remain-5-above-pre-covid-forecasts 下载的 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 L’enfant terrible 孩子真可怕 Martin Amis was the lurid chronicler of a whole generation 马丁 · 艾米斯是整整一代人的骇人听闻的记录者 The novelist, essayist and provocateur died on May 19th, aged 73 这位小说家、散文家和煽动者于5月19日逝世，享年73岁 May 24th 2023 | 2023年5月24日 I T BEGAN WITH a throb. A recognition of something he had probably buried long ago in his subconscious, which could be exposed now. A calorific infusion, announcing the next novel he had to write. 开始是一阵悸动。他意识到了很久以前埋藏在他潜意识里的东西，现在可能会暴露出来。注入热量，宣布他要写的下一部小说。 The throb went into his notebook, and when he had set up the morning—a double espresso, then that first cigarette his lungs were sobbing for—he went to his desk to work. 这种悸动进入了他的笔记本，当他准备好早上的工作ーー一杯双倍浓缩咖啡，然后是他肺部抽泣的第一支烟ーー他走到办公桌前去工作。 Eleven til one were his writing hours, in biro on paper for the first draft. With computers there was no provenance, no sense of crossing-out struggle. For much of that time he was apparently doing nothing much: staring at the ceiling, trolling around, picking his nose. 十一点到一点是他的写作时间，用圆珠笔在纸上写第一稿。有了计算机，就没有了出处，没有了划线斗争的意识。在大部分时间里，他显然什么也没做: 盯着天花板，到处闲逛，挖鼻孔。 He was making himself receptive to shaping the destinies of his characters and himself. Where they would go, he wasn’t sure; once they started creating, he couldn’t stop them. 他让自己接受塑造他的角色和自己的命运。他不确定他们会去哪里; 一旦他们开始创造，他就无法阻止他们。 Where the book was going, he hoped, was on a long shelf of titles by Martin Amis, with challenging jackets and good blurbs on their backs. 他希望这本书的目的地在马丁 · 艾米斯(Martin Amis)的一长排书架上，书的背面是富有挑战性的夹克和优秀的简介。 There had been 14 novels, eight non-fiction collections, two volumes of short stories. His first novel, “The Rachel Papers” in 1973, featuring a horny young man’s pre-university adventures, had won the Somerset Maugham award, but after that he was not much garlanded. 一共有14部小说，8部非小说集，两卷短篇小说。1973年，他的第一部小说《蕾切尔的文件》(The Rachel Papers)获得了毛姆文学奖，该书讲述了一个性欲旺盛的年轻人大学前的冒险故事。 His books did not unite people, let alone judges. They were verbally prodigious, structurally wayward, profane and often vividly experimental. He might look, and speak, like a shy public schoolboy, but as a writer he smashed plates. 他的书没有把人们团结起来，更不用说法官了。他们的言语惊人，结构任性，亵渎和往往生动的实验。他可能看起来，说起话来，像一个害羞的公立学校男生，但作为一个作家，他打碎了盘子。 He came closest to winning the Booker in 1989 and 1991, but in 1989 two female judges objected to his portrayals of women. 他在1989年和1991年最接近获得布克奖，但在1989年，两名女性法官反对他对女性的描绘。 True, his main interest was masculinity of the toxic, lowlife, rabidly competitive sort: Lionel Asbo, violent criminal and Lottery-winner, a metaphor for moral decrepitude; ad director John Self in “Money”, “200lb of yob genes, booze, snout and fast food”; or magisterial Keith Talent in “London Fields”, fanatical darts-player and professional cheat. 诚然，他的主要兴趣是那种有毒的、卑鄙的、激烈竞争的男子气概: 莱昂内尔 · 阿斯博(Lionel Asbo) ，暴力罪犯和彩票中奖者，道德沦丧的隐喻; 《金钱》(Money)中的广告导演约翰 · 赛尔夫(John Self) ，“200磅的青少年基因、酒精、猪鼻子和快餐”; 或者《伦敦田野》(London Fields)中的权威基思 · 达特(Keith Talent) ，狂热的飞镖玩家和职业骗子。 Set against these, his women characters could look tame; but not Nicola Six, also of “London Fields”, with her wardrobe of alluring underwear, her joyful scheming and her unabashed enjoyment of sodomy. 面对这些，他笔下的女性角色可能看起来很温顺; 但同样来自“伦敦田野”的尼古拉 · 西克斯(Nicola Six)却不是这样，她的衣橱里满是诱人的内衣，她快乐的诡计和对鸡奸毫不掩饰的享受。 He had first seen her as a “murderee”; but as she sat on the sofa with her legs folded seethingly underneath her, she was arranging her murder herself. 他起初认为她是一个“杀人犯”; 但是当她坐在沙发上，双腿紧紧地交叠在身下时，她正在安排自己的谋杀。 Real victims were hard to write about. He produced two books on the Holocaust, two on Stalin; in “Time’s Arrow”, about a doctor in Auschwitz, time was reversed so that the doctor, in his own warped mind, restored his patients rather than destroying them. 真正的受害者很难写。他出版了两本关于大屠杀的书，两本关于斯大林的书; 在《时间之箭》(Time’s Arrow)一书中，关于奥斯维辛的一名医生，时间被逆转了，医生在他自己扭曲的思维中，恢复了他的病人，而不是摧毁他们。 In “House of Meetings” (2006) about Stalin’s gulag, he felt he had not suffered enough to tell the tale. The story itself began to beg him to set it aside, perhaps stop writing altogether; and that very thought so pained him that he managed to finish the book. 在《会议室》(2006)中关于斯大林的古拉格集中营，他觉得自己受的苦还不够多。故事本身开始恳求他把它放在一边，也许干脆停止写作; 正是这个想法使他如此痛苦，以至于他设法完成了这本书。 His father, Kingsley Amis, had been famous for comic novels, but he did not believe that talent was inherited. Their relationship was vexed. 他的父亲金斯利 · 艾米斯(Kingsley Amis)以喜剧小说闻名，但他不相信天赋是遗传的。他们的关系很纠结。 At times the Amis franchise was a burden; he suspected that his first novel had been published only out of “mercenary curiosity” to see how the son would compare. 有时，阿米斯特许经营是一个负担; 他怀疑他的第一部小说出版只是出于“唯利是图的好奇心”，看看儿子会如何比较。 He read his father’s books, but his father soon gave up on his, hurling “Money” across the room for “buggering about with the reader”. 他读过他父亲的书，但他父亲很快就放弃了他的书，把“钱”扔到房间的另一头，因为他“和读者鬼混”。 His father wrote carefully, with hearty guffaws he heard as a child; he himself went headlong into the voice of whichever grotesque had strayed across his path. 他的父亲小心翼翼地写着，他小时候听到过他发自内心的大笑; 他自己也一头扎进了那个从他面前走过的怪异的人的声音里。 Yet they were oddly united, father and son, in respect for a good English sentence—as well as jointly believing that writing was a feasible, even fine, way to make a living. 然而，父亲和儿子却奇怪地团结在一起，尊重一个好的英语句子，并且共同相信写作是一种可行的、甚至是很好的谋生方式。 His father envied him, perhaps. It was the besetting sin of writers. “The Information” detailed the worst horror, a man’s best friend becoming more celebrated than he was. 也许他父亲嫉妒他。这是作家最大的罪过。“信息”详细描述了最可怕的事情，一个人最好的朋友变得比他更有名。 His own inner egomaniac dreaded any blazing talent coming up on his flank, even though he moved in the most sparkling and influential literary set of the 1980s and 1990s—with Ian McEwan, Julian Barnes, Salman Rushdie and “Hitch”, Christopher Hitchens, his closest friend, who still lived for Trotskyist struggle. 他自己内心的自大狂害怕任何耀眼的才华出现在他的身边，尽管他进入了上世纪80年代和90年代最耀眼、最有影响力的文学圈子——与伊恩•麦克尤恩(Ian McEwan)、朱利安•巴恩斯(Julian Barnes)、萨尔曼•拉什迪(Salman Rushdie)以及他最亲密的朋友、仍为托洛茨基主义奋斗的克里斯托弗·希钦斯《希区》(Hitch)合作。 All were trying to write their own version of Trollope’s “The Way we Live Now”, and he had to believe he was the best at it. 所有人都在尝试写出自己版本的特罗洛普的《我们现在的生活方式》 ，而他必须相信自己是这方面的佼佼者。 The very best, he admitted, were in America, his “Moronic Inferno”: loudmouth Mailer, terrifying Nabokov, “sacramental” Saul Bellow with his stevedore’s shoulders. These men dared to write vast superpower novels about the whole of society. 他承认，最好的是在美国，他的“低能地狱”: 大嘴巴的梅勒，可怕的纳博科夫，“神圣的”索尔 · 贝娄与他的装卸工的肩膀。这些人敢于写关于整个社会的超级大国小说。 His own smaller efforts were symptomatic of Britain’s decline: its aura of filthy pub carpets, its morbidly obese children, phone booths “slobberingly coated with thick red paint”, London “like the insides of an old plug”. 他自己较小的努力反映了英国的衰落: 肮脏的酒吧地毯的光环，病态肥胖的儿童，“涂着厚厚的红色油漆的流着口水的电话亭”，伦敦“就像一个旧插头的内部”。 Purpose had been lost along with the empire, and under Thatcher, that old witch, civility and civilisation had fallen apart. Nothing but weak left-liberalism remained to confront the ruins; that, and the scathing onslaught of his prose. 在撒切尔的统治下，帝国失去了目标，老巫婆、文明和文明也分崩离析。除了软弱的左翼自由主义，没有其他东西可以对抗这片废墟，以及他的散文所带来的猛烈攻击。 Yet he did not feel appreciated. In the mid-1990s he was crucified by the press for having asked his publisher, Cape, for an advance of £500,000 for “The Information”. 然而，他并没有感到被赏识。上世纪90年代中期，他因为向出版商 Cape 预支50万美元购买《信息》(The Information)而被媒体钉死在十字架上。 (Cape refused; HarperCollins obliged.) He needed the money for his divorce and to get his awful teeth fixed, the flaw in his good looks; not cosmetic, like a boob job, but essential. In 1995 he left not only Cape but his agent and his wife. (卡普拒绝了，哈珀柯林斯公司答应了。)他需要钱离婚，还需要修补他那糟糕的牙齿，修补他美貌中的瑕疵; 不是像隆胸那样的化妆品，而是必需品。1995年，他不仅离开了开普敦，还离开了他的经纪人和妻子。 Eventually he abandoned England, settling for three years in Uruguay with Isabel Fonseca, his second wife, and then in Florida. In Uruguay he found the quiet life he wanted almost always. 最终，他放弃了英格兰，与第二任妻子伊莎贝尔 · 丰塞卡(Isabel Fonseca)在乌拉圭定居了三年，然后在佛罗里达定居。在乌拉圭，他几乎总能找到自己想要的宁静生活。 It made a striking contrast to the jet-setting party animal of his past: cigarette in hand, Hitch at shoulder, swagger in his look. 这与他过去那种时髦的派对动物形成了鲜明的对比: 手里拿着香烟，肩膀上搭着顺风车，神气十足。 Increasingly, he fretted about time. In “Time’s Arrow” Dr Unverdorben looked steadily towards the past, away from death. But everyone, with gravid arrest, would find it waiting. 他越来越担心时间。在《时间之箭》中，昂弗多本博士坚定地回望过去，远离死亡。但是每个孕妇都会发现它在等待。 It loomed as he slowly added to his shelf of books, the only particle of him that might stand the test of time. He had felt, not far into the 21st century, an outsurge of essence, an anti-afflatus, that signalled the dying of his creative powers. 当他慢慢地把书放到书架上时，这种感觉隐隐约约地出现了，这是他唯一可以经得起时间考验的一部分。在进入21世纪后不久，他就感觉到一种本质的涌现，一种反感，这预示着他的创造力的消亡。 The exit was coming closer, time hurtling like a runaway train. The Zeitgeist had moved on, fast, from the one he knew: the one in which he had strolled leonine towards that throb only he felt, the next novel that could illuminate and eviscerate the age. ■ 出口越来越近，时间像失控的火车一样疾驰。时代精神迅速地从他所熟悉的那部小说中走了出来: 在那部小说中，他漫步着狮子座，走向只有他能感受到的悸动，下一部小说可以照亮和挖掘这个时代。￭ This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/obituary/2023/05/24/martin-amis-was-the-lurid-chronicler-of-a-whole-generation 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/obituary/2023/05/24/martin-amis-was-the-lurid-chronicler-of-a-whole-generation 下载的 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个

Obituary 讣告 Sections 部分 The Economist reads 《经济学人》读道 The spy who read me: authors under surveillance ❗ 🔄 Spies and scribes :: The books that show espionage agents are not the most subtle literary critics 间谍与抄写员: 表现间谍特工的书不是最微妙的文学批评家 Obituary 讣告 Sections 部分

Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个 Spies and scribes 间谍和抄写员 The spy who read me: authors under surveillance 读过我的间谍: 被监视的作家 The books that show espionage agents are not the most subtle literary critics 展示间谍活动的书籍并不是最微妙的文学批评家 May 24th 2023 | 2023年5月24日 E RNEST HEMINGWAY thought his phone was tapped. Doris Lessing reckoned that British spies were following her every move. Claude McKay suspected that the FBI was monitoring his travel in Europe. Their instincts were right. 欧内斯特·海明威认为他的电话被监听了。多丽丝 · 莱辛认为英国间谍在跟踪她的一举一动。克劳德 · 麦凯怀疑联邦调查局在监视他在欧洲的行踪。他们的直觉是对的。 During the 20th century intelligence agencies in Britain and America spent countless hours investigating “dangerous” authors. This was in part an alternative to censoring or banning the work of troublesome writers, which dictatorships do more readily than democracies. The literary snooping eased up, though did not cease, after America won the cold war. Writing by novelists and essayists came to seem less dangerous and files on them became thinner. 在20世纪，英国和美国的情报机构花费了无数的时间来调查“危险的”作家。这在一定程度上可以替代审查或禁止麻烦作家的作品，独裁政权比民主政体更容易做到这一点。美国赢得冷战后，文学界的窥探活动有所缓和，但并未停止。小说家和散文家的写作似乎变得不那么危险了，有关他们的文件也变得更薄了。 Most snoops are not very good readers. They sometimes get basic facts wrong. 大多数窥探者都不是很好的读者，他们有时会把基本事实搞错。 In one file MI5, Britain’s domestic-intelligence agency, adds an extra “e” to “Orwell” (spelling it “Orewell”) and mistitles his book “Down and Out in Paris and London” as “A Down and Out in London and Paris”. 在一份文件中，英国国内情报机构军情五处(MI5)在“ Orwell”(拼写为“ Orewell”)后面多加了一个“ e”，并将他的书《巴黎和伦敦的穷困潦倒》(Down and Out In Paris and London)误称为《伦敦和巴黎的穷困潦倒》(A Down and Out In London and Paris)。 The FBI’s assessment of McKay, a important figure in the Harlem renaissance, is true but unenlightening: he “is apparently a poet, or at least he has written considerable verse”. One FBI agent describes James Baldwin, an American writer, as “white… 6’, neat”. 美国联邦调查局(FBI)对哈莱姆文艺复兴重要人物麦凯的评估是真实的，但没有启发性: 他“显然是一位诗人，或者至少他写过相当多的诗歌”。一位联邦调查局探员形容美国作家詹姆斯 · 鲍德温为“白人... ... 6英尺高，整洁”。 (He was black and five foot six.) Some investigations last a few dull days. Others take decades. Here are six of the best books by and about authors under surveillance. (他是黑人，身高5英尺6英寸。)有些调查持续了几天无聊的日子。有些则需要几十年。以下是六本最好的书籍，它们都是由受监视的作家所著，也是关于这些作家的。 They show how the security state watched, read and sometimes influenced writers whose approaches to literature ranged from modernist to postmodernist and whose ideologies stretched from libertarianism to communism. 它们展示了安全国家是如何观察、阅读并有时影响作家的，这些作家对文学的态度从现代主义到后现代主义，他们的意识形态从自由意志主义延伸到共产主义。 Writers Under Surveillance: The FBI Files. By JPat Brown, B. C. D. Lipton and Michael Morisy. MIT Press; 393 pages; $24.95 and £20 监视下的作家: 联邦调查局档案。作者: JPat Brown，B.C.D。 Lipton，Michael Morisy。麻省理工出版社; 393页; 24.95美元和20美元 J. Edgar Hoover, director of the FBI from 1924 to 1972, assumed that left-leaning writers were radicals and communists. His agents snooped on American authors from Allen Ginsberg to Susan Sontag. 1924年至1972年担任美国联邦调查局(FBI)局长的 J •埃德加•胡佛(J. Edgar Hoover)认为，左倾作家是激进分子和共产主义者。他的代理人窥探从艾伦 · 金斯伯格到苏珊 · 桑塔格的美国作家。 They listened in on phone calls, followed writers to meetings and spoke to their associates. Sometimes agents read and annotated their books. They targeted authors who were socialists, civil-rights activists or critical of the government, or who had friends with those attributes. 他们监听电话，跟踪作家参加会议，并与他们的同事交谈。有时代理人阅读和注释他们的书籍。他们的目标是那些社会主义者、民权活动家或者批评政府的作家，或者那些拥有这些特质的朋友。 This volume explores the intelligence files of 16 prominent writers, acquired with freedom-of-information requests. The documents are reproduced in facsimile—typewritten, stamped and scribbled on. JPat Brown and his co-editors provide context and close readings of the files. 本卷探讨了16个著名作家的情报文件，获得的信息自由的要求。这些文件以传真方式复制ーー打字、盖章和涂写。JPat Brown 和他的合作编辑提供了这些文件的上下文和近距离阅读。 Readers learn that Ray Bradbury, who wrote “Fahrenheit 451”, a novel that condemns censorship, had no idea why the FBI was watching him. Hemingway, on the other hand, knew that he was being followed and once drunkenly called the FBI Nazis and cowards. 读者了解到，雷布拉德伯里，谁写了“华氏451度”，一部谴责审查制度的小说，不知道为什么联邦调查局监视他。另一方面，海明威知道自己被跟踪了，他曾醉醺醺地骂 FBI 是纳粹和懦夫。 “Writers under Surveillance” shows how state surveillance worked in mid-20th-century America. It is vital, the editors argue, that the public learn about the agency’s literary espionage, which probably continues today. “监控下的作家”展示了20世纪中期美国国家监控的工作原理。编辑们认为，让公众了解该机构的文学间谍活动至关重要，这种活动可能会持续到今天。 For Whom the Bell Tolls. By Ernest Hemingway. Simon & Schuster; 576 pages; $20. Arrow; £8.99 战地钟声，欧内斯特·海明威西蒙与舒斯特，576页，售价20美元，Arrow 出版社，8.99美元 For 40 years FBI agents followed and read Hemingway. 40年来，联邦调查局探员一直跟踪研究海明威。 A newspaper clipping in his FBI file suggests that “For Whom the Bell Tolls”, his novel about the Spanish Civil War, published in 1940, was “so miserable, so slanderous, that it met with excellent reception among the Fascists, the Trotskyists and the Munichists [appeasers of Hitler]”. 他在美国联邦调查局(FBI)档案中的一份剪报显示，他在1940年出版的关于西班牙内战的小说《战地钟声》“如此悲惨，如此诽谤，以至于在法西斯主义者、托洛茨基主义者和慕尼黑主义者(希特勒的绥靖者)中受到了极好的接受”。 The FBI saw the novel as sympathetic to the Republicans who were fighting Spanish fascism, and therefore to Stalinism. Its themes—such as treason and extramarital sex—unsettled the conservative Hoover. But he eventually dismissed the charge that Hemingway was a communist. 联邦调查局认为这部小说同情那些反抗西班牙法西斯主义的共和党人，因此也同情斯大林主义。它的主题ーー例如叛国和婚外性行为ーー令保守派的胡佛感到不安。但他最终驳斥了关于海明威是共产主义者的指控。 In a short note Hoover wrote, “Knowing Hemingway as I did, I doubt he had any communist leanings.” Some scholars have interpreted his dogged interest in Hemingway as revenge for something the novelist said or did while he was working in the service of the American government in pre-revolutionary Cuba (ironically, just after “For Whom the Bell Tolls” was published). 胡佛在一份简短的笔记中写道: “像我这样了解海明威的人，我怀疑他是否有任何共产主义倾向。”一些学者将他对海明威的执着兴趣解读为对这位小说家在古巴为美国政府服务期间所说所做的报复(具有讽刺意味的是，就在《战地钟声》出版后不久)。 F.B. Eyes: How J. Edgar Hoover’s Ghostreaders Framed African American Literature. By William J. Maxwell. Princeton University Press; 384 pages; $30.95 and £25 F.B.Eyes: How J. Edgar Hoover’s Ghostreader Framing African American Cultural F.B.Eyes: 胡佛的影子读者如何构建非裔美国文学。作者: 威廉 · J · 麦克斯韦。普林斯顿大学出版社; 384页; 30.95美元和25美元 Almost half of the writers in “The Norton Anthology of African American Literature” were anthologised in FBI files. “F.B. Eyes” describes the bureau as “perhaps the most dedicated and influential forgotten critic of African American literature”. 《诺顿非裔美国人文学选集》中几乎一半的作者都被收录在 FBI 的档案中。“ F.B.Eyes”将该局描述为“也许是最有献身精神和影响力的被遗忘的非裔美国文学评论家”。 In some 14,000 pages of files its agents delved into the socialist politics of some black authors and dwelled on the supposed dangers of African-American activism. 在大约14,000页的文件中，它的代理人深入研究了一些黑人作家的社会主义政治，并详细阐述了所谓的非裔美国人行动主义的危险。 The FBI sent an agent to see an early staging of Lorraine Hansberry’s “A Raisin in the Sun”, about a poor black family in Chicago. 美国联邦调查局(FBI)派了一名特工去看洛兰·汉斯贝里《阳光下的葡萄干》(a Raisin in The Sun)的早期版本，该剧讲述的是芝加哥一个贫穷的黑人家庭的故事。 He filed a surprisingly sensitive review, offering a droll commentary on its characters and confirming that it contained “no comments of any nature about Communism”. The book also records black authors’ creative responses to their spy-readers. 他提交了一份出人意料的敏感评论，对影片人物进行了滑稽的评论，并确认其中“没有任何关于共产主义本质的评论”。这本书还记录了黑人作家对他们的间谍读者的创造性反应。 William Maxwell contends that McKay’s restless sentences, Baldwin’s exasperated tone and Richard Wright’s sardonic analogies were in part reactions to the agents who not only read their books but followed them to their homes and stopped them at state borders. 威廉 · 马克斯韦尔(William Maxwell)认为，麦凯不安分的句子、鲍德温愤怒的语气以及理查德 · 赖特(Richard Wright)讽刺性的类比，在一定程度上是对特工的反应。这些特工不仅读他们的书，还跟踪他们到他们的家里，并在州边境阻止他们。 Both the agency and its targets were energised by their fear of and respect for one another. 这个机构和它的目标都因为他们对彼此的恐惧和尊重而充满活力。 The Devil Finds Work. By James Baldwin. Vintage; 127 pages; $14 《魔鬼找到工作》 ，詹姆斯 · 鲍德温著，复古出版社，127页，14美元 Hoover is “history’s most highly paid (and most utterly useless) voyeur”, quips Baldwin in this book-length essay from 1976, which surveys racism in American movies. The author’s own surveillance record seems to bear him out. 胡佛是“历史上收入最高(也是最没用的)偷窥狂”，鲍德温在这篇1976年的长篇文章中打趣道。这篇文章调查了美国电影中的种族主义。作者自己的监视记录似乎证实了他的话。 FBI snoops trailed and photographed him from 1960 to 1973. They labelled him “a pervert” (because he was gay) and “a communist” (because he signed a petition sponsored by the Fair Play for Cuba Committee, a leftist group). 联邦调查局的探子从1960年到1973年跟踪并拍摄了他的照片。他们给他贴上了“变态”(因为他是同性恋)和“共产主义者”(因为他签署了一份由左翼组织古巴公平竞争委员会发起的请愿书)的标签。 The files they compiled are sloppily written, full of spelling mistakes, terrible grammar and repetition. Hoover added Baldwin to his “Security Index”, a list of citizens who were a threat to the state and who, in a state of emergency, would be the first to be arrested. 他们编译的文件写得很草率，充满了拼写错误，糟糕的语法和重复。胡佛把鲍德温加入了他的“安全指数”，这是一份对国家构成威胁的公民名单，在紧急状态下，他们将是第一批被逮捕的公民。 The surveillance took a toll on Baldwin. He wrote less than he normally did during those years, all the while promising to blast Hoover “to the wall” in an exposé, which he never finished. 监视对鲍德温造成了伤害。在那些年里，他写得比平时少，同时还承诺要在一篇揭露胡佛的文章中把他“轰到墙上”，而这篇文章他一直没有写完。 His witty musings on Hoover in this collection hint at the acerbic insight such a book might have provided. 他在这本集子中对胡佛的机智思考暗示了这本书可能提供的尖刻洞察力。 British Writers and MI5 Surveillance, 1930-1960. By James Smith. Cambridge University Press; 228 pages; $100 and £55 《英国作家和军情五处监视，1930-1960》 ，詹姆斯 · 史密斯著，剑桥大学出版社，228页，100美元和55美元 Britain’s domestic intelligence agencies kept secret records on many left-wing writers after the first world war. James Smith has written a comprehensive account of the clandestine surveillance of British literary culture, which encompassed artists from W. H. 第一次世界大战后，英国国内情报机构对许多左翼作家保存了秘密记录。詹姆斯史密斯写了一个全面的帐户秘密监视英国文学文化，其中包括艺术家从 W。 Auden, a poet, to Joan Littlewood, a theatre director. Mr Smith describes how MI5 intercepted their letters, followed their families and recruited their close friends to be informants. 诗人奥登写给戏剧导演琼 · 利特尔伍德。史密斯先生描述了军情五处如何截获他们的信件，如何跟踪他们的家人，如何招募他们的密友做线人。 Especially fascinating is the relationship between the agency and Arthur Koestler, a Hungarian-born author who became disillusioned with communism and did a spot of clandestine work himself for the British government during the cold war. 特别引人入胜的是情报局和 Arthur Koestler 之间的关系。 Arthur Koestler 是一位匈牙利出生的作家，他对共产主义的幻想破灭了，在冷战期间为英国政府做了一些秘密工作。 His dealings with MI5 involved mendacity and blackmail by both sides. Mr Smith sometimes plods like the agents he writes about. His advice to MI5’s reluctant literary critics, though, is sound: to know a writer, don’t listen to their phone calls, read their books. 他与军情五处的交易涉及双方的谎言和勒索。史密斯先生有时象他所描写的那些代理人一样拖拖拉拉。不过，他对军情五处不情愿的文学评论家的建议是合理的: 了解一个作家，不要听他们的电话，读他们的书。 Although his book sometimes lapses into unfounded speculation and tiresome abstraction, it is well worth reading. 尽管他的书有时会陷入毫无根据的猜测和令人厌烦的抽象，但它还是很值得一读。 Nineteen Eighty-Four. By George Orwell. Berkley; 304 pages; $17.00. Penguin; £16.99 一九八四: 乔治•奥威尔(George Orwell)著，伯克利出版社(Berkley)出版，304页，17.00美元，企鹅出版社(Penguin)出版，16.99美元 MI5 and Britain’s India Office were not the Thought Police. The British prime minister was hardly Big Brother. Yet the surveillance from 1936 to 1951 of George Orwell, who called himself a democratic socialist, helped inspire those terrifying inventions. 军情五处和英国驻印度办事处不是思想警察。这位英国首相算不上什么老大哥。然而，自称民主社会主义者的乔治•奥威尔(George Orwell)在1936年至1951年间受到的监视，帮助激发了那些可怕的发明。 It was sporadic and cursory, gathering less than 40 pages of material all told. The India Office took an interest in him when he was offered a job as an assistant editor of The Pioneer , an Anglo-Indian newspaper in Lucknow. Despite their mistrust, agents didn’t uncover any evidence that Orwell was a communist. (Indeed, he was a fervent foe of Stalinism.) The India Office quickly lost interest when Orwell withdrew from the job on medical grounds. 这是零星的和粗略的，收集不到40页的材料总结。当勒克瑙的英印报纸《先锋报》(The Pioneer)向他提供了一份助理编辑的工作时，印度办事处对他产生了兴趣。尽管不信任，特工们没有发现任何证据证明奥威尔是共产主义者。(事实上，他是斯大林主义的狂热反对者。)奥威尔因健康原因退出印度办事处后，印度办事处很快就失去了兴趣。 His MI5 file—glib, repetitive and often scribbled with red ink—refers to his “bohemian fashion” and “many Indian friends”. 他在英国军情五处(MI5)的档案中提到他的“波西米亚风格”和“许多印度朋友”。 It is evidence to support Orwell’s dim view of the police: “the policeman who arrests the ‘Red’ does not understand the theories the ‘Red’ is preaching”, he wrote in an essay published in 1941. The Thought Police of “Nineteen Eighty-Four” are less hapless and far more sinister. 这是支持奥威尔对警察的悲观看法的证据: “逮捕‘红色’的警察不理解‘红色’所宣扬的理论”，他在1941年发表的一篇文章中写道。“一九八四”的思想警察没有那么倒霉，而是更加险恶。 Yet they owe something to the snoops who followed him around. 但是他们欠那些跟踪他的窥探者一些东西。 Also try 也试试 Our recent coverage of espionage includes an article about how the war in Ukraine has battered the reputation of Russian spies. China’s spies know how to hack and harrass dissidents but in other areas they have a lot to learn . Here we explain why companies need to take corporate espionage more seriously. As this column from 2016 points out, scribes sometimes become spies, which isn’t surprising because they have much in common. Read our recommendations of books that explain how espionage and intelligence work. ■ 我们最近对间谍活动的报道包括一篇关于乌克兰战争如何损害俄罗斯间谍名誉的文章。中国的间谍知道如何黑客和骚扰持不同政见者，但在其他领域，他们还有很多东西要学。这里我们将解释为什么公司需要更严肃地对待商业间谍活动。正如这篇2016年的专栏文章所指出的，抄写员有时候会成为间谍，这并不奇怪，因为他们有很多共同点。阅读我们推荐的解释间谍活动和情报工作的书籍。￭ This article was downloaded by calibre from https://www.economist.com/the-economist-reads/2023/05/24/the-spy-who-read-me-authors-under-surveillance 本文是通过 Calibre 从 https://www.economist.com/the-economist-reads/2023/05/24/the-spy-who-read-me-authors-under-surveillance 下载的 Articles 文章 Sections 部分 Next 下一个
